### [1] [Medial Model of Elastic Curve Contacts, from Clasps to Knits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05688)
*Kathryn Chain,Haoye Sun,Michael S. Dimitriyev*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 论文检验“纤维间接触面近似为极小常平均曲率曲面”的假设，借助中轴变换（medial axis transformation）及其基于根Voronoi镶嵌（radical Voronoi tessellation）的推广，对正交扣与针织结构进行数值研究，为缠结纤维的接触建模提供几何方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由互锁与缠结纤维构成的材料难以建模，原因之一是纤维形状与接触力学（contact mechanics）的复杂耦合；传统Hertz接触描述基于凸体（convex bodies）交互，而纤维常形成扣状（clasp）鞍形接触面，使现有接触模型失效。

Method: 基于纤维间接触面形似极小、常平均曲率曲面（minimal, constant mean-curvature surfaces）的观察，采用中轴变换（将Voronoi镶嵌推广至生成曲线与曲面）进行几何建模；并对不同半径曲线引入基于根Voronoi镶嵌的“根轴变换（radical axis transform）”。计算对象为正交扣（orthogonal clasp）与简单针织结构（knit structure）。

Result: 中轴变换可为相同曲线间的接触面提供合理模型；根轴变换能较好近似非相同曲线间的接触面，且在不同半径曲线的算例中呈现接触面趋于有限平均曲率（finite mean curvature）的趋势。

Conclusion: 结果表明中轴变换类几何方法可有效描述纤维接触面，并可能为缠结（tangles）的更精细几何建模方法指明方向。

Abstract: Materials constructed from interlocking and tangled fibers are challenging to model and understand due in part to the complex interplay between fiber shape and contact mechanics. Much of our intuition about contact mechanics comes from the Hertzian description, which is based on interactions between convex bodies. However, fibers often form clasp shapes, which involve saddle-shaped contact surfaces; the ability for two interacting bodies to conform to each other along a saddle surface confounds many existing mechanical contact models. Following an observation that contact surfaces between fibers tend to look like minimal, constant mean-curvature surfaces, we seek to test this hypothesis and provide a direct link to geometric modeling methods. To do this, we leverage the medial axis transformation, which generalizes the notion of a Voronoi tessellation to generating curves and surfaces. Through computational studies on an orthogonal clasp and a simple knit structure, we find that the medial axis transformation provides a reasonable model for contact surfaces between identical curves. We also show that a generalization of the medial axis transformation based on radical Voronoi tessellations can be used to well-approximate contact surfaces between non-identical curves. We apply this ``radical axis transform'' to curves of different radii and demonstrate a trend for surfaces to adopt finite mean curvature. Our results may point the way towards more sophisticated geometric methods for modeling tangles.

</details>


### [2] [Frank Elastic Constants of Associating Rigid-Rodlike Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05967)
*Akihiko Matsuyama*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用平均场理论结合Straley弹性自由能形式，自洽处理缔合与取向有序，发现缔合通过聚集体尺寸分布的长尾结构选择性增强弯曲弹性常数，导致显著弹性各向异性K33≫K11>K22。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究缔合刚性棒状体系（associating rigid rodlike systems）的Frank弹性常数，理解聚集平衡与取向有序如何共同影响弹性常数的浓度与温度依赖性。

Method: 将平均场理论（mean field theory）与Straley弹性自由能形式（Straley elastic free energy formalism）结合，自洽（self-consistently）处理聚集平衡与取向有序；分别考虑单体–二聚体模型（monomer–dimer model）与完全多分散棒状聚集体模型（fully polydisperse rodlike aggregate model）。

Result: 聚集通过聚集体尺寸分布（aggregate-size distribution）的长尾结构选择性增强弯曲弹性常数（bend elastic constant），在向列相中产生显著弹性各向异性（elastic anisotropy）K33≫K11>K22；降温或升浓促进长棒状聚集体形成并使弯曲弹性常数快速增大，而K11=3K22关系保持不变。

Conclusion: 所预测的异常大弯曲弹性常数应视为刚性聚集体极限（rigid-aggregate limit）下的上限估计（upper-bound estimates）。

Abstract: Frank elastic constants of associating rigid rodlike systems are investigated using a mean field theory combined with the Straley elastic free energy formalism. The aggregation equilibrium and orientational ordering are treated self-consistently to analyze the concentration and temperature dependences of the elastic constants. Both a monomer--dimer model and a fully polydisperse rodlike aggregate model are considered. We find that aggregation selectively enhances the bend elastic constant through the long-tail structure of the aggregate-size distribution. As a result, a pronounced elastic anisotropy, $K_{33}\gg K_{11}>K_{22}$, emerges in the nematic phase. Decreasing temperature or increasing concentration promotes the formation of long rodlike aggregates and induces a rapid increase in the bend elastic constant, while the relation $K_{11}=3K_{22}$ remains unchanged. The exceptionally large bend elastic constants predicted here should be regarded as upper-bound estimates for the rigid-aggregate limit.

</details>


### [3] [Charged Three-Dimensional Solitons in a Ferroelectric Nematic Liquid Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06884)
*Netra Prasad Dhakal,Zakaria Siddiquee,Hiroya Nishikawa,Fumito Araoka,James Gleeson,Samuel Sprunt,Antal Jakli*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究报告了在铁电向列相液晶中，由快速降温自发产生的带电传播三维孤子（propagating 3D solitons）的实验观测与计算机模拟，其传播方向平行或反平行于铁电极化，碰撞时相互湮灭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前实验上从未观测到带电的传播型三维孤子，本文旨在报道并解释此类新结构在无外场条件下自发出现的现象及其成因。

Method: 结合实验观测与计算机模拟（computer simulation），研究对象为从反铁电相（antiferroelectric SmZA phase）快速冷却至铁电向列相（ferroelectric nematic, NF）的液晶体系，并据此提出电荷与极化形变的机制解释。

Result: 孤子在快速冷却过程中自发出现，无需外场；其传播方向平行或反平行于铁电极化（ferroelectric polarization），彼此碰撞时湮灭，并被归因于正或负带电的传播型局域极化扇变（polarization splay），用以屏蔽NF相中过量的可动电荷团。

Conclusion: 可动电荷的存在源于反铁电相中空间周期性电荷分布在温度淬火（quench）穿过反铁电—铁电相变后短暂保留，从而使带电传播孤子得以形成。

Abstract: Experimental observations and computer simulation of formation and structural properties of so far experimentally never seen electrically charged propagating 3D solitons are reported. The propagating solitons appear spontaneously without an external field in a ferroelectric nematic (NF) liquid crystal under rapid cooling from the antiferroelectric (SmZA) phase. The observed solitons propagate either parallel or antiparallel to the ferroelectric polarization and annihilate when they collide with each other. They are attributed to either positively or negatively charged propagating local polarization splay formed to screen excess negative or positive mobile charge lumps in the NF phase. The presence of mobile charge is attributed to a spatially periodic charge distribution present in the antiferroelectric phase that temporarily remains after the temperature is quenched through the antiferroelectric- ferroelectric phase transition.

</details>

### [4] [Exact Transport-Information Correspondence for Distribution-Adaptive Discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06059)
*Koretaka Yuge*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文提出一种以概率加权质心为代表性点的局部映射离散化方案，使每个网格单元的传输代价可用条件分布的协方差矩阵与Fisher度量精确表达，从而将传输-信息对应关系推广到有限尺度和任意非重叠有界凸集划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的不可避免典型非线性（UCN）构造依赖单元几何、空间覆盖及单元尺度趋于零等特定条件，其传输-信息对应关系适用范围受限；且离散化所诱导的结构通常无法仅由信息几何（information geometry）确定。

Method: 引入替代性的局部映射离散化方案，将每个单元内的概率质量传输至其自身的概率加权质心（probability-weighted centroid），而非几何质心；据此推导单元传输代价与该单元条件概率分布协方差矩阵及连续分布族Fisher度量（Fisher metric）之间的精确关系。

Result: 在所提方案下，传输代价与Fisher诱导二次型的期望之间存在精确对应，且无需均匀单元几何、样本空间完全覆盖或无穷小单元尺度极限等条件。

Conclusion: 该构造将传输-信息对应关系推广至有限尺度离散化，适用于由任意非重叠有界凸集（bounded convex sets）组成的集合划分。

Abstract: Discretization of continuous probability distributions is an operation on the sample space, and the structure induced by this operation cannot generally be determined solely from information geometry. In our recent work on unavoidable canonical nonlinearity (UCN), we established a non-arbitrary connection between the transport cost associated with discretization and the information-geometric indistinguishability of continuous probability distributions. Through the transport-information correspondence, the UCN quantify the information-theoretic cost due to discretization as the expected Kullback-Leibler divergence between nearby continuous distributions associated with the discretization geometry. In the original UCN construction, discretization is implemented by transporting the probability mass within each cell to its geometric centroid. The resulting transport-information correspondence, however, is derived under specific conditions involving the cell geometry, spatial coverage, and the vanishing-cell-scale limit. In this work, we introduce an alternative local-map discretization scheme in which the probability mass within each cell is transported to its own probability-weighted centroid. This change in the representative-point selection enables an exact expression for the transport cost of each cell in terms of the covariance matrix of its conditional probability distribution and the Fisher metric of the underlying continuous family. We establish an exact correspondence between the resulting transport cost and the expectation of the associated Fisher-induced quadratic form, without requiring uniform cell geometry, complete coverage of the sample space, or an infinitesimal cell-scale limit. The proposed construction thus generalizes the transport-information correspondence to finite-scale discretization using arbitrary collections of non-overlapping bounded convex sets.

</details>

### [5] [The Start Time of Ringdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05472)
*Adrien Kuntz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文确定了点粒子坠入史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）时准正规模（quasi-normal modes）求和的收敛性，指出动态振幅模型在粒子越过反弹半径后能再现波形，而常振幅求和仅在特定起始时间 t_S 之后收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清准正规模求和在点粒子坠落黑洞这一非平稳源情形下的收敛行为，并明确动态振幅模型与常振幅模型之间的关系。

Method: 采用点粒子坠入史瓦西黑洞的模型，对比动态振幅（dynamical amplitude）求和与常振幅（constant amplitude）求和的收敛性质，并以从最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）坠落的情形为具体算例。

Result: 动态振幅模型在粒子越过反弹半径（bounce radius）后可再现波形；常振幅求和仅在起始时间 t_S 之后收敛，t_S 对应粒子到达中心奇点（central singularity）的超前时间（advanced time）。对从 ISCO 坠落的情形，t_S 位于 22 模峰值之后约 9.4M 处。t_S 之后两种振幅模型等价。

Conclusion: 分析表明，t_S 之后动态与常振幅模型等价，并暗示任何"直接波（direct wave）"在 t_S 之后都必须退化为晚期尾（late-time tail）。

Abstract: We determine the convergence of quasi-normal modes sums for a point-particle plunging into a Schwarzschild black hole. While a dynamical amplitude model reproduces the waveform after the particle crosses the bounce radius, a constant amplitude sum converges only after the start time $t_S$, which corresponds to the advanced time at which the particle hits the central singularity. For a plunge from the ISCO, $t_S \simeq 9.4 M$ after the peak of the $22$ mode. After $t_S$, dynamical and constant amplitudes models are equivalent; our analysis also implies that any "direct wave" must reduce to the late-time tail after $t_S$.

</details>


### [6] [The quasi-topological space of quasi-topological gravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05485)
*Pablo Bueno,Robie A. Hennigar,Matteo G. Marrone,Ángel J. Murcia*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究含无限多项多项式密度塔的准拓扑引力（quasi-topological gravity）及其正则真空黑洞（regular vacuum black hole）解的一般性质，证明正质量正则解必具 de Sitter 核心且黑洞视界可任意多重退化，并表明允许正则黑洞的耦合空间是可缩的、正则性对耦合微扰稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则黑洞解通常被认为需要对高阶曲率耦合进行精细调节（fine-tuning），本文旨在厘清准拓扑引力中此类解是否存在普遍性，以及其视界结构与耦合空间拓扑性质。

Method: 在球对称背景下考察含无限多项式密度塔的准拓扑引力理论，通过解析论证研究正则真空解的核心结构与视界结构，并将结论推广至球对称背景下具有二阶方程（second-order equations）的更一般引力理论；同时以拓扑视角刻画允许正则黑洞的耦合空间，分析其在小形变下的稳定性。

Result: 证明每个正质量正则真空解必然具有 de Sitter 核心，而 Minkowski 与 anti-de Sitter 核心被排除；渐近平直（asymptotically flat）与渐近 anti-de Sitter 正则黑洞总存在极端构型（extremal configurations）；视界数目无上界且可出现任意高退化度的视界。允许正则黑洞的准拓扑理论空间是可缩的（contractible），因而是道路连通且单连通的，正则性在高阶耦合足够小形变下保持稳定；在 Spanier 意义下该空间自然构成可缩的准拓扑空间。

Conclusion: 任意有限阶曲率耦合定义的理论均可补全为产生正则黑洞解的理论，同时保持相同的低阶耦合集合。结果表明正则解出现在一大类准拓扑引力中，并不需要对耦合进行精细调节。

Abstract: We investigate general properties of quasi-topological theories of gravity involving infinite towers of polynomial densities and of their regular vacuum black hole solutions. We show that every positive-mass regular vacuum solution necessarily possesses a de Sitter core, while Minkowski and anti-de Sitter cores are excluded. Regarding the horizon structure, we prove that asymptotically flat and anti-de Sitter regular black holes always admit extremal configurations. We find that there is no upper bound on the number of horizons and that horizons of arbitrarily high degeneracy can arise. These results extend to more general theories of gravity with second-order equations on spherically symmetric backgrounds. Finally, we characterize the space of quasi-topological theories admitting regular black holes, showing that it is contractible (in particular, path-connected and simply connected) and that regularity is stable under suitably small deformations of the higher-order couplings. In the sense of Spanier, this space is naturally a contractible \emph{quasi-topological} space. Additionally, we prove that any theory defined by a set of couplings up to any arbitrarily high, but finite, curvature order can be completed into a theory yielding regular black hole solutions, while preserving the same set of lower-order couplings. These results show that regular solutions arise in a broad class of quasi-topological gravities and do not require fine-tuning of the couplings.

</details>


### [7] [Black hole quasinormal mode excitation and mixing for coupled fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05488)
*Caio F. B. Macedo,Orival R. de Medeiros,Emanuele Berti,Hector O. Silva,Sebastian H. Völkel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于参数化准正规模形式，在频域与时间域系统研究修改引力理论中扰动与额外自由度耦合下的准正规模谱与振幅激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞光谱学（black hole spectroscopy）已成为检验黑洞本质与广义相对论（general relativity）偏离的实验科学，而修改引力中引力扰动与额外自由度的耦合会导致模式混合与更复杂的铃宕（ringdown）信号，其频率与振幅特征尚需系统刻画。

Method: 采用非旋转黑洞的参数化准正规模（parametrized quasinormal mode）形式，频域上用直接积分与矩阵值连分式法（matrix-valued continued-fraction method）计算耦合系统的准正规模谱，并与时间域演化对比，用Prony方法与最小二乘拟合提取模式频率与振幅。

Result: 弱耦合区，引力主导与次级主导模式的振幅标度可由微扰论理解；即使频率移动温和，额外模式族的激发仍可能显著；并由数值时间演化给出代表性初始数据下可直接使用的振幅系数，示例包括动力学Chern-Simons引力及极向与轴向准正规模近共振耦合情形。

Conclusion: 该与理论无关的耦合系统刻画有助于量化下一代引力波探测器黑洞光谱学中的系统效应。

Abstract: The idea of testing the nature of black holes and possible deviations from general relativity from their oscillation spectrum, known as ``black hole spectroscopy'', has become an experimental science. In many modified theories of gravity, the gravitational perturbations couple to additional degrees of freedom, leading to mode mixing and to a more complex ringdown signal. We adopt the parametrized quasinormal mode formalism for nonrotating black holes to study coupled perturbations in modified gravity theories in order to understand how the interaction with additional fields affects the quasinormal mode frequencies and their amplitudes. We compute the quasinormal mode spectrum of the coupled system in the frequency domain using direct integration and a matrix-valued continued-fraction method. We then compare the results with time-domain evolutions, where we extract the mode frequencies and amplitudes using the Prony method as well as least-squares fitting. In the weak-coupling regime, the scaling of the amplitudes associated with the gravitational-led and secondary-led modes can be understood perturbatively. The excitation of additional families of ringdown modes can become significant even when the frequency shifts remain moderate. We use our numerical time evolutions to provide ready-to-use coefficients for the mode amplitudes for representative initial data configurations. We work out specific examples, including dynamical Chern-Simons gravity and the special near-resonant case of coupled perturbations between the polar and axial quasinormal modes. Our theory-agnostic characterization of coupled systems should be useful to quantify systematic effects in black hole spectroscopy with next-generation gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [8] [Geodesic completeness and photon propagation in a superconducting T-duality black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05630)
*Kimet Jusufi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文用Raychaudhuri方程研究弦论T-对偶黑洞的测地完备性，指出视界/中心处的膨胀发散对应同余的焦散而非测地线终点；进一步把黑洞核心建模为超导体（London响应），使Hamiltonian光线在重参数化意义下沿类时测地线运动并复现三类径向观测者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是双重的：一是厘清弦论T-对偶黑洞（string T-duality black hole）中径向测地线的完备性，特别是膨胀发散是否意味着测地线终止（与Schwarzschild时空的对比）；二是为黑洞核心提供物理模型，解释其正则中心与光子质量等特征。此外还需处理无质量Maxwell光线的对应问题。

Method: 采用Raychaudhuri方程分析任意比能ε的有质量径向粒子的测地完备性，给出精确的原时求积（proper-time quadratures）；利用lapse函数的解析性与关于r的偶性讨论穿过中心的正则延拓。对无质量Maxwell光线作类似分析。并构造含真空能偏移的对称破缺势（symmetry-breaking potential），以London响应μ²=4πκρ描述核心超导性，导出Hamiltonian光线与μ²g_{μν}类时测地线的对应关系及核心密度梯度的散焦贡献。

Because a non-zero Ricci curvature grows and by analogy with black hole scalarisation, the latter black hole formation mechanism is dubbed \emph{curvaturisation}. Abstract: We discuss a possible early Universe scenario, where radiation era is interrupted by the epoch of domination of domain walls eventually annihilating into particles. During this period the Universe undergoes accelerated expansion, while the comoving correlation length of the domain wall network remains constant. The network serves as a source of tensor modes, which get frozen upon exiting the horizon similarly to the case of inflationary fluctuations. The modes re-enter the horizon during the subsequent stage of radiation domination and since then contribute to the gravitational wave background. The spectral shape of gravitational waves predicted in this scenario is markedly different compared to the case of domain walls remaining sub-dominant all the way till annihilation. These gravitational waves are strong enough to be observed with present-day and future facilities in a broad range of frequencies. In particular, their energy density is sufficient to produce a significant impact on the effective number of neutrino species, unless the duration of wall domination is suitably constrained.

Motivation: 从恒星质量到中等质量（约1–10^5太阳质量）的致密天体丰度约束不足，原初黑洞（primordial black holes）与暗物质子晕（dark matter subhalos）等几乎不辐射电磁波，难以用传统手段探测；引力波微透镜的波动光学（wave-optics）效应提供了互补探针。

Method: 构建分层贝叶斯推断框架，对微引力透镜化引力波事件集合进行群体层面分析，约束幂律透镜质量函数斜率α_ML、透镜数密度n_0以及致密透镜占暗物质的比例f_DM；测试数据为基于真实双黑洞（binary black hole）群体生成的模拟微透镜化引力波信号。

Result: 约100个探测到的微透镜化事件即可同时约束α_ML与n_0，但两者间仍存在简并（degeneracy）。针对低质量主导（α_ML=2）、n_0=10^6 Mpc^-3的透镜群体，O4灵敏度下三年观测可得约130个可探测事件，并复原注入的暗物质比例f_DM≈1.2×10^-4，90%可信区间为7.3×10^-5≲f_DM≲1.8×10^-4。

Conclusion: 引力波微透镜结合分层贝叶斯群体推断，可有效约束低辐射致密天体群体参数，并对天体物理与宇宙学推断产生影响；但质量函数斜率与数密度之间的简并仍需进一步处理。

Abstract: The abundance of compact objects ranging from stellar to intermediate masses ($\sim 1 - 10^{5}M_{\odot}$) remains poorly constrained, particularly for populations such as primordial black holes and dark matter subhalos that emit little or no electromagnetic radiation. Gravitational-wave (GW) microlensing provides a complementary way to probe these populations through wave-optics effects imprinted on the GW signals, with implications for astrophysical and cosmological inference. We develop a hierarchical Bayesian framework to infer the population properties of microlenses from an ensemble of microlensed GW events, constraining the slope of the power-law lens mass function ($α_{\rm ML}$), the lens number density ($n_0$), and the fraction of dark matter in compact lenses ($f_{\rm DM}$). Using simulated microlensed GW signals drawn from a realistic binary black hole population, we find that $\sim 100$ detected microlensed events are able to constrain both $α_{\rm ML}$ and $n_0$, although a degeneracy between the two remains. For a low-mass-dominated lens population ($α_{\rm ML}=2$) with $n_0=10^6~\mathrm{Mpc}^{-3}$, a three-year observation at the O4 sensitivity yields $\sim 130$ detectable microlensed events and recovers the injected dark-matter fraction, $f_{\rm DM}\simeq 1.2 \times 10^{-4}$, with a 90% credible interval of $7.3 \times 10^{-5} \lesssim f_{\rm DM} \lesssim 1.8 \times 10^{-4}$.

</details>

### [33] [Unsupervised Neural Operators for Rotating Relativistic Stars: High-Fidelity Equation of State Inference and Pulsar Spin Evolution at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05790)
*Anarya Ray*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文提出基于物理信息神经算子（physics-informed neural operators）的求解器，以无监督方式高精度求解轴对称广义相对论流体静力学平衡方程，实现约10^3倍加速，从而支撑考虑自转的 scalable 物态方程（EoS）推断与双星演化中的重复星体结构计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转中子星（neutron stars, NSs）观测是基础物理探针，但轴对称广义相对论流体静力学平衡的强耦合偏微分方程求解成本高昂，导致重复结构计算难以承受；非旋转近似或近似自转修正会偏置物态方程（equation of state, EoS）推断，并限制族群合成（population synthesis）的保真度。

Method: 开发物理信息神经算子（physics-informed neural operators），在完全无监督（fully unsupervised）设置下训练，训练目标仅由偏微分方程约束，用于求解轴对称旋转中子星内部结构问题。

Result: 求解器达到高精度，计算成本可忽略，实现10^3倍加速且精度损失可忽略（negligible accuracy loss）。

Conclusion: 该求解器使可扩展的EoS推断能够自洽纳入自转效应与双星演化中的重复结构计算，并使考虑自转结构与EoS不确定性的自旋预测在族群合成中具备计算可行性。

Abstract: Observed populations of rotating neutron stars~(NSs) with millisecond-order periods are powerful probes of fundamental physics. Mass-radius measurements inform poorly constrained properties of degenerate nuclear matter, whereas high-fidelity population synthesis connects unknowns in NS formation and binary evolution to large radio-timing surveys. However, repeated structure calculations are prohibitive due to the computational cost of solving the highly coupled partial differential equations of axisymmetric general relativistic hydrostatic equilibrium that govern rotating NS interiors. Non-rotating calculations or approximate rotational corrections can bias equation of state inference and limit the fidelity of population synthesis in tracing the evolutionary history of progenitors and propagating equation of state uncertainties. In this \textit{Letter}, we develop physics-informed neural operators that can solve the axisymmetric problem with high accuracy and negligible computational cost. \textit{Our solvers are trained in a fully unsupervised setting, with the training objective informed solely by the partial differential equations, and achieve a $10^3\times$ speed-up with negligible accuracy loss}. These solvers enable scalable EoS inference that self-consistently accounts for rotation and repeated stellar-structure calculations during binary evolution, making spin predictions that account for rotational structure and EoS uncertainties computationally accessible to population synthesis.

</details>

### [34] [On isospectral potentials on periodic discrete graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06820)
*Natalia Saburova*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文在周期图（periodic graphs）上研究带周期（可能复值）势的离散薛定谔算子（discrete Schrödinger operators），利用谱不变量系统（system of spectral invariants）刻画等谱势集合，并证明在六角晶格（hexagonal lattice）与Kagome晶格上Floquet谱在一般情形下可唯一确定势（至平移与旋转对称）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是逆谱问题：Floquet谱（Floquet spectrum）能否唯一确定周期图上的势，即等谱势集合（isospectral potentials）有多大。已有谱不变量工具尚未用于六角晶格与Kagome晶格这类具体周期图上的唯一性判定。

Method: 采用周期图（periodic graphs）上周期势Q的离散薛定谔算子框架，借助先前工作给出的谱不变量系统（system of spectral invariants）来分析等谱势集合，并考虑对势Q作扰动时的一般性（genericity）。

Result: 在六角晶格（hexagonal lattice）与Kagome晶格上，Floquet谱在一般情形（相对于Q的扰动）下唯一确定势Q，唯一性精确到周期图的显式对称（平移与旋转）。

Conclusion: Floquet谱对这两类晶格上的周期势具有一般性的唯一决定作用（模去显然对称），说明等谱势集合在一般情况下退化为对称轨道；该结果将谱不变量方法推广到具体晶格的逆谱唯一性分析。

Abstract: We consider discrete Schrödinger operators with periodic (possibly complex valued) potentials $Q$ on periodic graphs. Using a system of spectral invariants obtained in \cite{S26}, we investigate the sets of isospectral potentials on periodic graphs. In particular, we show that the Floquet spectrum of the Schrödinger operators on the hexagonal lattice and on the Kagome lattice generically (with respect to perturbations of $Q$) determines the potential $Q$ uniquely up to the obvious symmetries (translations and rotations) in the periodic graph.

</details>

### [35] [Quasicrystals are Functionally Graded Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05478)
*Michael J. Landry,Dominic Bollettieri,Akhil Sadam,Denisse Córdova Carrizales,Mingda Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文将准晶体精确表示为具有空间梯度相位子（phason）的周期近似体（periodic approximant），据此建立受控的量子理论，并给出判断有限近似可靠性的控制参数及可实验检验的衍射方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶体（quasicrystals）具有长程有序但无平移周期性，其电子性质计算面临困难，缺乏可控制的近似理论框架。

Method: 将准晶体精确映射为带空间梯度相位子（phason）的周期近似体（periodic approximant），与功能梯度材料（functionally graded materials）建立联系；由此推导由局域布洛赫态（local Bloch states）量子几何（quantum metric）决定的态依赖控制参数，并构造在有限傅里叶窗口内对所有相位子应变阶数均正确的哈密顿量。

Result: 该哈密顿量能正确预言布拉格隙（Bragg gap）位置、准动量等价性（quasimomentum equivalence）及准周期本征态；理论经数值验证，并提出基于衍射（diffraction）的实验检验以观测相位子应变引起的能隙移动（gap shift）。

Conclusion: 该框架为准晶体电子结构与功能性质的预测提供了途径，可借助功能梯度材料的已有方法实现。

Abstract: Quasicrystals exhibit long-range order without translational periodicity, posing challenges to compute their electronic properties. Here we show that a quasicrystal can be represented exactly as a periodic approximant with a spatially graded phason, establishing a connection to functionally graded materials. Building on this connection, we develop a controlled quantum theory of quasicrystals. We derive a state-dependent control parameter governed by the quantum metric of local Bloch states, determining when finite approximations are reliable. We further construct a Hamiltonian that correctly predicts Bragg gap positions, quasimomentum equivalence, and quasiperiodic eigenstates to all orders in phason strain within a finite Fourier window. We verify the theory numerically and propose a diffraction-based experimental test of the gap shift induced by phason strain. Our framework opens a route to predicting the electronic structure and functional properties of quasicrystals using methods developed for functionally graded materials.

</details>


### [36] [Low cross-plane thermal conductivity of $κ$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05647)
*Seungwon Park,Jingyu Tang,Minsung An,Alan J. H. McGaughey,Lisa M. Porter,Robert F. Davis,Jonathan A. Malen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用频域热反射技术测量蓝宝石衬底上(001) κ-Ga₂O₃薄膜热导率，发现其随厚度（159–689 nm）仅由1.83增至2.26 W/m·K，弱尺寸依赖性表明多数本征声子平均自由程小于所测厚度范围，且该相热导率低于其他Ga₂O₃多晶型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: κ-Ga₂O₃作为Ga₂O₃多晶型之一在电子器件中具应用潜力，但缺乏对其薄膜横截面热导率（cross-plane thermal conductivity）及其尺寸、温度依赖性的实验数据，需评估其自热（self-heating）风险。

Method: 利用频域热反射（frequency-domain thermoreflectance, FDTR）测量沉积于c面蓝宝石衬底上(001) κ-Ga₂O₃薄膜的热导率，并结合包含声子-声子与声子-边界散射（phonon-phonon and phonon-boundary scattering）的第一性原理计算进行对照。

Result: 热导率随厚度由159 nm增至689 nm时从1.83 W/m·K升至2.26 W/m·K；689 nm薄膜在293–400 K内热导率随温度升高而下降，而159 nm薄膜温度依赖性更弱（边界散射更显著）；同厚度下其横截面热导率低于其他实验测量的Ga₂O₃多晶型。

Conclusion: κ-Ga₂O₃的低热导率源于其复杂晶体结构导致非谐性（anharmonicity）增强与声子群速度（phonon group velocity）降低，实验与既有预测一致；基于该材料的电子器件或需与高热导率衬底集成等热管理策略以抑制自热。

Abstract: The thermal conductivities of (001) $κ$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ thin films deposited on $c$-plane sapphire substrates were measured with frequency-domain thermoreflectance (FDTR). The thermal conductivity increases from 1.83 $^{+0.19}_{-0.18}$ W/m$\cdot$K to 2.26 $^{+0.35}_{-0.40}$ W/m$\cdot$K as the thicknesses increase from 159 nm to 689 nm. This weak thickness dependence indicates that the majority of the intrinsic phonon mean free paths are smaller than the range of investigated film thicknesses. First-principles-based calculations that include phonon-phonon and phonon-boundary scattering capture this size-dependent behavior and support our experimental observation. Temperature-dependent measurements of a 689 nm film from 293 to 400 K show a decrease in thermal conductivity with increasing temperature due to enhanced phonon-phonon scattering. A weaker temperature dependence is observed in a 159 nm film where phonon-boundary scattering becomes more prominent. At comparable film thicknesses, our measured $κ$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ films exhibit lower values of cross-plane thermal conductivity than the other experimentally measured Ga$_2$O$_3$ polymorphs. Our experimental observations are supported by prior predictions, which suggest that the low thermal conductivity of $κ$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ results from its complex crystal structure, which leads to increased anharmonicity and lower phonon group velocities relative to other polymorphs. Thermal management strategies, such as integration with high-thermal-conductivity substrates, may thus be required to mitigate self-heating for $κ$-Ga$_2$O$_3$-based electronic devices.

</details>


### [37] [DefectSkills: Guiding AI agents through first-principles calculations for point defects in semiconductors and insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05654)
*Zhenkun Yuan,Yizhi Zhu,Geoffroy Hautier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: DefectSkills 是一套结构化提示（structured prompts），为人机协同（human-in-the-loop）的 AI 智能体（AI agents）利用 VASP 开展半导体与绝缘体点缺陷（point defects）的第一性原理（first-principles）计算提供可复用的技能封装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理缺陷计算已可由 AI 智能体自主完成，但可靠且可重复的缺陷计算需要清晰的科学与技术指导，以帮助智能体消除歧义、避免在计算设置、执行、后处理与分析中出错。

Method: 提出 DefectSkills，将人类专家经验编码为结构化提示，覆盖晶胞弛豫（unit-cell relaxation）、电子结构与介电计算、相稳定性与化学势分析、带电缺陷超胞（charged-defect supercell）生成与计算、有限尺寸修正（finite-size corrections）、形成能与热力学分析、数据备份等环节，并以可复用的智能体技能形式打包。

Result: DefectSkills 通过技能封装实现专家经验复用，支持在各类智能体 AI 系统间一致地执行工作流，并促进缺陷计算的可重复性（reproducibility）。

Conclusion: 以结构化提示封装专家知识可为 AI 智能体执行 VASP 点缺陷第一性原理计算提供规范化的逐步指导，从而提升工作流一致性与计算可重复性。

Abstract: First-principles defect calculations can now be performed autonomously by AI agents, enabled by established computational methods and open-source Python toolkits. However, reliable and repeatable defect calculations require clear scientific and technical guidance that helps agents resolve ambiguities and avoid errors in calculation setup, execution, postprocessing, and analysis. Here, we present DefectSkills, a set of structured prompts that provides human-in-the-loop guidance to AI agents performing first-principles calculations of point defects in semiconductors and insulators using the Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package (VASP). The prompts encode human expertise and guide agents through unit-cell relaxation, electronic-structure and dielectric calculations, phase-stability and chemical-potential analysis, charged-defect supercell generation and calculations, finite-size corrections, formation-energy calculations and thermodynamic analysis, and data backup. By packaging this expertise as reusable agent skills, DefectSkills supports consistent workflow execution across agentic AI systems and reproducible defect calculations.

</details>


### [38] [Shock-driven Jetting at HMX Crystal Surface Asperities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05752)
*Ethan Holbrook,Matthew P. Kroonblawd,H. Keo Springer,Alejandro Strachan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用全原子非反应分子动力学模拟，研究HMX晶体正弦表面凸起在冲击加载下的射流响应，揭示冲击强度与凸起曲率主导射流速度与温度，射流形貌与HMX常压相图及液相窄温区密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冲击诱导射流（jetting）与再压缩（recompression）被认为是含能材料中热点（hot spot）形成的可能机制，但其认识仍不充分，特别是射流响应对冲击强度、几何特征、体系尺寸与晶向的依赖缺乏定量刻画。

Method: 对晶体炸药HMX的冲击加载正弦表面凸起开展全原子非反应分子动力学（MD）模拟，系统覆盖约4–47 GPa的冲击压力区间（涵盖起爆起始与稳态爆轰两种响应区），并考察冲击强度、凸起几何、体系尺寸和晶体取向的影响。

Result: 冲击强度与凸起曲率对射流速度、温度和形貌影响显著，而特征尺寸与晶体取向作用相对有限；射流形貌与HMX常压相图及其液相存在的窄温度区间强相关——局部温度超过熔点时可形成连贯射流，超过液-气转变温度时似可形成分子羽流（molecular plume），此行为不同于液相温区更宽的金属。

Conclusion: 提出射流形貌与常压相图相关联的简明假说，可在其他炸药和金属中检验，并为后续非反应与反应MD模拟中射流撞击与再压缩形成热点的研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Shock-induced jetting and recompression are plausible mechanisms for hot spot formation in energetic materials, but remain poorly understood. To this end, we apply all-atom nonreactive molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of a shock-loaded sinusoidal surface asperity in the crystalline explosive HMX (octahydro-1,3,5,7-tetranitro-1,3,5,7-tetrazocine) to quantify the roles of shock strength, asperity geometry, system size, and crystallographic orientation in determining the jetting response. Our simulations span a shock pressure interval of $\approx$4-47 GPa, which captures responses in both detonation initiation and steady-state detonation regimes. We find that shock strength and asperity curvature exert strong influence on jet velocity, temperature, and morphology, while feature size and crystallographic orientation play more modest roles. Jet morphology correlates strongly with the HMX ambient-pressure phase diagram and the narrow temperature range associated with its liquid phase. Coherent jets form when focusing and dissipation are sufficient to drive local temperatures above the melting point, while formation of molecular plumes is seemingly connected to jet temperatures exceeding the liquid-vapor transition temperature. Formation of plumes is unlike the jetting responses seen in metals, which typically exhibit liquid phases that are stable over wider temperature intervals. This work proposes a simple hypothesis for the connection between jet morphology and ambient-pressure phase diagrams that can be readily tested in other explosives and metals. It also provides a foundation for ongoing nonreactive and reactive MD simulations of jet impact and recompression to quantify the role played by jets in forming hot spots in HMX-based explosives.

</details>


### [39] [Atomic-Scale Phonon Engineering for Enhanced Thermal Transport in Wafer-Scale-Bonded $β$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$/SiC Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05810)
*J. Liu,R. Mao,Z. Liu,Y. Yin,S. Li,F. Sun,B. Liu,Z. Wang,P. Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出“声子桥接（phonon-bridging）”策略，在β-Ga₂O₃与SiC键合界面引入纳米晶Si中间层，以桥接两者失配的声子态，将界面热导提升至165 MW m⁻² K⁻¹，为β-Ga₂O₃/SiC体系已报道的最高值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将低导热材料键合到高导热衬底可缓解散热问题并规避常规外延（epitaxy）的晶格匹配约束，但晶格失配导致两侧声子态（phonon states）不匹配，加之界面质量差引起声子散射，二者共同阻碍跨界面声子输运，使界面热阻（interfacial thermal resistance）成为关键瓶颈，因而需要精细的界面工程（interface engineering）。

Method: 以β-Ga₂O₃/SiC为模型体系，通过晶圆键合（wafer bonding）设计并制备纳米尺度晶态Si中间层，用以桥接两种材料失配的声子态；结合空间分辨电子能量损失谱（spatially resolved electron energy-loss spectroscopy）与分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）表征与分析界面声子输运行为。

Result: 表征与模拟表明，Si中间层为跨界面声子输运开辟了新通道；经界面工程的样品界面热导（interfacial thermal conductance）达到165 MW m⁻² K⁻¹，为β-Ga₂O₃/SiC界面已报道的最高值。

Conclusion: 该结果为β-Ga₂O₃器件的热管理提供了有效途径，并提出一种可推广的声子桥接界面构建思路，用于改善异质体系（heterogeneous systems）的散热性能。

Abstract: Bonding semiconductors to substrates with high thermal conductivity is a promising way to solve the heat-dissipation problem of materials that transport heat poorly, and it avoids the lattice-matching constraints of conventional epitaxy. However, the lattice mismatch remains, leaving the two materials with mismatched phonon states, while poor interface quality scatters phonons even more. Together, these effects block phonon transport across the bonded interface and make interfacial thermal resistance a critical bottleneck, calling for careful interface engineering. Here, $β$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$/SiC serves as a model system for a phonon-bridging strategy. A nanoscale crystalline Si interlayer is designed and fabricated by wafer bonding to bridge the mismatched phonon states of $β$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$ and SiC. Spatially resolved electron energy-loss spectroscopy and molecular dynamics simulations show that this Si layer opens new channels for phonon transport across the interface. The engineered interface raises the interfacial thermal conductance to 165 MW m$^{-2}$ K$^{-1}$ -- the highest value yet reported for $β$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$/SiC interfaces. These results offer an effective way to manage heat in $β$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$ devices and point to a general approach for building phonon-bridging interfaces that improve heat dissipation in heterogeneous systems.

</details>


### [40] [Ferroelectricity and Peierls transition in MOI$_2$ (M=Ta, Nb): a first-principle analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05836)
*Carmel Dansou,Charles Paillard,Laurent Bellaiche*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用第一性原理计算与Landau型能量分析，指出NbOI₂的铁电极化畸变并非源于Nb链的Peierls二聚化，而是独立序参量；MOI₂家族中存在极性模P、反极性模η与Peierls模δ三种模式，其稳定性与耦合随金属元素变化。

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D-STEM 被广泛用于映射铁性织构（如畴壁、涡旋、麦纫、斯格明子）中的极化，但这些织构伴随局部应变、晶格转动与取向差，会调制用于推断极序的衍射特征；即使极小的偏离晶带轴也可能产生与极化信号无法区分的衬度，因此需定量评估倾转敏感性。

Result: 仅 0.01–0.05 度的倾转即可使多数 Friedel 对盘不对称性反转；最稳健的盘对在 BaTiO3 中于 0.04 度、PbTiO3 中于 0.14 度、硼酸盐中于 0.18 度反转，产生与极化等大反向的衬度。菊池带衬度是唯一无需已知样品厚度即可报告绝对极性的信号，但其有效范围仅为 PbTiO3 中 0.09 度、BaTiO3 中 0.015 度、硼酸盐中 0.02 度。

Conclusion: 研究为各信号与材料定义了倾转容差（tilt tolerance），并提出区分极化衬度与取向差的实际判据；该分析同样适用于基于扫描电子显微镜的衍射技术，包括电子背散射衍射（electron backscatter diffraction, EBSD）。

Abstract: Four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) is increasingly used to map polarisation in ferroic textures such as domain walls, vortices, merons and skyrmions. The analysis also applies to scanning electron microscopy-based diffraction, including electron backscatter diffraction.

  <summary>Details</summary>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We analyze the unusual microstructure resulting from the cubic to orthorhombic phase transformation in the shape-memory alloy ${\rm Ti}_{76}{\rm Nb}_{22}{\rm Al}_{2}$. This seems to represent an approximate experimental realization of a deformation having a finite number of incompatible deformation gradients, which has been known to exist only as a theoretical possibility. Because the middle eigenvalue $γ$ of the transformation strain is very close to 1, the phase transformation in this alloy takes place via nucleation of exact austenite-martensite interfaces rather than, as usual for other alloys, by classical austenite-martensite interfaces separating the austenite from a finely-twinned laminate of martensite. According to the moving mask approximation of Della Porta this leads to the question whether a microstructure can be formed from the set $H$ of the twelve matrices ${\mathbf V}_i$ on the martensite energy wells that are rank-one connected to the identity, even though these matrices are mutually incompatible.

This is a consequence of $H$ forming a degenerate $T_{12}$ configuration in the sense of Kirchheim. In fact there are such microstructures supported on just 8 of the 12 matrices in $H$. However there is no deformation satisfying the same boundary condition whose gradient belongs to $H$ almost everywhere. An additional prediction, confirmed experimentally, is that the macroscopic deformation gradient equals the identity everywhere. We also analyse the approximate compatibility of the ${\mathbf V}_i$. Although mutually incompatible, they are nearly compatible, with some pairs much more nearly compatible than others, these pairs corresponding to interfaces that are more frequently observed.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压合成的镧多氢化物（lanthanum polyhydrides）具有近室温超导性，但其氢亚晶格（hydrogen sublattice）的精确排布因氢原子X射线散射形状因子过小而难以实验解析，制约了对强电声耦合（electron-phonon coupling）机制的理解。

By implementing a similarity metric, we screen the computationally generated configurations against experimental powder X-ray diffraction data. We specifically identify the trigonal $R\bar{3}$-LaH$_{12}$ and pseudo-hexagonal $C222_1$-La$_2$H$_{24}$ phases, which demonstrate close agreement with the experimental X-ray diffraction data. Crucially, our lattice dynamics analysis using the stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation (SSCHA) indicates that these structures are dynamically stable. We reveal the presence of molecular H$_2$ units and soft phonon modes in the trigonal LaH$_{12}$ that suppresses superconductivity ($T_c \ge 30$ K), while the hydrogen clathrate network in the $C222_1$ phase drives strong electron-phonon interactions, yielding the coupling parameters of $λ\approx 1.83$, $ω_{\log} \approx 87.5$ meV and transition temperature of $T_c\approx 150$ K at 150 GPa.

### [50] [Explicit pore pre-conditioning and inverse design of amorphous nanoporous carbon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06614)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔碳（porous carbon）在储能、气体捕获与水净化等应用中性能高度依赖孔几何结构，而现有原子尺度模拟多以分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟合成过程，结果依赖原子间势（interatomic potential），且孔网络只是输出而非可指定的设计量。

Method: 将孔网络作为输入：按目标孔径分布（pore size distribution）绘制孔隙并填充至规定体积分数，用随机成环放置法在孔隙周围生长碳原子，再以粒子群优化从结构指纹（structural fingerprint）反演构建参数，最后经短时退火（anneal）完善网络。

Conclusion: 短时退火在不改变孔径分布形状的前提下恢复了参考结构的配位（coordination）。分子动力学仍负责局部化学描述，而孔几何成为面向特定应用的设计变量（design variable）。

Abstract: Porous carbons store energy, capture gases and purify water, and their performance depends strongly on the geometry of their pores. Here we show that the pore network can be an input: pores drawn from a target size distribution fill a prescribed volume fraction, carbon is grown around them by stochastic ring placement, and a particle swarm recovers the construction parameters from a structural fingerprint. The search reproduces the density, surface area and pore volume of a carbon produced by quenched molecular dynamics within about 5 %, together with its pore size distribution. As a stress test, it also recovers a bimodal distribution, and the search history reveals which parameters the targets constrain and flags a failed search. A short anneal completes the network, recovering the coordination of the reference while the pore size distribution keeps its shape. Molecular dynamics still supplies the local chemistry, but the pore geometry becomes a design variable for a given application.

</details>


### [51] [Anomalous thermal transport in isotope-enriched germanium carbide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06663)
*Wenjiang Zhou,Bai Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过求解包含三声子与四声子相互作用的玻尔兹曼输运方程并结合机器学习分子动力学模拟，发现同位素纯立方碳化锗（GeC）室温热导率达1018 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹，其源于强烈的同位素增强效应与较弱的四声子散射抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电子器件散热需求接近1000 W cm⁻²，亟需热导率超过1000 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹的材料，而具备超高热导率的候选材料仍十分有限。

Method: 求解同时考虑三声子（three-phonon）与四声子（four-phonon）相互作用的玻尔兹曼输运方程（Boltzmann transport equation），并以先进的机器学习分子动力学（machine-learning molecular dynamics）模拟进行交叉验证。

Result: 同位素纯立方GeC在室温下热导率达1018 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹；其高κ源于289%的显著同位素增强效应与仅22%的四声子散射抑制，尽管存在较宽的声学—光学声子带隙（acoustic-optical phonon gap）。此外，GeC偏离传统的Leibfried-Schlomann公式，并在高温下随静水压呈现非单调变化。

Conclusion: 同位素富集的GeC被确立为一种具有超高热导率（ultrahigh κ）的材料，并可作为探索新奇声子物理（phonon physics）的理想平台。

Abstract: Advances in modern electronics demand the dissipation of heat fluxes approaching 1000 W cm$^{-2}$, which drives an urgent need for materials with thermal conductivity ($κ$) exceeding 1000 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$. Here, by solving the Boltzmann transport equation considering both three- and four-phonon interactions, we observe that isotopically pure cubic germanium carbide (GeC) exhibits a remarkable $κ$ of 1018 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$ at room temperature, which is further corroborated by state-of-the-art machine-learning molecular dynamics simulations. Two unique mechanisms underlie this anomalously high $κ$: a remarkably strong isotopic enhancement effect (289%) and a surprisingly modest suppression due to four-phonon scattering (22%)-despite the presence of a wide acoustic-optical phonon gap. Moreover, we find that GeC defies the conventional Leibfried-Schlomann formula and exhibits a non-monotonic variation of $κ$ with external hydrostatic pressure at elevated temperatures. Our findings establish isotope-enriched GeC as a material of ultrahigh $κ$ and an ideal platform for exploring exotic phonon physics.

</details>


### [52] [First-principles study of the formation of Group-IV--vacancy color centers in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06671)
*Xiangru Han,Adam Gali*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过第一性原理计算揭示，XV 色心虽在能量上易于形成，但其易与碳自间隙或额外空位结合生成 X_s 与 XV_2 等竞争缺陷，导致分离空位构型不稳定、产率偏低；费米能级调控或可抑制这些缺陷转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XV（X=Si、Ge、Sn、Pb，V 为碳空位）色心因其优良的光学与自旋性质被视为量子计算与量子信息科学（quantum information science）的有前景平台，但离子注入制备不可避免引入空位与碳自间隙（C_i），其微观形成机制不清，限制了色心的产率与稳定性。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）系统研究空位 V 与碳自间隙 C_i 在 XV 色心形成过程中的作用，并结合结合能、扩散势垒与电荷跃迁能级（charge transition levels）分析竞争缺陷反应。

Result: 计算表明 XV 形成在能量上有利，具有较大结合能（binding energies）与较低扩散势垒（diffusion barriers）；但 XV 易俘获 C_i 形成替位缺陷 X_s，或俘获额外 V 形成 XV_2 复合体。电荷跃迁能级显示，反应物电荷符号相反时不会相遇，同号电荷时的库仑排斥（Coulomb repulsion）仅在强 n 型或 p 型条件下才成为俘获 C_i 的阻碍。

Conclusion: 这些竞争性缺陷反应破坏分离空位构型（split-vacancy configuration）的稳定性，可解释 XV 色心形成产率偏低、实际浓度远低于注入剂量的现象；结果阐明了该本征不稳定性的微观起源，并提示费米能级控制（Fermi-level control）或可抑制缺陷转化、稳定分离空位构型。

Abstract: $XV$ ($X$ is Si, Ge, Sn, or Pb, and $V$ is the carbon vacancy) color centers are promising platforms for quantum computing and quantum information science due to their favorable optical and spin properties. They are commonly fabricated by ion implantation, which inevitably introduces vacancies and carbon self-interstitials (C$_i$) into the lattice. However, the microscopic formation mechanism remains unclear, limiting the yield and stability of $XV$ centers. Here, first-principles calculations are conducted to systematically investigate the roles of $V$ and C$_i$ in $XV$ formation. We find that $XV$ formation is energetically favorable, with large binding energies and low diffusion barriers. Nevertheless, $XV$ readily captures C$_i$ to form substitutional defect $X_s$, or captures an additional $V$ to form the complex $XV_2$. Charge transition levels show that oppositely charged reactants never meet in these reactions, and that Coulomb repulsion blocks them only when both reactants carry charges of the same sign, which for the capture of C$_i$ requires strongly n- or p-type conditions. These competing defect reactions destabilize the split-vacancy configuration and could account for the low formation yield of $XV$ centers, so that they appear at concentrations well below those expected from the implanted dose. Our results elucidate the microscopic origin of this intrinsic instability and suggest that Fermi-level control may suppress defect conversions and stabilize the split-vacancy configuration.

</details>


### [53] [Millikelvin magnetometry up to 30~T using a multi-cross micro-Hall sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06676)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向极低温（∼15 mK）与强磁场（达30 T）条件下对百微米至毫米级块体样品的高灵敏度直流磁化测量需求，需要可靠且可重复的磁测量手段与样品安装方案。

Conclusion: 该微型霍尔传感器结合所提供的样品安装方案，可实现极低温强磁场下高灵敏度、可重复的直流磁化测量，适用于多种量子磁性材料研究。

Abstract: We report a multi-cross micro-Hall sensor fabricated from an MBE-grown GaAs/AlGaAs heterostructure for high-sensitivity magnetometry. The device enables dc magnetization measurements at temperatures down to $\sim$15~mK and in dc magnetic fields up to 30 T. Bulk samples with dimensions ranging from 100~$μ$m to 1~mm can be measured with a resolution better than 1$~μ$T with a 1~$μ$A excitation at 13.77 Hz. Test-of-performance measurements were carried out by probing the field-dependent magnetization plateaus of the triangular-lattice quantum magnet K$_{2}$Co$_{2}$(SeO$_{3}$)$_{3}$ at 20~mK, the antiferromagnetic transition of K${_3}$GdTe${_2}$O${_9}$ near 220 mK via temperature-dependent magnetization, and the quantum oscillations in a ruthenium single crystal. A simple and reliable sample-mounting setup that ensures reproducible magnetic measurements is provided.

</details>


### [54] [Evolutionary Design of Interpretable Spatial Descriptors for Structure-Property Relationship Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06811)
*Aditya Raghavan,Sergei V Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种构建空间描述符（spatial descriptors）的框架，用于预测局部谱（local spectra）与迟滞回线（hysteresis loops），并利用遗传算法（genetic algorithm）优化空间划分与池化操作配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的回归（image-based regression）中，邻域大小（image neighborhood size）与空间分辨率（spatial resolution）共同决定预测器可用信息：过小邻域会遗漏相关结构，过大且精细采样的邻域则引入冗余或无关信息，二者权衡缺乏系统刻画方法。

Method: 对回归模型进行基准测试（benchmark），用 SHAP 图（SHAP maps）考察其空间归因（spatial attribution），并探究图像块大小（patch size）与压缩对预测精度—描述符维度（descriptor dimensionality）关系的影响；随后以遗传算法优化空间划分及各区域均值、最小值、最大值池化（mean/min/max pooling）的分配，并构建笛卡尔与极坐标（Cartesian and polar）两种组织形式。

Result: 每个保留区域给出图像的显式测量值，使搜索可在预测误差与描述符数值个数之间取得平衡；所得描述符可揭示何处需要空间细节、何处区域汇总（regional summaries）已足够，以及这些选择如何依赖于预测器与所测响应。

Conclusion: 该框架为局部谱与迟滞回线的空间描述符设计提供了系统方法；在确立相应遗传算法规则后，同一方法可推广至分子（molecules）与固体（solids）。

Abstract: Structure property prediction is a classical task in machine learning, ranging from molecular activity and materials properties to local functional responses measured in microscopy. For image-based regression, two choices define the information available to the predictor: the size of the image neighborhood and the spatial resolution retained within it. A small neighborhood can omit relevant surrounding structure, whereas a large, finely sampled neighborhood can introduce redundant or irrelevant information. Here, we develop a framework for constructing spatial descriptors for prediction of local spectra and hysteresis loops. We benchmark regression models, use SHAP maps to examine their spatial attribution, and explore the effect of the patch size and compression to establish the relationship between predictive accuracy and descriptor dimensionality. We then use a genetic algorithm to optimize spatial partitions and the assignment of mean, minimum, and maximum pooling operations. Each retained region contributes an explicit measurement of the image, allowing the search to balance prediction error against the number of descriptor values. Cartesian and polar constructions provide complementary ways to organize this information. The resulting descriptors make it possible to examine where spatial detail is needed, where regional summaries are sufficient, and how these choices depend on the predictor and the measured response. The same approach can be used for molecules and solids once the genetic algorithm rules for these are established.

</details>


### [55] [Thermally Conditioned Time-Series Forecasting of Grain Growth in Polycrystalline Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06843)
*Eliane Younes,Elie Hachem,Marc Bernacki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将晶粒长大（grain growth）预测从等温条件扩展至非等温条件，通过在循环神经网络框架中显式嵌入热信息（瞬时温度与热速率），实现对晶粒尺寸分布（grain size distribution）演化的秒级预测，交叉验证平均相对误差约 2.99%–4.51%，决定系数超过 0.99。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶粒长大显著影响多晶材料（polycrystalline materials）的微观组织与力学性能，而前沿追踪/前沿捕捉（front-tracking / front-capturing）等数值模拟计算成本高，难以用于长时或非等温热循环；此前基于循环神经网络（recurrent neural network）的时间序列预测仅在等温条件下得到验证。

Method: 将晶粒尺寸分布视为时间序列，用卷积神经网络（convolutional neural network）编码分布、用专门的多层感知机（multilayer perceptron）嵌入热描述符，并以循环网络预测时间演化；在每个时间步输入瞬时温度与热速率，使模型适应变化的热条件，训练与评估数据由经验证的前沿追踪模拟在多种非等温热路径下生成。

Result: 热条件化模型能准确再现复杂热历史下的晶粒长大动力学；在全部模拟的 k 折交叉验证（k-fold cross-validation）中，一小时循环与四小时循环的平均相对误差分别为 2.99% 与 4.51%，决定系数（coefficient of determination）均超过 0.99；在四条复杂度递增的未见热历史对应的十组模拟上，平均相对误差为 1.91%–14.66%。

Conclusion: 显式引入热信息的深度学习代理框架（surrogate framework）可在非等温条件下高效预测晶粒长大，替代昂贵的偏微分方程（PDE）数值模拟；但其在未见热历史下的误差上限（14.66%）表明泛化能力仍随热路径复杂度上升而下降。

Abstract: Grain growth during heat treatment strongly affects the microstructure of polycrystalline materials and, consequently, their mechanical properties. Although numerical methods such as front-tracking or front-capturing simulations can accurately describe grain growth, their high computational cost limits their use for long or non-isothermal thermal cycles. In previous work, grain growth was successfully predicted under isothermal conditions by treating grain size distributions as time series and forecasting their evolution using recurrent neural networks, allowing complete grain growth trajectories to be generated in seconds rather than through costly PDE-based simulations. In the present work, this forecasting framework is reformulated to handle non-isothermal conditions by explicitly incorporating thermal information into the model architecture. Grain size distributions are encoded using a convolutional neural network, thermal descriptors are embedded through a dedicated multilayer perceptron, and temporal evolution is predicted using a recurrent network. Instantaneous temperature and thermal rate are provided at each time step, allowing the model to adapt its predictions to changing thermal conditions. The framework is trained and evaluated on datasets generated from validated front-tracking simulations across a wide range of non-isothermal thermal paths. The results show that the thermally conditioned model accurately reproduces grain growth dynamics under complex thermal histories, achieving, in k-fold cross-validation over all simulations, mean relative errors of 2.99% (one-hour cycles) and 4.51% (four-hour cycles) with coefficients of determination exceeding 0.99, and mean relative errors between 1.91% and 14.66% on ten simulations following four unseen thermal histories of increasing complexity.

</details>


### [56] [Longitudinal and Transverse Thermoelectric Properties in Topological Quantum Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06865)
*Jyoti Duhan,Anil Kumar B. M.,Satya N. Guin,Koushik Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦具有非平庸拓扑序（non-trivial topological order）的量子材料，系统讨论纵向与横向热电效应（longitudinal/transverse thermoelectricity），并探讨其结构、拓扑与输运性质之间的关联及可调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期实验发现的Bi、Sb、Sn、Pb基硫属化合物等三维拓扑材料，长期也被认为是优良热电材料，这一巧合提示拓扑带结构与热电性能之间存在内在联系，值得系统梳理。

Method: 采用综述方式，对纵向与横向两类热电效应进行归纳：横向响应可由外磁场（普通能斯特效应，ordinary Nernst effect）、本征剩磁或非零贝里曲率（Berry curvature，对应反常能斯特效应，anomalous Nernst effect）驱动，并引入具有轴依赖导电极性（axis-dependent conduction polarity）的角极化材料（goniopolar materials）作为新类别。

Result: 重原子带来强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling），促发能带反转（band inversion）与非平庸拓扑；线性色散的类狄拉克能带（Dirac-like bands）带来高电迁移率，多能谷的能带反转电子结构带来高塞贝克系数（Seebeck coefficient），并伴随低晶格热导率。

Conclusion: 非平庸拓扑材料的热电性质可通过结构、拓扑与输运的关联加以理解，并有望在电子-声子相互作用（electron-phonon interactions）等传统机制之外实现性能调控。

Abstract: Thermoelectric materials can be topologically trivial or non-trivial, enabling reversible interconversion of heat and electricity using a solid-state technology. Incidentally, few of the first experimentally discovered 3D topological materials, namely, Bi, Sb, Sn and Pb-based chalcogenides, were long known as good thermoelectric materials. In these compounds, the heavy atoms that give rise to strong spin-orbit coupling often become essential for band inversion and non-trivial band topology. They lead to low lattice thermal conductivity, linearly dispersing Dirac-like bands induce high electrical mobility, and a band-inverted electronic structure with multiple band extrema gives rise to high Seebeck coefficients. In materials, where the applied temperature gradient induces electrical voltage (Seebeck effect) in the same direction, they are termed as longitudinal thermoelectrics. On the other hand, when the generated electrical voltage and applied thermal current remain orthogonal to each other, the materials are termed as transverse thermoelectrics. The transverse response can be driven by an additional external magnetic field (ordinary Nernst effect), intrinsic remanent magnetisation, or non-zero Berry curvature of a material, which arises from their non-trivial topology (anomalous Nernst effect). We discuss another class of quantum materials, known as goniopolar materials, that exhibit axis-dependent conduction polarity and hence transverse thermoelectric effects, which are based on mechanisms of longitudinal thermoelectric properties. In this review, we cover both types of thermoelectricity, focusing on materials that possess non-trivial topological order. We make connections between their structures, topology and transport properties and suggest tunability of thermoelectric properties going beyond conventional mechanisms such as electron-phonon interactions.

</details>

### [57] [Cryogenic suppression of correlated loss in Rydberg atom arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06613)
*Zhenpu Zhang,Brendan P. Marsh,Ting You Tan,Quinn Manning,Daniel H. Slichter,Adam M. Kaufman,Cindy A. Regal*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用低温屏蔽抑制黑体辐射（blackbody radiation, BBR），将BBR确认为里德堡原子阵列中雪崩衰减（avalanche decay）的主导触发因素，并建立无自由参数的速率模型定量描述雪崩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBR会驱动里德堡原子跃迁至邻近反宇称态，缩短里德堡寿命，并长期被怀疑触发导致关联原子损失的“雪崩”事件，这构成里德堡量子科学的核心挑战。

Method: 采用可低温或室温运行的里德堡原子阵列，以45 K辐射屏蔽（radiation shield）降低环境BBR，并在不同BBR环境下对比里德堡缀饰（Rydberg-dressed）原子阵列，最多含70个原子、n=50与n=60两种缀饰。

Result: BBR诱导衰减率被压制30%–50%，对应有效黑体温度T_eff≈190 K，n=50至75的里德堡nS寿命延长；低温下雪崩率随BBR衰减同步受抑，且无参数速率模型与实测雪崩率一致。

Conclusion: 研究实现了对雪崩衰减的定量描述，表明进一步改进BBR屏蔽可将关联损失抑制至现有装置可达的T_eff=45 K，为中性原子量子处理器（neutral-atom quantum processors）容错运行提供缓解关联误差的途径。

Abstract: Blackbody radiation (BBR) poses a central challenge for quantum science with Rydberg atoms. By driving transitions to nearby opposite-parity states, BBR shortens Rydberg lifetimes and has long been suspected to trigger "avalanche" events known to cause correlated atom loss. Here, we suppress avalanche decay and establish BBR as its dominant trigger using a Rydberg atom array that operates either cryogenically or at room temperature. A $45~\text{K}$ radiation shield reduces ambient BBR, extending Rydberg $nS$ lifetimes for $n$ ranging from 50 to 75. BBR-induced decay rates are suppressed by 30%-50%, corresponding to an effective blackbody temperature $T_{\rm eff}\approx190~\text{K}$. This enables controlled comparisons of Rydberg-dressed atom arrays operating in different BBR environments. Arrays of up to 70 atoms demonstrate cryogenically suppressed avalanche rates, tracking the suppression in BBR-induced decay, for both $n=50$ and $n=60$ dressing. A parameter-free rate model agrees with the measured avalanche rates after accounting for frequency-dependent cryogenic shielding, photoionization-triggered avalanches, laser phase noise, BBR decay channels, and Rydberg interaction effects. We demonstrate a quantitative description of avalanche decay and show that improved BBR shielding should further suppress correlated loss to the $T_\text{eff}=45~\text{K}$ achievable in the current apparatus. Our results establish a route to mitigating correlated errors that otherwise impede fault-tolerant operation in neutral-atom quantum processors.

</details>

### [58] [Classification and Signatures of Dissipative Quantum Hall-Superconductor Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05555)
*Evgenii Zheltonozhskii,Barak A. Katzir,Yuval Oreg,Erez Berg,Netanel H. Lindner*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文针对交叉Andreev反射（crossed Andreev reflection, CAR）实验中观测到的非量子化边缘电导，提出超导体亚能隙模式耗散机制，并给出分数量子霍尔-超导结相图，可定性解释温度依赖行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邻近超导诱导的分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall, FQH）边缘可承载宇称费米子零模（parafermionic zero modes, PZM），是拓扑量子计算（topological quantum computing）的候选平台；但近期实验测得的CAR电导偏小且非量子化，温度依赖存在两种截然不同的行为，尚缺乏理论解释。

Method: 引入超导体中亚能隙模式（sub-gap modes）导致的耗散通道，构建FQH-超导结的理论模型并绘制相图（phase diagram）；分析Jain态下整数模（integer mode）与耗散通道强耦合的条件。

Result: 对于Jain态，温度无关的非量子化电导仅在存在与耗散通道强耦合的整数模时出现（如填充因子ν=1±1/m，m≥3为奇数）；所预测的温度趋势与实验定性一致——ν=2/3与整数态呈温度无关电导，而ν=1/3与ν=2/5不符合。

Conclusion: 耗散至超导体亚能隙模式可解释CAR电导的非量子化与温度依赖差异；建议通过比较ν=4/3与2/3来检验整数模与分数量子模相对手性（relative chirality）的预测影响。

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall (FQH) edges with proximity-induced superconductivity can host parafermionic zero modes (PZMs), making them a promising platform for topological quantum computing. Recent experiments [Gül et al., Phys. Rev. X 12, 021057 (2022)] measured the edge conductance from crossed Andreev reflection (CAR) between counter-propagating FQH edges separated by a narrow superconductor. The observed conductance was small and non-quantized, with two distinct behaviors: some states show temperature-independent conductance, while others show conductance that grows as temperature decreases. We explain these results by dissipation to sub-gap modes in the superconductor and present a phase diagram for the FQH-superconductor junction. We show that for Jain states, temperature-independent non-quantized conductance occurs only in the presence of an integer mode (such as at filling $ν=1\pm\frac{1}{m}$, with odd $m\geq3$) strongly coupled to the dissipative channel. The predicted temperature trends qualitatively agree with the experiment, which shows temperature-independent conductance for $ν=2/3$ and integer states, but not for $ν=1/3$ and $ν=2/5$. We propose comparing $ν=4/3$ and $2/3$ to test the predicted influence of the relative chirality of integer and fractional modes.

</details>


### [59] [Bypass Transition via the Non-Hermitian Skin Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05563)
*Konghao Sun,Haiping Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 非厄米趋肤晶格（non-Hermitian skin lattice）的方向性放大与非线性反馈共同驱动了一类“旁路转捩（bypass transition）”，使静止态在线性稳定时仍可被有限局部激励触发持续运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究关注非厄米趋肤效应（skin effect）对传播信号的方向性放大，但该放大与非线性反馈联合作用下的全局动力学（尤其转捩行为）尚不明确；本文旨在建立趋肤效应通往旁路转捩的路径。

Method: 提出回归映射理论（return-map theory），用于预测触发阈值、阈值随方向性增益（directional gain）的标度关系，以及近临界动力学的主导不稳定性；并结合有限局部激励与弱噪声两种触发情形进行分析。

Result: 有限局部激励可触发持续的周期或混沌运动，而静止态保持线性稳定；触发阈值随链长呈指数下降；弱噪声同样可触发持续运动，在固定观测时间下其特征点火长度（ignition length）随噪声减弱呈对数增长。

Conclusion: 研究确立了由趋肤效应导致的旁路转捩机制，为非线性波动动力学的预测与调控提供了新途径。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian skin lattices can directionally amplify propagating signals. Here we show that this amplification and nonlinear feedback together drive a bypass transition to self-sustained motion, familiar from shear flows. A finite local excitation can trigger sustained periodic or chaotic motion while the resting state remains linearly stable. We develop a return-map theory that predicts the triggering threshold, its scaling with directional gain, and the instability governing near-critical dynamics. The threshold decreases exponentially with chain length. Weak noise triggers sustained motion as well, with a characteristic ignition length that grows logarithmically as noise weakens at fixed observation time. Our work establishes a skin-effect route to bypass transitions, opening a new avenue for predicting and controlling nonlinear wave dynamics.

</details>


### [60] [Emergent Incompressibility in Polariton Condensates for Scalable XY Spin Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05782)
*Chengyong Yu,Youya Xu,Xuan Ye,Yan Zhou,Sanjib Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种原位双重增强机制，通过高阶非线性损耗实现相位-幅度解耦、并以弱厄米脉冲帮助系统逃离亚稳态，从而提升驱动-耗散极化激元网络模拟连续自旋哈密顿量的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动-耗散极化激元网络（driven-dissipative polariton networks）是模拟连续自旋哈密顿量的有力类比平台，但其实际可扩展性受限于幅度-相位耦合（amplitude-phase coupling）与高阻挫能量景观中的亚稳态俘获（metastable trapping）。

Method: 其一，基于密度重标度的重整化群分析（renormalization-group analysis）表明高阶非线性损耗可驱使凝聚体进入不可压缩区间，从本质上抑制幅度涨落、实现纯相位动力学；其二，引入弱厄米脉冲（weak Hermitian pulses），并通过多阶段协议反复逃离局部极小值。

Result: 在多达10,000个自旋的宏观XY网络中，相位-幅度解耦使能量降低逾50倍，同时标准差缩小为原来的六分之一，并恢复优化XY能量随系统尺寸的广延标度（extensive scaling）；弱厄米脉冲在多阶段协议下驱动系统到达更深的能量极小值，且优势随系统尺寸增长，在每个采样的宏观实现中均优于经典Monte Carlo-SQP基线。

Conclusion: 无需外部逐点位精细调节，该原位双重增强机制即可同时缓解幅度-相位耦合与亚稳态俘获两大瓶颈，为可扩展的极化激元模拟计算（polariton analog computing）提供了物理框架。

Abstract: Driven-dissipative polariton networks offer a powerful analog platform for simulating continuous-spin Hamiltonians. However, their practical scalability is fundamentally limited by amplitude-phase coupling and metastable trapping in highly frustrated energy landscapes. Here, we present an \textit{in situ} dual-enhancement mechanism to address these bottlenecks without requiring external site-specific fine-tuning. First, a renormalization-group analysis based on density rescaling shows that higher-order nonlinear loss drives the condensates toward an incompressible regime, intrinsically suppressing amplitude fluctuations and enforcing phase-only dynamics. For macroscopic XY networks (up to 10,000 spins), this phase-amplitude decoupling yields a more than 50-fold reduction in energy, while simultaneously shrinking the standard deviation by a factor of six. More generally, it restores the extensive scaling of the optimized XY energy with system size. Second, to navigate the rugged, nonconvex energy landscape, we introduce weak Hermitian pulses. When implemented through a multistage protocol, these pulses enable repeated escapes from local minima and drive the system to deeper energy minima. Benchmarking shows an increasing advantage with system size, with the polariton simulator outperforming the classical Monte Carlo-SQP baseline in every sampled macroscopic realization. Our work provides a physical framework for scalable polariton analog computing.

</details>


### [61] [Gate-reconfigurable Josephson microwave source limited by thermal noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05834)
*Jayeon Kim,Seong Jang,Seunghan Lee,Dongsung T. Park,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Gil-Ho Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于石墨烯弱连接与独立栅控石墨烯分流电阻构成的栅极可重构约瑟夫森微波辐射器，实现频率与发射功率的静电解耦调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约瑟夫森辐射器通过单一偏置电压同时耦合发射频率、输出功率与耗散，难以独立调控，本文旨在突破这一限制。

Method: 将石墨烯弱连接（graphene weak link）与单独栅控的石墨烯分流电阻（graphene shunt resistor）集成；偏置电压设定约瑟夫森频率，局部栅极调节约瑟夫森电流与总电阻，并以集总元件电路模型（lumped-element circuit model）与热平衡模型分析。

Result: 器件可分辨至四阶约瑟夫森谐波（Josephson harmonics），辐射功率随源电流与阻抗的变化符合集总元件电路模型；发射线宽可由电子温度下的约翰逊-奈奎斯特噪声（Johnson-Nyquist noise）定量描述，并受维德曼-弗兰兹扩散（Wiedemann-Franz diffusion）与电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）主导的电阻相关加热影响。

Conclusion: 该器件在选定频率下实现发射功率的静电控制，且线宽可作为偏置状态下的片上温度计（on-chip thermometer）。

Abstract: Josephson junctions are compact on-chip sources of coherent microwave and terahertz radiation, but conventional radiators couple the emission frequency, output power and dissipation through a single bias voltage. Here we demonstrate a gate-reconfigurable Josephson microwave radiator based on a graphene weak link integrated with a separately gated graphene shunt resistor. The bias voltage sets the Josephson frequency, while the local gates tune the Josephson current and the total device resistance, enabling electrostatic control of emitted power at a chosen frequency. The device resolves Josephson harmonics up to fourth order, and its radiation power scales with source current and impedance as expected from a lumped-element circuit model. We further show that the emission linewidth is quantitatively described by Johnson-Nyquist noise at the electron temperature. The linewidth therefore provides an on-chip thermometer under bias, with the resistance-dependent heating captured by a minimal heat-balance model based on Wiedemann-Franz diffusion and electron-phonon coupling.

</details>


### [62] [Floquet-Volkov spin polarization in spin-degenerate Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05856)
*Jiyang Fan,Dinghui Wang,Tongshuai Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作表明，在无自旋轨道耦合（SOC）的共线反铁磁狄拉克模型中，Floquet态与Volkov修饰出射光电子之间的干涉（Floquet-Volkov interference）可产生自旋极化光电子发射，即使Floquet准能保持自旋简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动可通过光子缀饰Floquet-Bloch态实现电子结构的超快调控，但实测光电子谱不仅受Floquet态影响，还受其与Volkov修饰出射光电子的干涉影响，该干涉能否产生自旋极化光电流此前缺乏明确机理刻画。

Method: 在共线反铁磁狄拉克模型（collinear antiferromagnetic Dirac model）中采用面内线偏振泵浦以保持$\mathcal{PT}$对称；解析识别Floquet-Volkov干涉机制，并用周期驱动与有限泵浦-探测脉冲下的非平衡格林函数（nonequilibrium Green-function）计算进行验证。

Result: 近共振驱动下光子缀饰导带与价带杂化，产生自旋相关的相对相位；经由到达同一Volkov修饰末态的多条路径问的干涉，该相位差转化为不相等的自旋分辨光电子强度。旋转探测偏振可在不改变Floquet能带的情况下抑制或反转自旋极化。

Conclusion: Floquet-Volkov干涉是驱动反铁磁体中实现自旋选择性光电子发射的一条途径，无需Floquet自旋劈裂或自旋轨道耦合。

Abstract: Periodic driving enables ultrafast control of electronic structures in solids through the formation of photon-dressed Floquet-Bloch states. However, the measured photoemission spectra are influenced not only by these states but also by their interference with Volkov-dressed outgoing photoelectrons. Here, we demonstrate that Floquet-Volkov interference can generate spin-polarized photoemission in a collinear antiferromagnetic Dirac model without spin-orbit coupling (SOC). An in-plane linearly polarized pump preserves $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry, leaving the Floquet quasienergies spin degenerate. Under near-resonant driving, photon-dressed conduction and valence bands hybridize, producing spin-dependent relative phases that are converted into unequal spin-resolved photoemission intensities through interference between pathways reaching the same Volkov-dressed final state. We analytically identify this interference mechanism and verify it using nonequilibrium Green-function calculations under periodic driving and finite pump-probe pulses. We further demonstrate that rotating the probe polarization can suppress or reverse the spin polarization without modifying the Floquet bands. Our findings establish Floquet-Volkov interference as a route to spin-selective photoemission in driven antiferromagnets without Floquet spin splitting or SOC.

</details>


### [63] [The quantized Hall effect knows which way the current flows: mirror covariance and rectification in a laterally asymmetric Hall bar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06004)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 量子霍尔（quantum Hall）平台值具有普适性，但平台边界并非如此。在一条硬边、一条软边的门控霍尔条中，电流反向会使平台边缘移动，交换两侧边缘则移动方向相反；其根源是垂直磁场下仍保留的面内 $C_2$ 旋转对称性，给出协变关系 $R_\alpha[B,V_{\mathrm{conf}}(x),I]=R_\alpha[B,V_{\mathrm{conf}}(-x),-I]$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔效应（quantized Hall effect）通常被视为无视样品形状、边缘与电流方向的普适性典范，但这一“无关性”仅在平台数值层面成立。论文旨在揭示平台边界对电流方向与边缘构型的依赖，并给出支配该现象的基本对称性。

Method: 采用自洽屏蔽模型（self-consistent screening model）分析硬边与软边组合的门控霍尔条，通过对称性论证导出协变关系（covariance relation），并进行自洽数值计算与预言性测量方案设计（场反向测试、压缩一条不可压缩条带）。

Result: 自洽计算显示，电流反向时软边条带由 69 nm 展宽至 106 nm，霍尔电阻（Hall resistance）在有限磁场窗口内由 0.940 变为 $0.999994\,h/2e^2$。该协变关系在深度非线性区严格成立，不依赖线性响应或微观可逆性，并预言一种在栅压交换下为奇、随磁场反向的整流效应。

Conclusion: 该效应被界定为可栅极调控的矢量积型非互易性（vector-product nonreciprocity），而非磁手性各向异性（magnetochiral anisotropy）。基于场反向的检验不扰动冻结的侧向无序，可在单一样品上实施但尚未开展；压缩一条不可压缩条带的进一步测量可区分电流的共向图像与手性图像。

Abstract: The quantized Hall effect is the textbook emblem of universality: a resistance fixed by $h/e^2$, apparently indifferent to the shape of the sample, to its edges and to the direction of the current. That indifference is only half true. The plateau value is universal; the plateau is not. In a gate-defined Hall bar with one hard and one soft edge, reversing the current moves the plateau edge, and exchanging the edges reverses the move. We identify the symmetry that forces this. Because a perpendicular field breaks time reversal, the relevant operation is the in-plane $C_2$ rotation; within the self-consistent screening model it gives the covariance relation $R_α[B,V_{\mathrm{conf}}(x),I]=R_α[B,V_{\mathrm{conf}}(-x),-I]$, exact deep in the nonlinear regime and independent of linear response or microscopic reversibility. It predicts a rectification that is odd under exchange of the gate voltages and reverses with the magnetic field. The field-reversal test leaves the frozen lateral disorder untouched, so it holds for a single sample; it has not yet been performed. A further measurement, collapsing one incompressible strip, discriminates between co-directed and chiral pictures of the current. Self-consistent calculations show the soft-edge strip widening from 69 to 106\,nm on current reversal, moving the Hall resistance from 0.940 to $0.999994\,h/2e^2$ within a bounded window in magnetic field. The effect is a gate-tunable vector-product nonreciprocity, not magnetochiral anisotropy.

</details>


### [64] [Spin Simulators based on Exciton-Polariton Waveguide Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06087)
*Kevin Dini,Simone Zanotti,Timothy C. H. Liew*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于可重构波导平台（reconfigurable waveguide platform）的极化激元XY模拟器（polaritonic XY simulator），同时解决了幅度均匀性（amplitude homogeneity）与任意对称耦合矩阵（arbitrary symmetric coupling matrices）编码两大限制，为可编程极化激元自旋模拟器提供可行架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NP难（NP-hard）优化问题可映射为相互作用自旋系统（interacting spin systems），其基态（ground state）编码最优解。基于增益-耗散动力学（gain-dissipative dynamics）的极化激元方案具备内禀并行性（parallelism）与超快操作优势，但受制于幅度均匀性难以维持、以及多数架构仅支持最近邻相互作用（nearest-neighbour interactions）两大根本限制。

Method: 采用可重构波导平台构建极化激元XY模拟器：通过平台设计保证幅度均匀性，同时支持任意对称耦合矩阵的实现，从而可直接编码多种XY哈密顿量（XY Hamiltonians）。

Result: 所提方法在保证幅度均匀性的同时实现了任意对称耦合矩阵，使多种XY哈密顿量可被直接编码，克服了既有方案的两项限制。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了可编程极化激元自旋模拟器（programmable polaritonic spin simulators）的实用架构，并为可扩展的模拟优化光学硬件（scalable optical hardware for analog optimisation）奠定基础。

Abstract: Many NP-hard optimization problems can be mapped onto interacting spin systems, whose ground state encodes the optimal solution. Polaritonic implementations based on gain-dissipative dynamics have recently emerged as promising analog computing platforms owing to their intrinsic parallelism and ultrafast operation. However, existing approaches are hindered by two fundamental limitations: maintaining the homogeneous amplitudes required for faithful problem encoding remains challenging in realistic physical systems, and most architectures are restricted to nearest-neighbour interactions, severely limiting the variety of optimization problems that can be efficiently addressed. We introduce a feasible polaritonic XY simulator based on a reconfigurable waveguide platform that overcomes both limitations. Our approach guarantees amplitude homogeneity while enabling the implementa- tion of arbitrary symmetric coupling matrices, thereby allowing the direct encoding of a wide variety of XY Hamiltonians. These results establish a practical architecture for programmable polaritonic spin simulators and pave the way towards scalable optical hardware for analog optimisation.

</details>


### [65] [Electrostatics of Chern junctions in a magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06134)
*Robin Durand,Pascal Simon,Ion Garate*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了垂直磁场下二维（2D）陈半导体（Chern semiconductor）横向结的静电性质，指出陈能带的光谱不对称性（spectral asymmetry）充当由陈数（Chern number）决定的有效掺杂密度，从而在结区产生拓扑起源的内建电势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静电控制是传统材料半导体结工作的核心，而二维材料器件中电子拓扑（electronic topology）与静电学的相互作用尚待阐明，本文旨在探究陈半导体结的静电特性。

Method: 将陈半导体光谱不对称性视为磁场诱导的有效掺杂密度（effective dopant density），以其产生的拓扑内建电势（built-in potential）为边界条件，求解二维泊松方程（2D Poisson equation），得到电势与电荷密度分布及电容。

Result: 在电荷中性（charge neutrality）下，陈数不同的两个区域具有不同的化学势（chemical potential），由此在结两侧形成拓扑起源的内建电势；求解泊松方程给出了电势、电荷密度剖面与电容（capacitance）的结果。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了由二维材料构成的器件中电子拓扑与静电学之间的相互影响，为陈半导体结的静电调控提供理论依据。

Abstract: Electrostatic control is central to the operation of semiconductor junctions made from conventional materials. Here, we theoretically investigate the electrostatic properties of lateral junctions between two-dimensional (2D) Chern semiconductors in a perpendicular magnetic field. The spectral asymmetry of a Chern semiconductor acts as a magnetic-field-induced effective dopant density that is dependent on the Chern number. As a consequence, two regions with different Chern numbers acquire different chemical potentials at charge neutrality, leading to a built-in potential of topological origin across the junction. Using this built-in potential as a boundary condition, we solve the 2D Poisson equation to obtain the electric potential and charge density profiles, as well as the capacitance, of Chern semiconductor junctions. Our results inform the interplay between electronic topology and electrostatics in devices made from 2D materials.

</details>


### [66] [Spin swapping mediated by spin-orbital textures in centrosymmetric multiorbital materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06163)
*Chengyuan Cai,Hanchen Wang,Rui-Chun Xiao,Tao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在无自旋能带劈裂的中心对称多轨道材料中，揭示了一条由轨道自由度驱动、无需自旋轨道散射或反演对称破缺的自旋交换（spin swapping）新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋交换机制被归因于自旋轨道散射（spin-orbit scattering）或自旋能带劈裂（spin-band splitting），这限制了可用材料范围与调控自由度。

Method: 采用自旋泵浦（spin pumping）将纵向自旋流由动态近邻铁磁体注入中心对称多轨道材料界面；自旋轨道纠缠的轨道织构（spin-orbit-entangled orbital textures）将注入自旋导向为纵向与横向轨道流，再转换回自旋部门（spin sector）。

Result: 转换回自旋部门后产生传播方向与极化方向相对初级自旋流发生交换的横向自旋流，Ge 与 Si 中的交换效率达 50%；该效应由受晶体对称性约束的四阶张量（fourth-rank tensor）描述。

Conclusion: 自旋-轨道织构（spin-orbital textures）可作为自旋交换的隐藏中间通道，且不依赖自旋轨道散射或反演对称破缺，拓展了材料选择与功能设计空间。

Abstract: Spin swapping converts a primary spin current into a secondary one by interchanging its flow and spin-polarization directions, attributed to spin-orbit scattering or spin-band splitting. Here we uncover a fundamentally distinct route to spin swapping arising from the orbital degree of freedom in the spin pumping to centrosymmetric multiorbital materials \textit{without spin-band splitting}. When the dynamical proximity ferromagnet injects a longitudinal spin current flowing normal to the interface, spin-orbit-entangled orbital textures redirect the injected spins into longitudinal and transverse orbital currents. Their subsequent conversion back to the spin sector generates transverse spin currents with propagation and polarization directions swapped relative to the primary spin current. The swapping efficiency achieves $50\%$ for Ge and Si. Governed by a fourth-rank tensor constrained by crystalline symmetry and without requiring spin-orbit scattering or inversion symmetry breaking, our finding reveals spin-orbital textures as hidden intermediate channels of spin swapping with unexplored material choices and functionalities.

</details>


### [67] [Hall-Density Collapse as Direct Evidence for Percolative Conduction Onset in Monolayer MoS$_2$ Transistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06815)
*R. V. Upadya,M. Introna,X. Wu,C. J. L. de la Rosa,K. Banerjee,D. Lin,K. De Greve*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究以温度依赖的输运与霍尔测量，在非晶HfO$_2$介电层修饰的MoS$_2$单层FET中，将二维电子系统的表观金属—绝缘体转变（metal-insulator transition）归因于经典渗流（classical percolation）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维电子系统中表观金属—绝缘体转变的物理起源长期存在争议，需要明确其是否源于无序驱动的渗流效应而非本征能带效应。

Method: 采用温度依赖的输运（transport）与霍尔（Hall）测量，对工业相关（industry-relevant）的、以非晶HfO$_2$为介电层的MoS$_2$单层场效应晶体管（FET）进行研究，从绝对栅压（gate voltage）依赖关系与激活能（activation energy）行为两方面提取判据。

Result: 观察到两个互补的渗流特征：霍尔密度（Hall-density）随绝对栅压塌缩且与温度无关；在特征载流子密度$n_{\mathrm{c}}$（即渗流阈值，percolation threshold）处激活能趋于消失。所提取的激活势垒显著高于室温热能量，表明有序化起始在远离低温输运区间仍具相关性。

Conclusion: 介电界面无序（dielectric-interface disorder）而非单纯沟道质量，是决定器件开启（turn-on）行为的关键参数；无序驱动导通的起始机制可延伸至室温以上温区。

Abstract: The origin of the apparent metal-insulator transition in two-dimensional electron systems remains debated. Using temperature-dependent transport and Hall measurements on industry-relevant MoS$_2$ monolayer FETs with amorphous HfO$_2$ dielectrics, we establish classical percolation as the underlying mechanism. We report two complementary signatures of disorder-driven conduction onset: a temperature-independent Hall-density collapse as a function of absolute gate voltage and a vanishing activation energy at a characteristic carrier density $n_{\mathrm{c}}$, identified as the percolation threshold. These findings identify dielectric-interface disorder, rather than channel quality alone, as the key parameter governing device turn-on. The extracted activation barriers reach values substantially exceeding room-temperature thermal energies, indicating that disorder-driven conduction onset remains relevant well beyond cryogenic transport.

</details>
