### [68] [Exact Mirror-Parity Selection Rule for Electron-Phonon Coupling in Two-Dimensional Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05710)
*Nicolás F. Barrera,Javiera Cabezas-Escares,Francisco Munoz,Carlos Cardenas,Julia Contreras-García*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文针对具有水平镜面（horizontal mirror plane, σ_h）的二维晶体，推导出电子-声子（electron-phonon, e-ph）散射的对称性选择定则：只有当电子态镜面宇称与声子模宇称之积为+1时，e-ph 矩阵元才非零，从而将布里渊区内的散射通道划分为对称允许与对称禁戒两类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子相互作用是常规超导（conventional superconductivity）以及固体输运和诸多多体现象的核心机制，但缺乏针对水平镜面对称性对 e-ph 散射所施加约束的系统性选择定则，该工作旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从声子诱导形变势（deformation potential）在 σ_h 下的协变性出发，结合群论矩阵元选择定则的 C_s 情形进行推导，并以第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）在二维超导体 Sc_2BCH_4 与体相 MgB_2 中进行验证与拓展。

Result: 在 Sc_2BCH_4 中镜面宇称在整个布里渊区均有明确定义，对称禁戒的矩阵元在数值精度内严格为零；镜面偶（mirror-even）声子耦合同宇称电子态（偶-偶或奇-奇），镜面奇（mirror-odd）声子仅耦合相反宇称态，带内与带间过程均如此。在体相 MgB_2 中该定则仅在镜面不变平面（mirror-invariant planes）上严格成立，偏离这些平面会激活原本禁戒的散射通道。

Conclusion: 水平镜面对称性可作为一种精确且实用的 e-ph 散射过程过滤器（filter），为理解与调控二维及层状材料的电子-声子耦合提供对称性判据。

Abstract: The electron--phonon (e-ph) interaction underlies conventional superconductivity and plays a central role in transport and other many-body phenomena in solids. Here, we derive a symmetry selection rule for e-ph scattering in two-dimensional crystals possessing a horizontal mirror plane, $σ_h$. For electronic states of mirror parity $s_n(\mathbf{k})$ and $s_m(\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{q})$, and a phonon mode of parity $χ_ν(\mathbf{q})$, we show that the e-ph matrix element $g_{mn,ν}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{q})$ vanishes identically unless $s_n(\mathbf{k})\,χ_ν(\mathbf{q})\, s_m(\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{q})=+1$. The rule follows from the covariance of the phonon-induced deformation potential under $σ_h$, constitutes the $C_s$ case of the general group-theoretical selection rule for matrix elements, and partitions the e-ph interaction throughout the Brillouin zone into symmetry-allowed and symmetry-forbidden scattering channels. Consequently, mirror-even phonons couple electronic states of the same mirror parity, either even--even or odd--odd, whereas mirror-odd phonons couple only states of opposite mirror parity, for both intra- and interband processes. We illustrate the selection rule through first-principles calculations for the two-dimensional superconductor $\mathrm{Sc_2BCH_4}$, where mirror parity is well defined throughout the entire Brillouin zone and symmetry-forbidden matrix elements vanish up to numerical precision. We then extend the analysis to bulk $\mathrm{MgB_2}$, where the rule applies exactly only on mirror-invariant planes of the three-dimensional Brillouin zone. Moving away from these planes removes the exact mirror constraint and activates scattering channels that are symmetry forbidden when the mirror eigenvalues are well defined. Our results establish horizontal mirror symmetry as an exact and practical filter of e-ph scattering processes.

</details>


### [69] [Predictions of superconducting properties from chemical bonding interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05784)
*Francesco Belli,Trinidad Novoa,Ion Errea,Eva Zurek*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该展望（perspective）文章系统梳理并整合了基于化学键（chemical bonding）描述符预测超导临界温度（superconducting critical temperature, Tc）的方法理论，重点讨论电子局域函数（electron localization function, ELF）衍生的网络值（networking value）描述符，并提出将Tc预测推广至常规超导体（conventional superconductors）家族的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理（first-principles）与人工智能（artificial intelligence）方法使材料空间（materials space）的探索规模与化学复杂度不断提升，亟需对超导临界温度Tc进行快速估算；而现有基于化学键的描述符方法主要局限于氢化物超导体（hydride superconductors）。

Method: 综述并整合基于化学键量的描述符方法理论，以电子局域函数（ELF）的拓扑性质为基础，讨论在实空间（real-space）视角下估算Tc的可行性，核心描述符为网络值（networking value）。

Result: ELF拓扑性质与Tc之间存在相关性，网络值可作为预测超导性的有效描述符；在实空间图像下可实现Tc的估算。

Conclusion: 提出一个框架，可基于易于计算的中间物理量（intermediate physical quantities），对广泛的常规超导体家族进行Tc预测，从而突破氢化物体系的限制。

Abstract: The need for rapid estimates of superconducting critical temperatures, Tc s, is becoming increasingly urgent as first-principles and artificial intelligence based methods enable the exploration of ever-larger and more chemically complex materials spaces. In this context, chemical bonding based quantities have emerged as promising descriptors for predicting superconductivity. To date, these approaches have mainly been developed for hydride superconductors, notably through the networking value, a descriptor based upon the electron localization function, whose topological properties have been found to correlate with Tc . In this perspective, we review and consolidate the method's theory, and we discuss how the estimate of the Tc is achievable within a real-space view. We conclude by proposing a framework that enables Tc prediction for the broad family of conventional superconductors from easily computable intermediate physical quantities.

</details>


### [70] [Variational Monte Carlo Study of Superconductivity in a Correlated System with Broken Inversion Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05964)
*Tsuyoshi Imazu,Katsunori Kubo*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 采用变分蒙特卡洛方法研究蜂窝晶格单带Hubbard模型，发现自旋轨道耦合可改变配对态竞争结果：λ/t=0时f波最稳定，λ/t=0.1时手性p波胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 破缺反演对称（inversion symmetry）体系中的非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）受到关注，因反对称自旋轨道耦合（antisymmetric spin-orbit coupling, ASOC）可显著影响超导态的自旋结构与稳定性；本文旨在探究强关联下ASOC对配对态竞争的作用。

Method: 在蜂窝网络（honeycomb network）对称性的关联体系中，以变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）方法研究含在位库仑相互作用U与ASOC耦合强度λ的单带Hubbard模型；试探波函数由粒子数守恒与Gutzwiller投影的BCS态（Gutzwiller-projected BCS states）构造，对每个能隙幅度（gap amplitude）优化变分参数μ与Gutzwiller参数g，并比较多种配对对称性态的能量稳定性。

Result: 在U/t=10时，λ/t=0条件下f波（f-wave）态变分能量最低；而λ/t=0.1时手性p波（chiral p-wave）态成为能量上最有利的配对态。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在强电子关联存在时，ASOC强烈影响超导配对态之间的竞争关系。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity in systems with broken inversion symmetry has attracted considerable interest because antisymmetric spin-orbit coupling (ASOC) can strongly affect the spin structure and stability of superconducting states. In this study, we investigate superconductivity in a correlated system with the symmetry of a honeycomb network using the variational Monte Carlo method. We consider a single-band Hubbard model including an on-site Coulomb interaction $U$ and ASOC with coupling strength $λ$, and compare several pairing states with different pairing symmetries. The trial wave functions are constructed from particle-number- and Gutzwiller-projected BCS states. To examine the energetic stability of each pairing state, we calculate the variational energy as a function of the gap amplitude, while optimizing the variational parameter $μ$ and the Gutzwiller parameter $g$ for each gap amplitude. At $U/t = 10$, the $f$-wave state has the lowest variational energy for $λ/t = 0$, whereas the chiral $p$-wave state becomes the most energetically favorable for $λ/t = 0.1$. These results indicate that ASOC strongly affects the competition among superconducting pairing states in the presence of strong electron correlations.

</details>

### [71] [The super Weil-Petersson asymptotic expansion and the coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06621)
*Xuanyu Huang,Kefeng Liu,Hao Xu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文确定了超 Weil-Petersson 体积（super Weil-Petersson volumes）在大亏格渐近展开中的普适常数，结果为 \(C=\pi^{-9/2}\)，并证明了 Griguolo-Papalini-Russo-Seminara 猜想在固定边界长度与固定降阶次数下的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是确定超 Weil-Petersson 体积大亏格渐近展开中的普适常数（universal constant），并验证相关的 Griguolo-Papalini-Russo-Seminara 猜想。

Method: 采用针对移位 theta 相交数（shifted theta intersection numbers）的一致输运策略（uniform-transport strategy），并借助 Guo-Norbury-Yang-Zagier 关于归一化 BGW 数（normalized BGW numbers）的大亏格一致定理确定较简端点处的振幅；另用两种精确递推方法计算前四个逆亏格系数。

Result: 得到普适常数 \(C=\pi^{-9/2}\)；边界公式在长度之和为 \(o(\sqrt g)\) 时一致，并在 \(\sqrt g\) 尺度上存在障碍（obstruction）；给出前四个逆亏格系数及一般多项式结构结果；并证明对每个固定亏格，超 Weil-Petersson 体积除以 \(n!\) 是标记点数 \(n\) 的多项式。

Conclusion: 超 Weil-Petersson 体积的大亏格渐近行为具有可确定的普适常数与一致边界公式，但一致性在 \(\sqrt g\) 尺度失效；固定亏格下体积除以 \(n!\) 关于标记点数呈多项式依赖。

Abstract: We determine the universal constant in the large-genus asymptotic expansion of super Weil-Petersson volumes. The argument adopts the uniform-transport strategy for shifted theta intersection numbers. The uniform large-genus theorem for normalized BGW numbers of Guo-Norbury-Yang-Zagier determines the amplitude at a simpler endpoint. The result is $C=π^{-9/2}$. We give a proof of the Griguolo-Papalini-Russo-Seminara conjecture for fixed boundary lengths and fixed descendant degrees. The boundary formula is uniform when the sum of the lengths is $o(\sqrt g)$, and we exhibit an obstruction at the $\sqrt g$ scale. We also compute the first four inverse-genus coefficients by two exact recursive methods and derive general polynomial structure results. Finally, we show that, for each fixed genus, the super Weil-Petersson volume divided by $n!$ is a polynomial in the number $n$ of marked points.

</details>


### [72] [Heavy-Light Wall Crossings and Motivic Asymptotics of Moduli Spaces of Curves. Part I: Stabilization Graphs and Euler Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06862)
*Giordano Cotti*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文显式计算 Hassett 空间（Hassett spaces）在 minimal all-light chamber 与 Deligne-Mumford 紧化下的普通拓扑欧拉示性数（topological Euler characteristics），并将其推广为标记数趋于无穷时的完整渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是给出 Hassett 空间底层粗模空间（coarse moduli spaces）欧拉示性数的显式计算框架，并回答 S. Kannan、S. Serpente、C. H. Yun 提出的关于 all-light 与普通欧拉示性数渐近比值的问题。

Method: 对 g≥2，通过稳定化图（stabilization graphs）及其装饰（decorations）作分解，并利用 Burnside-Lefschetz 公式计入图对称性（graph symmetries）与不动点轨迹（fixed loci）的贡献；all-light 到普通欧拉示性数的转换由直接墙穿越论证（wall-crossing argument）导出的通用变量替换 Φ(z)=(2-z)e^z-2 控制。

Result: g=2 情形显式验证方法，并在 Deligne-Mumford 情形复现 Bini–Gaiffi–Polito 的生成函数（含 Bini–Harter 修正）；g=3 在附录中完成。结合 g=1 基于碰撞划分与有理项链的初等计算，得到标记数趋于无穷的完整渐近展开；对任意固定 g≥2，主导项由有理三价图（rational trivalent graphs）给出，渐近尺度为 n!(e-2)^{-n}n^{(3g-5)/2}，其主导常数即图的对称权重，并给出相应生成函数、递推式与大亏格行为。

Conclusion: 该框架统一处理各亏格的显式计算与渐近分析，确定了 all-light 与普通欧拉示性数的渐近比值，从而回答了 Kannan–Serpente–Yun 的问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an explicit calculation of ordinary topological Euler characteristics of the coarse moduli spaces underlying Hassett spaces in the minimal all-light chamber and the Deligne-Mumford compactification. For genus $g\ge2$, we organize the calculation through a decomposition indexed by stabilization graphs and their decorations. The Burnside-Lefschetz formula allows us to compute the resulting contributions, taking into account graph symmetries and their fixed loci.
  The calculation in genus two illustrates the method explicitly and, in the Deligne-Mumford case, recovers the generating function of G. Bini, G. Gaiffi, and M. Polito (arXiv:math/9806048), with the corrections of G. Bini and J. Harer (arXiv:math/0506083), providing a check on the construction. We also carry out the calculation in genus three in an appendix. Together with an elementary genus-one calculation based on collision partitions and rational necklaces, these formulas yield complete asymptotic expansions as the number of labeled markings tends to infinity. The passage from all-light to ordinary Euler characteristics is governed by the universal change of variables $Φ(z)=(2-z)e^z-2$, which we derive by a direct wall-crossing argument.
  For arbitrary fixed genus $g\ge2$, the same framework identifies the leading contribution of rational trivalent graphs and gives the ordinary asymptotic scale $n!(e-2)^{-n}n^{(3g-5)/2}$. The leading constants are the symmetry weights of these graphs; we derive their generating function, recurrence, and large-genus behavior. We also determine the asymptotic ratio of all-light to ordinary Euler characteristics, answering a question of S. Kannan, S. Serpente, and C. H. Yun (arXiv:2207.02800).

</details>

### [73] [How much data does a memory kernel need? Measurement requirements for fractional models of battery and corrosion degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05426)
*Conrard Giresse Tetsassi Feugmo*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 该研究给出拟合记忆核（memory kernel）所需数据量的实用判据：分布式阶（distributed-order）记忆需要约3.1个数量级的观测数据才能以90%精度分辨，而公开电池数据集的观测窗口仅1.23–2.24个数量级。基于弱形式（weak formulation）的估计器可从单一老化曲线中以5%噪声内恢复有效阶alpha，并可验证de Levie多孔电极模型的高频阶0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池容量衰减（capacity fade）与腐蚀膜生长（corrosion-film growth）具有历史依赖性，常用广义分数阶微积分（general fractional calculus, GFC）中任意Sonine核描述记忆。然而，在何种数据量下记忆核拟合才具有意义，此前缺乏明确的定量判据，且标准电池退化测试（battery-degradation tests）的数据窗口可能不足。

Method: 采用弱形式（weak formulation）估计器，在无分数阶算子的两个机理模型与de Levie多孔电极模型（porous-electrode model）上验证阶数恢复能力；通过不同数量的Arrhenius条件评估调温参数（alpha, lambda）的可分离性；并分析分布式阶记忆在不同观测跨度下的可分辨性及谱塌缩（spectral collapse）现象。

Result: 估计器准确恢复de Levie模型的高频阶为0.500；单一老化曲线可在5%噪声内恢复有效阶alpha；调温参数误差随Arrhenius条件从1个增至4个而由(0.25, 0.43)改善至(0.04, 0.07)。分布式阶记忆需3.1个数量级数据方能达到90%分辨，数据不足时两阶记忆会塌缩为单一中间阶且拟合优于真实谱；对饱和钝化（saturating passivation），通用核将R2由0.862提升至0.998。20个电池单元（来自两个公开数据集）的观测窗口仅1.23–2.24个数量级。

Conclusion: 3.1个数量级的结果构成一条实用设计规则（design rule）：它界定了拟合记忆核之前必须测到何种数据跨度，而不仅是说明当前数据无法拟合。因此现有公开电池数据的观测窗口普遍不足以分辨分布式阶记忆，实验设计需相应延长观测。

Abstract: How much data does a memory kernel need before a fit is meaningful? For a distributed-order model, the answer is about 3.1 decades of observation, more than standard battery-degradation tests provide. Battery capacity fade and corrosion-film growth depend on their history, so they are often modelled using general fractional calculus (GFC), where memory is described by an arbitrary Sonine kernel rather than a single power law. We tested this using two mechanistic models without fractional operators and de Levie's porous-electrode model, whose high-frequency order is exactly 0.5; our estimator recovered 0.500. A single aging curve can recover an effective order, alpha, to within 5% noise using a weak formulation, while separating the tempered parameters (alpha, lambda) requires several Arrhenius conditions, with errors improving from (0.25, 0.43) with one condition to (0.04, 0.07) with four. A distributed-order memory needs 3.1 decades of data to be resolved to 90%; below that, a two-order memory can collapse into one intermediate order that fits the data better than the true spectrum. For saturating passivation, however, a general kernel improves the fit substantially, raising R2 from 0.862 to 0.998. Across 20 cells from two public datasets, observation windows span only 1.23 to 2.24 decades, making the 3.1-decade result a practical design rule: it defines what must be measured before fitting a memory kernel, not only what cannot be fitted.

</details>

### [74] [Experimental evaluation of Kolmogorov's first similarity hypothesis for shear-dependent viscosity fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06834)
*Luciano Hergenreder,Serge Simoëns,Wernher Brevis*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本实验检验科尔莫戈罗夫第一相似性假说对剪切依赖黏性流体的适用性，发现以平均黏度和平均耗散率归一化后，二三阶结构函数在不同工况下塌缩并与经典牛顿流体结果吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科尔莫戈罗夫（Kolmogorov）第一相似性假说原针对牛顿流体，其在剪切变稀等剪切依赖黏性流体中的适用性尚待实验验证，近期数值研究提出了基于平均黏度与平均耗散率扩展该假说的设想。

Method: 在近均匀各向同性湍流（nearly homogeneous and isotropic turbulence）中，采用立体粒子图像测速（stereo particle image velocimetry）测量两种浓度的 Carbopol 水溶液，覆盖多种强迫条件，考察耗散区（dissipative range）统计量的自相似行为。

Result: 以平均黏度与平均耗散率归一化后，二阶与三阶结构函数（structure functions）在不同实验条件下塌缩，其函数形式与经典牛顿流体结果相容。

Conclusion: 实验结果总体支持 Rosti（2025）的数值预测，即通过采用基于平均黏度、平均耗散率和流动指数（flow index）的耗散尺度，科尔莫戈罗夫相似性假说可推广至剪切变稀流体（shear-thinning fluids）。

Abstract: We experimentally investigate the applicability of Kolmogorov's first similarity hypothesis to shear-dependent viscosity fluids. We assess whether dissipative range statistics exhibit a self-similar behavior in nearly homogeneous and isotropic turbulence, using stereo particle image velocimetry in aqueous Carbopol solutions across two concentrations and a range of forcing conditions. We observe that, when normalized by the mean viscosity and mean dissipation rate, second- and third-order structure functions collapse across the different experimental conditions and exhibit functional forms compatible with classical Newtonian results. These observations are in general agreement with the recent numerical results of Rosti [J. Fluid Mech. 1012, R5 (2025)], who proposed that Kolmogorov's similarity hypotheses can be extended to shear-thinning fluids by considering dissipation scales based on the mean viscosity, the mean dissipation rate, and the flow index.

</details>


### [75] [Odd Capillarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06882)
*Nadia Bihari Padhan,Axel Voigt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文提出“奇毛细作用（odd capillarity）”这一破坏宇称的界面力学响应，将对称、非变分、宇称奇的毛细应力引入 Cahn–Hilliard–Navier–Stokes（CHNS）方程，证明其可在无常规惯性能量级联的情况下维持湍流运动并抑制畴粗化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规毛细作用（capillarity）通过宇称偶的机械应力支配二元流体界面弛豫与流体动力学，但缺乏对破坏宇称的界面力学响应的描述，作者由此引入奇毛细作用以拓展界面力学机制。

Method: 在 Cahn–Hilliard–Navier–Stokes（CHNS）方程中加入对称、非变分、宇称奇的毛细应力项，并进行直接数值模拟（direct numerical simulation）研究其对相分离与流体运动的影响。

Result: 该应力在界面处产生横向于常规毛细力的作用力，并做成分自由能变化无法平衡的机械功；模拟显示奇毛细作用阻止畴粗化（domain coarsening），在能量注入主要由黏性耗散平衡的情况下维持湍流运动而无需常规惯性能量级联；在非对称混合物中诱发形变与破碎，中断液滴生长，形成具有组分超均匀性（hyperuniformity）且大尺度涡量涨落受抑制的动态停滞乳液。

Conclusion: 奇毛细作用被确立为一种独特的界面机制，可用于控制相分离并维持非平衡流体运动。

Abstract: Capillarity governs interfacial relaxation and hydrodynamic motion in binary fluids through parity-even mechanical stresses. Here, we introduce \emph{odd capillarity}, a parity-breaking interfacial mechanical response, by incorporating a symmetric, nonvariational, parity-odd capillary stress into the Cahn--Hilliard--Navier--Stokes (CHNS) equations. This stress generates forces along interfaces, transverse to ordinary capillary forces, and performs mechanical work that is not balanced by changes in the composition free energy. Direct numerical simulations reveal that odd capillarity arrests domain coarsening while sustaining turbulent fluid motion without a conventional inertial energy cascade, as energy injection is predominantly balanced by viscous dissipation. In asymmetric mixtures, odd capillarity driven deformation and breakup interrupt droplet growth, producing dynamically arrested emulsions with composition hyperuniformity and suppressed large-scale vorticity fluctuations. These findings establish odd capillarity as a distinct interfacial mechanism for controlling phase separation and sustaining nonequilibrium fluid motion.

</details>

### [76] [Neural Networks with Exciton-Polariton Waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05846)
*Huawen Xu,Ruiqi Bao,Lingyu Tian,Yusong Gan,Junfeng Gao,Michal Matuszewski,Qihua Xiong,Timothy Liew*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出并数值研究基于激子-极化激元波导网格的神经网络方案，利用极化激元相位作为随机变量实现受限玻尔兹曼机，并通过无监督Hebbian/反Hebbian学习实现联想记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索激子-极化激元（exciton-polariton）波导结构作为可编程神经网络硬件的可行性，以支持层间耦合可任意编程、并具备随机性的网络架构。

Method: 构建基于激子-极化激元波导网格（waveguide meshes）的神经网络：该网格提供任意可编程的层间耦合（programmable interlayer couplings），适配受限玻尔兹曼机（restricted Boltzmann machine）架构，其中极化激元相位（polariton phases）充当随机网络变量；训练采用无监督的Hebbian与反Hebbian更新规则。

Result: 数值结果表明该网络可被有效训练，并可实现联想记忆（associative memory）：网络能够从不完美的输入中恢复已学习的模式（learned patterns）。

Conclusion: 激子-极化激元波导网格可作为受限玻尔兹曼机型神经网络的物理实现平台，结合无监督局部学习规则实现联想记忆功能。

Abstract: We propose and numerically study a scheme for neural networks using exciton-polariton waveguide meshes. These enable arbitrary programmable interlayer couplings suited to a restricted Boltzmann machine architecture where polariton phases act as stochastic network variables. We show that such a network can be trained using unsupervised Hebbian and anti-Hebbian updates. This allows the implementation of an associative memory, where the network recovers learned patterns from imperfect inputs.

</details>


### [77] [Finite-width shift of the edge of chaos in deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06582)
*Arn Caesar*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文指出有限宽度网络的"混沌边缘（edge of chaos）"相对无限宽度结果发生 $1/N$ 量级的偏移，并据此提出无需搜索的初始化规则 Crinit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在混沌边缘初始化时训练效果最佳，但该临界线仅在无限宽极限下已知；有限宽度下其移动方向与幅度存在争议，计算与仿真结果相互矛盾。

Method: 将宽度为 $N$ 的网络中两输入间距离的对数视为随机游走（random walk），在 $1/N$ 阶计算其漂移与方差；并分析多网络平均与典型网络差异（稀有网络主导平均）。

Result: 混沌边缘向更大权重方差移动，$\\tanh$ 网络在偏置方差 $0.05$ 时约移动 $8/N$，经精确有限宽度递推仿真验证；网络平均的临界点因稀有增长网络而反向移动，解释了既有分歧；反向传播（backpropagation）遵循同一随机游走，梯度偏移不变。

Conclusion: 沿混沌边缘，输入相似性信息在压缩超过约 1 nat 前保持不变，由此确定线上位置；所提 Crinit 规则在 MNIST 与 Fashion-MNIST、宽度 $64$ 的深网络中显著优于标准规则，且训练步数更少。

Abstract: Deep neural networks train best when they are initialised at the edge of chaos, the critical line in the plane of weight and bias variances on which the distance between two inputs neither grows nor shrinks with depth. The line is known for infinitely wide networks; at finite width it moves, and how it moves has been disputed. We treat the logarithm of the distance between two inputs through a network of width $N$ as a random walk and compute its drift and variance to order $1/N$. The edge of chaos moves to a larger weight variance, by $Δσ_w^2=-a_1/(N\,\partial_{σ_w^2}\lnχ_1)$, about $8/N$ for $\tanh$ networks at a bias variance of $0.05$, confirmed by simulations of the exact finite-width recursion. The shift describes a typical network; the average over many networks is dominated by rare networks in which the distance grows, and its critical point moves the other way, which explains why calculations and simulations have disagreed. Backpropagation follows the same random walk, so the shift applies to gradients unchanged. Along the edge of chaos, the information about the similarity of two inputs is constant until the compression over the depth exceeds about one nat, which fixes where to sit on the line. The results give an initialisation rule, Crinit, that places a network on its finite-width edge of chaos without a search. On MNIST and Fashion-MNIST at width $64$ it is more accurate than the standard rules at their usual settings, by a small margin in shallow networks and a large one in deep networks, where most standard rules fail; it trains in fewer steps, and it matches or beats the standard rules with a tuned gain.

</details>


### [78] [Cell-type-specific modularity in E/I networks shapes dynamical regimes and computational capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06720)
*Emmanouil Giannakakis,Victor Buendía,Oleg Vinogradov,Anna Levina*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究基于动力平均场理论与模拟，揭示兴奋性与抑制性群体模块化的非对称调控可控制平衡E/I网络的动力学区域，并在混沌与双稳态边界附近提升时间序列预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑网络具有普遍的结构模块性，但兴奋性与抑制性群体的模块组织可能不对称，这种不对称如何影响网络动力学与计算仍不清楚。

Method: 采用动力平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）与执行复杂时间任务的网络模拟，独立调节兴奋性与抑制性模块化程度。

Result: 独立调节E/I模块化可在整体E/I平衡下产生主导谱离群值（spectral outlier），实现由拓扑控制的体态与低秩主导机制间转变，并分别通过不同分岔通向混沌与双稳态；在混沌时间序列预测任务中，性能在混沌起始与双稳态转变等动力学边界附近提升。

Conclusion: 有利配置表现为兴奋性高度模块化而抑制相对均质，与皮层连接（cortical connectivity）的观测相符，表明E/I模块化的非对称性是调控网络动力学与计算的关键拓扑因素。

Abstract: Structural modularity is a ubiquitous feature of brain networks that shapes neural dynamics and information processing. Yet excitatory and inhibitory populations can differ in their modular organization, and it remains unclear how this asymmetry affects network dynamics and computation. Here, using dynamical mean-field theory and simulations of networks performing complex temporal tasks, we show that independently tuning excitatory and inhibitory modularity controls the dynamical regimes of balanced E/I networks. In particular, such tuning can generate a dominant spectral outlier despite overall E/I balance, producing a topology-controlled transition between bulk- and low-rank-dominated regimes, with different dynamical bifurcations leading to chaos and bistability, respectively. In chaotic time-series prediction tasks, computational performance is enhanced near these dynamical boundaries, including both the onset of chaos and the transition to bistability. Favorable configurations exhibit strongly modular excitation and comparatively homogeneous inhibition, similar to what is observed in cortical connectivity.

</details>

### [79] [Resonantly interacting $2e/3$ anyons: a controlled microscopic theory of anyon superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05529)
*Qingchen Li,Pavel A. Nosov,Zhaoyu Han,Si-Yuan Chen,Taige Wang,Eslam Khalaf*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立了一种微观任意子超导理论，将轻掺杂Laughlin态中的电子相互作用与共振相互作用的稀薄任意子气体的普适物理联系起来。通过几何量子化Laughlin准空穴，导出电荷2e/3任意子的有效多体哈密顿量，并围绕双体束缚共振组织稀薄任意子问题，在一个共振点发现可用Aharonov-Casher哈密顿量精确求解，从而直接证明Cooper对ODLRO及确定配对对称性与涡旋晶格结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子超导的微观机制此前缺乏将电子层面相互作用与任意子气体普适物理相联系的框架，本文旨在填补该空白，并类比共振费米/玻色气体建立可控的稀薄任意子理论。

Method: 采用Laughlin准空穴的几何量子化（geometric quantization）导出稀薄2e/3电荷任意子的有效多体哈密顿量，包含谷结构、色散、统计与短程相互作用及任意子—电子可观测量的显式映射；围绕双体束缚共振（two-body binding resonance）及其相关与边缘微扰组织问题，将多体问题约化为少数可由少任意子数值计算确定的参数。

Result: 对三谷2e/3任意子气体，识别出一个长程理论由Aharonov-Casher任意子哈密顿量（其磁场为统计Chern-Simons场）支配且基态可精确求解的共振；用该基态波函数在电子坐标下直接证明Cooper对的离对角长程序（off-diagonal long-range order），确定配对对称性与涡旋晶格结构。不动点处的主要微扰为相关配对失谐与谷单态道中的边缘三体接触项，决定压缩率与声速。

Conclusion: 排斥性三体耦合为边缘无关，使不动点控制配对共振附近参数上宽广的稀薄区域，而吸引性三体相互作用则生成电荷2e三聚体。更广泛地，竞争任意子复合体之间的束缚阈值承载了仅靠最低能任意子种类无法描述的普适共振区，为任意子物质的可控理论提供了新场景。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic theory of anyon superconductivity that connects electronic interactions in a lightly doped Laughlin state to the universal physics of a resonantly interacting anyon gas. Using geometric quantization of Laughlin quasiholes, we derive the effective many-body Hamiltonian of dilute charge-$2e/3$ anyons, including their valley structure, dispersion, and statistical and short-range interactions, together with an explicit map between anyon and electron observables. This reduces the dilute many-body problem to a few parameters fixed by data computable from few anyon numerics. In analogy with resonant Fermi and Bose gases, we organize the dilute anyon problem around a two-body binding resonance and its relevant and marginal perturbations. For a three-valley gas of charge-$2e/3$ anyons, we identify a resonance whose long-distance theory is governed by an Aharonov-Casher anyon Hamiltonian whose magnetic field is the statistical Chern-Simons field and whose ground state is exactly solvable. Using the ground state wavefunction, we demonstrate Cooper-pair off-diagonal long-range order directly in electron coordinates and determine the pairing symmetry and vortex-lattice structure. The leading perturbations at the fixed point are a relevant pair detuning and a marginal three-body contact in the valley-singlet channel, which determine the compressibility and sound velocity. A repulsive three-body coupling is marginally irrelevant, allowing the fixed point to control a parametrically broad dilute regime near pair resonance, while attractive three-body interactions instead generate charge-$2e$ trimers. More broadly, our results show that binding thresholds between competing anyon composites host universal resonant regimes that are not captured by the lowest-energy anyon species alone, providing a new setting for controlled theories of anyon matter.

</details>


### [80] [Ni ordering in the van der Waals gap of Ni-intercalated 2H-NbS$_2$: from disorder through $2\times2$ and $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$ order to stripe order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05699)
*Naveen Singh Dhami,Ricardo Guillermo Torres Ramirez,Gaurav Pransu,Ivo Batistić,Pierre Fertey,Erik Elkaim,El-Eulmi Bendeif,Bruno Gudac,Nikolina Penić,Naveen Kumar Chogondahalli Muniraju,Neven Barišić,Petar Popčević,Victor Balédent*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用同步辐射x射线衍射（synchrotron x-ray diffraction）系统研究了Ni插层2H-NbS₂在0.18≤x≤0.57范围内的结构演化，建立了NiₓNbS₂的结构相图（structural phase diagram），并揭示了插层无序（intercalant disorder）对磁性质解释的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ni插层2H-NbS₂的磁性质解释需要明确插层离子（intercalant）的占位与有序化行为，但此前缺乏覆盖较宽Ni含量范围、结合粉末与单晶的系统结构研究，尤其是堆垛缺陷（stacking faults）与插层无序对物性理解的影响尚未厘清。

Method: 以能量色散x射线谱（energy-dispersive x-ray spectroscopy）测定Ni含量，对全系列样品进行同步辐射粉末衍射（powder diffraction），并对两个公度相（commensurate phases，x=1/4与x=1/3）开展单晶衍射（single-crystal diffraction），辅以密度泛函（density-functional）计算。

Result: NbS₂母体（host）结构保持；范德华间隙（van der Waals gap）中的Ni由无序排列演化为x=1/4的2×2有序与x=1/3的√3×√3有序（其间两 motif 共存），直至x=0.57的正交条纹型（orthorhombic stripe-type）有序。面内晶格参数（in-plane lattice parameter）随Ni含量增大，层间距（interlayer spacing）基本不变但在x=1/4处出现显著极小值，且被密度泛函计算再现。x=1/4相为单一Ni位点与三聚化Nb晶格（trimerized Nb lattice）、无堆垛缺陷；手性（chiral）x=1/3相则含名义空位上的Ni、反演孪晶（inversion twins）与Ni层堆垛缺陷。粉末与晶体对比表明研磨主要破坏Ni层的堆垛。

Conclusion: 研究确立了NiₓNbS₂的结构相图，并指出在解释其磁性质时必须考虑插层无序及堆垛缺陷等结构因素。

Abstract: We report a synchrotron x-ray diffraction study of Ni-intercalated 2H-NbS$_2$ over the range $0.18 \le x \le 0.57$, with Ni contents measured by energy-dispersive x-ray spectroscopy. Powder diffraction on the whole series shows that the NbS$_2$ host is preserved, while the Ni ions in the van der Waals gap evolve from a disordered arrangement to $2\times2$ order at $x=1/4$ and $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$ order at $x=1/3$, through a range where both motifs coexist, to an orthorhombic stripe-type order at $x=0.57$. The in-plane lattice parameter increases with Ni content, whereas the interlayer spacing remains nearly constant except for a pronounced minimum at $x=1/4$, which density-functional calculations reproduce for the ordered structure. Single-crystal diffraction on the two commensurate phases shows that the $x=1/4$ structure, with Ni on a single site and a trimerized Nb lattice, is free of stacking faults, whereas the chiral $x=1/3$ structure contains Ni on the nominally empty site, inversion twins and stacking faults of the Ni layers. The comparison between powders and crystals shows that grinding mainly degrades the stacking of the Ni layers. These results establish the structural phase diagram of Ni$_x$NbS$_2$ and the intercalant disorder to be taken into account in the interpretation of its magnetic properties.

</details>


### [81] [Interplay between Spin Stripes and Pairing Crossover in the Bilayer Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05840)
*Zixuan Jia,Xuelei Sui,Shijie Hu,Tianxing Ma,Haiqing Lin,Bing Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过有限温度行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）模拟最小双层 Hubbard 模型（bilayer Hubbard model），发现空穴掺杂下自旋条纹（spin stripes）自发形成，主导配对对称性由 d 波向 s± 波过渡，并与层间/层内反铁磁关联相对强度的反转同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍氧化物 La3Ni2O7 中 d 波与 s± 波配对孰为主导尚无定论，且其自旋序与磁激发同配对机制之间的关系不清晰，需要从最小模型角度厘清强层间耦合下的配对竞争。

Method: 对最小双层 Hubbard 模型开展有限温度行列式量子蒙特卡洛模拟，聚焦层内与层间反铁磁关联强度相当的强层间耦合区域，考察随空穴掺杂演化的配对对称性与自旋关联。

Result: 随空穴掺杂增加，自旋条纹自发出现，主导配对对称性由 d 波（d-wave）过渡到 s± 波（s±-wave）；该过渡追随自旋关联相对强度的变化，层间关联在量值上超过层内关联；同时动力学自旋结构因子（dynamical spin structure factor）显示磁激发发生随动量依赖的重组，与涨落自旋条纹一致。

Conclusion: 结果将自旋涨落（spin fluctuations）的结构识别为连接自旋条纹与配对竞争的关键环节，为理解 La3Ni2O7 提供了最小模型视角。

Abstract: Whether $d$-wave or $s_\pm$-wave pairing prevails in the bilayer nickelate \ce{La3Ni2O7} remains unresolved, and how its observed spin order and magnetic excitations relate to the pairing mechanism is unclear. Here, we use finite-temperature determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations of a minimal bilayer Hubbard model to investigate pairing competition at strong interlayer coupling, where intralayer and interlayer antiferromagnetic correlations have comparable strengths. We find that spin stripes develop spontaneously with increasing hole doping, accompanied by a crossover of the dominant pairing symmetry from $d$-wave to $s_\pm$-wave. The crossover tracks the changing relative strengths of the spin correlations, with the interlayer correlations overtaking their intralayer counterparts in magnitude. Concomitantly, the dynamical spin structure factor exhibits a momentum-dependent reorganization of the magnetic excitations, consistent with fluctuating spin stripes. Our results identify the structure of spin fluctuations as a key link between spin stripes and pairing competition, offering a minimal-model perspective for understanding \ce{La3Ni2O7}.

</details>


### [82] [Stability of energy gap of macroscopic quantum matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06528)
*Kangle Li,Liujun Zou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作系统研究了仅满足局域性（locality）与平移不变性（translation invariance）的宏观量子物质能隙稳定性，证明在任意空间维度均存在具有唯一基态却不稳定能隙的局域哈密顿量，并将一维情形的不稳定性归结为必须同时破坏平移不变性且需精细调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体物理与量子场论中常假设有能隙（gapped）系统在足够弱的局域微扰下仍保持有能隙，但该假设除少数特殊系统外缺乏普遍性依据；本文旨在厘清该假设在多宏观量子物质中的适用边界。

Method: 在仅施加局域性与平移不变性两个物理自然条件的前提下，构造性分析有能隙局域哈密顿量（local Hamiltonians）在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下对任意弱局域微扰的能隙稳定性，并区分自旋（spin）与费米子（fermionic）系统及不同空间维度进行证明。

Result: 证明在任意空间维度都存在具有唯一基态（unique ground state）且能隙不稳定的有能隙局域哈密顿量；在一维自旋与费米子系统中，此类不稳定哈密顿量必须既破坏平移不变性又经过精细调节（fine-tuned）；在任意大于一维的情形，存在精细调节的、平移不变的局域哈密顿量具有唯一基态与不稳定能隙。

Conclusion: 结果显著深化了对量子物相（quantum phases of matter）稳定性这一基本问题的理解，并讨论了其对任意子超导（anyon superconductivity）与高形式对称性（higher-form symmetries）等方面的启示。

Abstract: In quantum many-body physics and quantum field theory, it is often assumed that gapped systems remain gapped under sufficiently weak local perturbations. However, to what extent this assumption holds is elusive, except for some special systems. In this work, we examine the stability of energy gap of macroscopic quantum matter that is only subject to two physically natural conditions, locality and translation invariance. We first show that in any spatial dimension, there are gapped local Hamiltonians with a unique ground state and an unstable gap, i.e., they become gapless under certain arbitrarily weak local perturbation in the thermodynamic limit. We then prove that for both spin and fermionic systems in one dimension, such Hamiltonians with an unstable gap must not only break translation invariance but also be fine-tuned. We further show that in any dimension greater than one, there are fine-tuned local translation invariant Hamiltonians with a unique ground state and an unstable gap. These results greatly sharpen our understanding of the fundamental problem of the stability of quantum phases of matter. We also discuss implications of our results on, for example, anyon superconductivity and higher-form symmetries.

</details>


### [83] [Field evolution of the multi-$k$ magnetic states in NdB$_4$ revealed by neutron diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06526)
*D. Brunt,N. Qureshi,E. Chan,B. Ouladdiaf,G. Balakrishnan,D. D. Khalyavin,O. A. Petrenko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用单晶中子衍射研究Shastry-Sutherland晶格磁体NdB₄在c轴磁场下的磁态，发现1/5磁化平台伴随传播矢量的改变，并证明高场"场极化"相中面内磁分量仍然存在、极化并不完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NdB₄作为Shastry-Sutherland晶格磁体可稳定分数1/5磁化平台（magnetisation plateau），但其磁场诱导磁态的微观结构，尤其高场相是否真正达到完全场极化，尚需直接的衍射证据。

Method: 采用单晶中子衍射（single-crystal neutron diffraction），沿四方c轴施加磁场，追踪不同磁场区间磁反射与传播矢量（propagation vector）的变化。

Result: 在4.9 K以下低温相中，约12 kOe进入平台态时传播矢量由(1/5,0,-2/5)及其倍频(2/5,0,1/5)变为(0,0,1/5)与(0,0,2/5)；约33 kOe由平台向名义"场极化"（field-polarised）态转变时非整数传播矢量的磁反射消失，而k=0分量贯穿整个场范围持续存在。

Conclusion: 面内磁分量在高场相中依然存在，极化实际上并不完全；该场依赖行为支持将NdB₄描述为两个耦合磁不稳定性（magnetic instability）的叠加，即面内矩的全进全出（all-in-all-out）序与面外分量的自旋密度波（spin-density wave）。

Abstract: We investigate the magnetic states of the Shastry-Sutherland-lattice magnet NdB$_4$ using single-crystal neutron diffraction. We focus on magnetic fields applied along the tetragonal $c$ axis, which stabilise the fractional 1/5~magnetisation plateau. In the low-temperature phase below 4.9 K, the transition into the plateau state at approximately 12 kOe is marked by a change in the propagation vectors from $(1/5,0,-2/5)$ and its double $(2/5,0,1/5)$ to $(0,0,1/5)$ and $(0,0,2/5)$. At the transition from the plateau to a nominally `field-polarised' state near 33 kOe, all magnetic reflections associated with non-integer propagation vectors disappear, whereas reflections corresponding to the $\mathbf{k}=0$ component persist throughout the entire field range. We conclude that that the in-plane components survive even in the high-field phase and that the polarisation is, in fact, incomplete. The observed field dependence provides direct support for a description of NdB$_4$ in terms of two coupled magnetic instabilities: all-in-all-out order of the in-plane moments and a spin-density wave of the out-of-plane components.

</details>


### [84] [Hidden entropic order of emergent charges in a classical Ampère spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06795)
*H. Diego Rosales,Emma M. Bizot,Flavia A. Gómez Albarracín,Pierre Pujol*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究八氯格（octochlore lattice）上的Ising型安培模型（Ising Ampère model），发现虽其基态约束（反对称二阶场的高斯定律）预期产生自旋液体特征，但系统经连续相变自发进入一种纯熵起源的子晶格长程有序态，从而将预测的代数自旋液体转化为部分有序的短程自旋液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典库仑自旋液体（classical Coulomb spin liquids）在基态流形中具有无散度局域约束，产生偶极关联与结构因子中的斧点（pinch points），但禁止涌现电荷；近期提出的零环流约束（安培定律，Ampère's law）模型则允许电荷不受约束。已有研究尚缺乏对安培型模型具体格点与维数实现中基态性质及相变的系统探讨，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用约束矢量理论（constraint-vector theory）、精确界与枚举（exact bounds and enumeration），并结合蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）、Wang-Landau算法与并行回火（parallel-tempering）模拟，对八氯格上的Ising安培模型进行综合研究。

Result: 基态满足反对称二阶场的高斯定律（等价于三个耦合的二维高斯定律），本应产生斧点；然而最大电荷（六箭头全进或全出的顶点）以无序符号自发聚集于立方晶格的两个子晶格之一。该有序态使大尺度斧点奇异性消失，不产生磁布拉格峰（magnetic Bragg peaks），并与广延熵共存，系统经看似三维Ising普适类（3D Ising universality class）的连续相变进入该态。

Conclusion: 该子晶格长程有序态无磁序且对时间反演为偶，是安培流形中一种隐藏的熵序（hidden, entropic order）。因此，一个高斯定律预测为代数自旋液体（algebraic spin liquid）的体系，通过非磁性有序相变转化为部分有序的短程自旋液体（short-range spin liquid）。

Abstract: Classical spin liquids are characterized by an extensive number of ground-state configurations. Coulomb spin liquids have, in this ground-state manifold, a divergence-free local constraint, which gives dipolar correlations and pinch points in the structure factor. Recently, models with a complementary ground-state constraint, defined by a zero-circulation constraint (Ampère's law), have been proposed. While classical Coulomb spin liquids forbid emergent charges, their Ampère counterparts leave the charges unconstrained. Here we study an Ising Ampère model on the octochlore lattice. The ground states obey a Gauss law for an antisymmetric rank-two field, i.e., three coupled two-dimensional Gauss laws, which are expected to produce pinch points in the structure factor. Nevertheless, maximal charges, vertices with all six arrows in or out, spontaneously accumulate on one of the two sublattices of the cubic lattice, with disordered signs. This order has a purely entropic origin; it washes out the pinch-point singularities at large scales, produces no magnetic Bragg peaks, and coexists with an extensive entropy. The system enters into this sublattice long-range ordered state through a continuous transition in what seems to be the three-dimensional Ising universality class. Moreover, the state has no magnetic order; it is even under time reversal: it is a hidden, entropic order within the Ampère manifold. Thus, a system whose Gauss law would predict an algebraic spin liquid becomes, through a non-magnetic ordering transition, a partially ordered short-range spin liquid. Our study is based on a combination of the constraint-vector theory, exact bounds and enumeration, and Monte Carlo, Wang-Landau, and parallel-tempering simulations.

</details>

### [85] [The Search for the Top Quark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05453)
*Paul D. Grannis*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 1995年顶夸克（top quark）的发现补齐了标准模型（Standard Model）预期的夸克与轻子（quarks and leptons）谱系，但顶夸克并不符合其他同类夸克所呈现的规律，已成为超越现有范式的新物理（physics beyond the Standard Model）探索中的关键对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出，顶夸克的发现使标准模型及其多种扩展方案所预期的物质粒子谱系得以完备，然而顶夸克偏离了同类夸克的既有模式，这一反常特征使其成为当前新物理搜寻的核心关注点，本文即围绕这一背景展开。

Method: 摘要未涉及具体研究方法；文中仅说明该文为2025年11月CERN举办的第4届粒子物理学史国际研讨会（4th International Symposium on the History of Particle Physics）会议论文集所准备的稿件，属历史回顾性论述。

Result: 摘要未报告具体研究结果或数据。可确认的要点为：顶夸克于1995年被发现，完成了标准模型预期的物质粒子谱系，但其性质不符合姐妹夸克（sister quarks）所建立的规律。

Conclusion: 顶夸克因其独特性状已成为检验并超越主流范式（prevailing paradigm）的关键探针，在当代粒子物理新物理搜寻中具有不可替代的地位。

Abstract: The discovery of the top quark in 1995 completed the roster of the matter particles, quarks and leptons, expected in the Standard Model and in many of its suggested extensions. However, the top quark does not conform to the pattern set by its sister quarks and has come to be a critical component of the ongoing searches for physics beyond the prevailing paradigm. (Prepared for the Proceedings of the 4th International Symposium on the History of Particle Physics, CERN, Nov. 2025.)

</details>

### [86] [Shapiro spikes in driven quantum fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05477)
*Vijay Pal Singh,Ludwig Mathey,Luigi Amico*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 通过模拟电势偏置的原子约瑟夫森结（atomic Josephson junction），在中性量子流体中实现沙皮罗尖峰（Shapiro spikes），并揭示其由量子化涡旋（quantized vortex）成核与相位滑移（phase slips）驱动的微观机制，包括负迁移率（negative mobility）输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙皮罗尖峰（Shapiro spikes）传统上由有效电路模型（effective circuit models）描述，缺乏微观机制解释；本文旨在在中性量子流体中寻找该共振现象并阐明其微观起源。

Method: 对电势偏置的原子约瑟夫森结（potential-biased atomic Josephson junction）进行数值模拟，分析直流电流（dc-current）共振随调制强度的标度行为，并考察相位绕转频率（phase-winding frequency）的锁定特性。

Result: 直流电流共振遵循预期的贝塞尔函数（Bessel-function）标度，包括随调制增强出现的单个共振抑制与复现；相位绕转频率在驱动频率整数倍处形成有限平台，锁定平台内直流电流与密度转移可反向而相位绕转频率仍锁定于驱动，产生负迁移率输运。

Conclusion: 研究建立了涡旋介导相位滑移（vortex-mediated phase slips）、沙皮罗共振与中性量子流体中反常输运（anomalous transport）之间的微观联系，表明量子化涡旋将共振输运转化为具有负迁移率的相位锁定动力学。

Abstract: Periodic driving of a Josephson junction produces resonant transport signatures known as Shapiro spikes, conventionally described by effective circuit models. Here, we demonstrate Shapiro spikes in a neutral quantum fluid using simulations of a potential-biased atomic Josephson junction. The dc-current resonances follow the expected Bessel-function scaling, including the suppression and revival of individual resonances with increasing modulation strength. We uncover their microscopic mechanism by showing that the phase-winding frequency develops finite plateaus at integer multiples of the drive frequency. These phase-locked states are sustained by quantized-vortex nucleation and the associated $2π$ phase slips. Remarkably, within a locking plateau, the dc current and density transfer can reverse direction while the phase-winding frequency remains locked to the drive, giving rise to negative-mobility transport. Our results establish a microscopic link between vortex-mediated phase slips, Shapiro resonances, and anomalous transport in a neutral quantum fluid, revealing how quantized vortices convert resonant transport into phase-locked dynamics with negative mobility.

</details>


### [87] [Coherence Spreading Law in Quenched Homogeneous Bose Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05775)
*Yi Cao,Li-Chen Zhao,Jie Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作从解析与数值两方面研究淬火均匀玻色气体（bosonic gases）中的非平衡相干性扩散（coherence spreading），确认相干长度平方随时间的线性增长及其对初始能量与愈合能量（healing energy）之比的依赖，并给出普适扩散速率的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验观测到普适的相干性扩散速度上限（Nature 647, 608-612 (2025)），其动力学起源尚不清楚，亟需理论解释并厘清该普适性成立的条件。

Method: 采用系综平均的 Gross-Pitaevskii 轨迹（Gross-Pitaevskii trajectory）方法与动理学分析（kinetic analysis），结合解析推导与数值计算。

Result: 证实相干长度平方（squared coherence length）随时间线性增长，并显式给出扩散规律对初始能量与愈合能量之比的依赖；当该比值较小时，扩散速率普适，仅由普朗克常数与粒子质量之比决定，对相互作用强度和初始动量分布不敏感。

Conclusion: 进一步计算揭示了该普适性的基本限制，其相边界（phase boundaries）由低系统能量、弱粒子间相互作用和多体可积性（many-body integrability）决定；理论完整重现关键实验特征并解释普适相干扩散速度上限的动力学起源，同时给出超冷原子实验的可检验预言。

Abstract: We investigate nonequilibrium coherence spreading in quenched homogeneous bosonic gases both analytically and numerically. Our ensemble-averaged Gross-Pitaevskii trajectory approach and kinetic analysis confirm the linear-in-time growth of the squared coherence length, and explicitly give the spreading-law dependence on the ratio of initial energy to healing energy. When the ratio is relatively small, the squared coherence length grows at a universal rate determined by the ratio of Planck's constant to the particle mass, insensitive to interaction strength and initial momentum distributions. However, our further calculations uncover fundamental limitations to such universality and establish its phase boundaries governed by low system energies, weak interparticle interactions and many-body integrability. Our theory fully reproduces the key experimental signatures and resolves the puzzle of the dynamical origin of the recently observed universal coherence-spreading speed limit [Nature 647, 608-612 (2025)]. Going beyond existing experimental observations, we provide some testable predictions for ultracold-atom experiments and clarify the conditions for the emergence of universal coherent dynamics in nonequilibrium bosonic many-body systems.

</details>


### [88] [Analytic two-body density-matrix spectrum of the Fermionic Tonks-Girardeau gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05820)
*Abel Rojo-Francàs,Joan Martorell,Francesc Sabater,Bruno Juliá-Díaz*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文解析求解了费米型Tonks-Girardeau气体（fermionic Tonks-Girardeau gas）双体密度矩阵（two-body density matrix, TBDM）的完整谱，识别出集合态与单行列式态两类本征态，并给出大本征值的渐近展开，证明其随粒子数线性增长从而揭示非对角长程序（off-diagonal long-range order）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双体密度矩阵（two-body density matrix, TBDM）蕴含粒子关联（如配对）信息，但该体系TBDM的完整谱结构及本征态物理起源尚缺乏系统性刻画。

Method: 利用二次量子化（second quantization）下的精确基态波函数，解析推导任意粒子数下TBDM的全部本征值与本源。

Result: 得到全部本征值与本源解析表达式，识别出由配对动量模叠加形成的集合态（collective states）与单行列式态两类；最大本征值呈粒子数线性标度，高指标集合态本征值按 n^{-4} 衰减且本源趋于与粒子数无关。

Conclusion: 该结构可用复合玻色子（composite bosons）有效描述，其谱性质与相互作用玻色体系的Tan contact行为一致。

Abstract: The two-body density matrix (TBDM) of a quantum many-body system provides information about particle correlations, e.g., pairing. In this work, we derive the complete spectrum of the TBDM of the fermionic Tonks-Girardeau gas using its exact ground-state wavefunction in second quantization. We obtain analytical expressions for all eigenvalues and eigenvectors for an arbitrary number of particles and identify two different types of eigenstates: collective states, formed by superpositions of paired momentum modes, and single-determinant states. We provide a physical interpretation of this structure, explaining the origin of the different eigenstates and their degeneracies. We further derive an asymptotic expansion for the largest eigenvalue, improving on previous estimates and analytically confirming its linear scaling with the particle number, thereby indicating off-diagonal long-range order. For the high-index collective states, we obtain simpler expressions for the eigenvalues and eigenvectors, showing that the eigenvalues decay as $n^{-4}$ and that the eigenvectors become independent of the number of particles. Finally, we discuss the connection between the collective states and an effective description in terms of composite bosons, whose spectral properties suggest a behavior consistent with Tan's contact for interacting bosonic systems.

</details>


### [89] [Majorana-like Josephson branch structure in one-dimensional transport of cold atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06693)
*Rory Whelan,Dimitri M. Gangardt,Igor V. Lerner*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究了吸引相互作用费米子一维通道连接两个三维BCS库的相位控制输运，发现通道相位刚度与接触对隧穿的竞争产生两族4π周期稳态，可用两个边界Majorana算符描述；重整化群分析表明零温下分支交叉受保护，并提出可实验检验的Majorana类Josephson分支特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究吸引相互作用的一维无隙通道在连接三维BCS超导库时，通道相位刚度与接触处库珀对隧穿的竞争如何影响平衡态的相位周期性，以及是否出现Majorana类（Majorana-like）的4π周期分支物理。

Method: 采用相位控制输运的理论框架，分析通道相位刚度（phase stiffness）与两端接触对隧穿（pair tunnelling）的竞争；以两个边界Majorana算符（Majorana operators）组合成非局域费米子模的形式描述分支结构；并通过重整化群（renormalization-group）分析考察量子涨落与相位滑移（phase slips）的影响。

Result: 系统出现两族4π周期稳态，其特征为边界处对称或不对称的相位降（phase drops）。将库相位差推进2π会交换这两族，使平衡能量保持2π周期，而4π周期性可能仅在非绝热（diabatic）区域实现。重整化群分析显示，在零温热力学极限下量子涨落与相位滑移被抑制，分支交叉得以保留。

Conclusion: 该结构可在两个边界Majorana算符构成的非局域费米子模框架下形式化描述；预测的分支交换（branch exchange）、亚稳电流（metastable currents）和依赖扫描速率的切换（sweep-rate-dependent switching）为无隙相互作用通道中的Majorana类Josephson分支物理提供了可实验检验的特征。

Abstract: We study phase-controlled transport through a one-dimensional channel of attractively interacting fermions connecting two three-dimensional BCS reservoirs. The competition between channel phase stiffness and pair tunnelling at the two contacts results in the emergence of two $4π$-periodic families of stationary states, characterised by symmetric or asymmetric phase drops at the boundaries. Advancing the reservoir phase difference by $2π$ exchanges these families so that the equilibrium energy remains $2π$ periodic, while the $4π$ periodicity might be attained in a diabatic regime. This structure admits a formal description in terms of two boundary Majorana operators, combining into a nonlocal fermion mode. Renormalization-group analysis shows that quantum fluctuations and phase slips are suppressed in the zero-temperature thermodynamic limit, preserving the branch crossings. The predicted branch exchange, metastable currents, and sweep-rate-dependent switching provide experimentally testable signatures of Majorana-like Josephson branch physics in a gapless interacting channel.

</details>

### [90] [A Note On Lattice Definition of Gauge Theories With Chiral Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05051)
*Edward Witten*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析一种格点规范场与手征费米子耦合算法的变体：将格点规范场插值为连续规范场，再与连续手征费米子耦合。该方案在原理上适用于任意维度和任意无反常表示，但因手征费米子路径积分非正，无法用蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）方法实际计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于费米子倍增问题（fermion doubling problem），构造能研究四维规范理论与手征费米子（chiral fermions）耦合的格点算法长期是难题；已有 Craig 提出的算法及若干部分结果，本文旨在给出其一个变体。

Method: 基于将格点规范场在连续时空上插值（interpolating），并把插值后的规范场与连续手征费米子耦合；该变体亦由 Hsu 近期独立提出。

Result: 在任意维度和任意无反常表示（anomaly-free representation）下，该方法原则上可在计算机上实现。

Conclusion: 实际执行受限：手征费米子路径积分（path integral）不具备正性（not positive），因此无法以蒙特卡洛方法进行数值模拟。

Abstract: Because of the fermion doubling problem, it has been a longstanding challenge to describe a lattice algorithm for studying four-dimensional gauge theories coupled to chiral fermions. Recently, an algorithm to do so has been described by Craig, extending partial results in the previous literature. Here we describe a variant of this algorithm, based on interpolating a lattice gauge field over the continuum (as in Craig's work) and coupling the interpolated gauge field to continuum chiral fermions. The same variant was also proposed recently by Hsu. In any dimension and for any anomaly-free representation, this approach can be implemented on a computer in principle, but not in practice by Monte Carlo methods because the chiral fermion path integral is not positive.

</details>


### [91] [Quantum Computing for Entanglement Asymmetry and the Quantum Mpemba Effect in Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05987)
*Keisuke Fujii,Harunobu Fujimura,Masazumi Honda,Duc Truyen Le*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一种量子测量协议（quantum measurement protocol），用于估计Rényi纠缠不对称性（Rényi entanglement asymmetry），并将其应用于格点Schwinger模型中量子Mpemba效应（quantum Mpemba effect）的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠不对称性可刻画子系统层面的对称性破缺（symmetry breaking），是量子Mpemba效应的探针，但缺乏高效的测量方案，尤其在量子多体系统与格点量子场论中。

Method: 协议将SWAP测试（SWAP test）与基于随机对称旋转（randomized symmetry rotations）的电荷分辨程序（charge-resolving procedure）相结合；并以经典态矢量模拟（classical state-vector simulations）研究含θ项的格点Schwinger模型。

Result: 在固定加性精度ε下，所需测量次数按O(ε^{-2})标度且不显含总系统尺寸依赖；论文还估计了在NISQ设备、容错量子计算机（FTQCs）及代表性早期FTQC架构上实施该协议所需资源。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究量子多体系统与量子场论中的反常对称性恢复动力学（anomalous symmetry-restoration dynamics）提供了一个测量高效的框架。

Abstract: We construct a quantum measurement protocol for estimating the Rényi entanglement asymmetry, which quantifies symmetry breaking at the level of a subsystem and provides a probe of the quantum Mpemba effect. The protocol combines the SWAP test with a charge-resolving procedure based on randomized symmetry rotations. For a fixed additive accuracy $ε$, the required number of measurements scales as $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-2})$ without explicit dependence on the total system size, making the protocol particularly attractive for quantum many-body systems and lattice quantum field theories. As a representative application, we study the quantum Mpemba effect in the lattice Schwinger model with a $θ$ term using classical state-vector simulations. We further estimate the resources required to implement the protocol on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices, fault-tolerant quantum computers (FTQCs), and a representative early-FTQC architecture. Our results provide a measurement-efficient framework for investigating anomalous symmetry-restoration dynamics in quantum many-body systems and quantum field theories.

</details>


### [92] [Simulating a chiral gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06880)
*Laurens Lootens,Hersh Singh*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于 clock-and-shift qudit 与 Z_N 规范场的 1+1 维阿贝尔手征规范理论（abelian chiral gauge theories）的规范不变哈密顿格点正则化方案，并利用无限矩阵乘积态（infinite matrix product states）数值验证其能重现连续理论的红外物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手征规范理论（chiral gauge theories）是标准模型的基础，但迄今尚无非微扰模拟；规避 Nielsen-Ninomiya 障碍的格点构造通常需要复费米子测度或无限维局域希尔伯特空间，代价高昂。

Method: 以 clock-and-shift qudit 配合 Z_N 规范场构造规范不变的哈密顿格点正则化，使整体手征对称性及其 't Hooft 异常（'t Hooft anomaly）精确保持到 mod N；借助格点上的异常抵消（anomaly cancellation）精确施加并求解高斯定律（Gauss laws），将理论约化为无约束的 qudit 链，再用无限矩阵乘积态进行模拟。

Result: 对 1+1 维标准手征规范理论即 34-50 模型进行模拟，结果与解析预言高度一致；N=6 的有限维局域希尔伯特空间即可重现连续 34-50 模型预期的红外物理。

Conclusion: 该工作为在经典与量子计算机上模拟 1+1 维一般阿贝尔手征规范理论提供了高效且实用的方案。

Abstract: Chiral gauge theories underlie the Standard Model, yet none has been simulated nonperturbatively. Lattice constructions that evade the Nielsen-Ninomiya obstruction entail complex fermion measures or infinite-dimensional local Hilbert spaces. Here we provide a gauge-invariant Hamiltonian lattice regularization of 1+1-dimensional abelian chiral gauge theories built from clock-and-shift qudits with $\mathbb{Z}_N$ gauge fields, in which the global chiral symmetry and its 't Hooft anomaly survive exactly mod $N$. Anomaly cancellation on the lattice lets the Gauss laws be imposed and solved exactly, reducing the theory to an unconstrained qudit chain. Using infinite matrix product states, we simulate the canonical 1+1-dimensional chiral gauge theory, the 34-50 model, and find excellent agreement with analytic predictions, showing that a finite-dimensional local Hilbert space at $N=6$ reproduces the expected infrared physics of the continuum 34-50 model. This provides an efficient and practical solution to the problem of simulating general abelian chiral gauge theories in 1+1 dimensions, both on classical and quantum computers.

</details>


### [93] [Exact chiral symmetry, non-Hermiticity, and the axial anomaly on the lattice: A symmetry-class criterion for disorder-induced undoubling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05395)
*Laurence A. Jacobs*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该文重新审视1983年提出的手征无序费米子（chiral disordered fermions, CDF）格点狄拉克算子，证明其无序平均算子精确满足反对易关系，但因手征对称性严格保持而导致轴矢指标（axial index）恒为零，因而无法重现轴矢反常（axial anomaly）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDF算子原被设计为通过淬火随机场（quenched random field）加权导数来精确保持手征对称性，但此前缺乏对其拓扑与指标性质的现代分析；该文旨在判定这一“无序路线”能否给出正确的手征格点费米子与轴矢反常。

Method: 以现代工具（含非厄米（non-Hermitian）算子的双正交指标（bi-orthogonal index）与Riesz投影算子指标）重新分析无序平均CDF算子，并依承载格点结构的狄拉克通道（Dirac channel）对邻近构造进行分类，考察由1983年算子到Kaplan畴壁（domain wall）的一参数族插值。

Result: 无序平均算子在所有无序强度阶上精确满足手征反对易关系；无序通过非厄米性而非Ginsparg--Wilson关系解除加倍子（doubler）简并，将加倍子驱离实轴至复质量而保持物理模式受保护。由此在三种表述下轴矢指标均为零（含例外点（exceptional points）处），CDF算子无法重现轴矢反常。分类表明：μ通道无序保持手征性并实现Altland--Znrbauer手征类但不承载指标；𝟙质量通道无序破坏手征性并给出Callan--Harvey/畴壁物理。

Conclusion: CDF算子的失败由其精确保持的手征对称性所强制；该工作将格点QCD的去加倍（undoubling）与无序狄拉克费米子的十重分类（tenfold-way classification）统一起来，并以无序所占通道而非无序本身作为区分判据，厘清了通向手征格点费米子的无序路线。

Abstract: A lattice Dirac operator designed to preserve exact chiral symmetry by weighting the derivative with a quenched random field---the \emph{chiral disordered fermions} (\CDF) of [L.\ Jacobs, Phys.\ Rev.\ Lett.\ \textbf{51}, 172 (1983)]---is reexamined with modern tools. We show that the disorder-averaged operator satisfies $\anticomm{\Deff}{\gfive}=0$ exactly, to all orders in disorder strength. Disorder lifts the doubler degeneracy, driving doublers off the real axis into a complex mass while the physical mode remains protected, but does so through non-Hermiticity rather than a Ginsparg--Wilson relation. Consequently the axial index vanishes in every formulation: the Hermitian trace $\Tr(\gfive D)$, the bi-orthogonal index for non-Hermitian operators, and the algebraic Riesz-projector index, including at exceptional points. The \CDF operator therefore cannot reproduce the axial anomaly: its failure is enforced by the exact chiral symmetry it preserves. We classify neighboring constructions by the Dirac channel carrying the lattice structure. Disorder in the $\g_μ$ channel preserves chirality, realizes an Altland--Zirnbauer chiral class, and cannot carry an index; disorder in the $\mathbb{1}$ mass channel breaks chiral symmetry and yields Callan--Harvey/domain-wall physics. A one-parameter family interpolates between them, with the 1983 operator and Kaplan's domain wall as opposite limits, distinguished not by disorder itself but by the channel it occupies. This unifies lattice-QCD undoubling with the tenfold-way classification of disordered Dirac fermions and settles the disorder route to chiral lattice fermions.

</details>
