### [94] [Toward Shifted symplectic Chiralization: Deformation of Courant Algebroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06031)
*Wenda Fang,Noriaki Ikeda*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文通过分次辛几何（graded symplectic geometry）研究 Poisson–Lie 理论的手征化（chiralization），比较 Courant 形变与经典 r-/R-矩阵构造，给出可积层级（integrable hierarchies）与自由场实现，并建立相容性障碍的变分描述与全阶量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于以移位辛手征化（shifted symplectic chiralization）作为几何方法，构造 Poisson–Lie 结构的手征类比，并厘清 Courant 形变与经典 r-/R-矩阵构造之间的关系。

Method: 方法上采用分次辛几何框架，比较 Courant 形变（Courant deformations）与经典 r-矩阵、R-矩阵构造，并对 Lie 共形双代数（Lie conformal bialgebra）相容性障碍给出变分描述；同时引入量子化泛包络代数的顶点双代数（vertex bialgebra）类比。

Result: 获得可积层级与自由场实现（free field realizations）；在 Virasoro 情形下复现 Liberati 障碍；并对 $\operatorname{Cur}(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 上 Cartan 型 Lie 共形双代数结构构造出全阶显式量子化。

Conclusion: 结果服务于构建移位辛手征化的更广泛研究纲领，即为 Poisson–Lie 结构构造手征类比，并探索其与 AKSZ 理论及量子化的联系。

Abstract: We study the chiralization of Poisson--Lie theory through graded symplectic geometry. We compare Courant deformations and classical $r$- and $R$-matrix constructions, obtaining integrable hierarchies and free field realizations. We give a variational description of the obstruction to Lie conformal bialgebra compatibility and recover Liberati's obstruction in the Virasoro case. We also introduce a vertex bialgebra analogue of quantized universal enveloping algebras and construct explicit quantizations to all orders of Cartan type Lie conformal bialgebra structures on $\operatorname{Cur}(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$. These results contribute to the first author's broader programme of developing shifted symplectic chiralization as a geometric method for constructing chiral analogues of Poisson-Lie structures via $n$-shifted symplectic geometry, and exploring its connections with AKSZ theory and quantization.

</details>


### [95] [Vertex Operator Algebras in Lean: a formal library and its companion book](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06616)
*Fedor K. Popov,Leonardo Rastelli*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: LeanVOA 是一个基于 Lean 4 的形式化数学库，覆盖顶点算子代数（vertex operator algebras, VOA）理论，并配套出版逐条对照教材陈述的书籍。该库形式化了 Moonshine 模、Zhu 模不变性、Verlinde 公式、$c=1$ VOA 分类等内容，同时给出若干新结果，且不引入任何自设公理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机；从内容可推知其目标是将顶点算子代数理论大规模形式化（formalization）并与教材表述并列呈现，以便验证与复用。

Method: 采用 Lean 4 构建形式化库，逐条编码 VOA 相关定义与定理；配套书籍将每个形式化结果与教材陈述并列展示，并以不引入自设公理的方式组织证明。

Result: 已完成 Moonshine 模 $V^\natural$ 的构造，形式化了 Zhu 模不变性（modular invariance）、Huang 的张量范畴与 Verlinde 公式、$c=1$ VOA 的 Gannon-Rayhaun 分类，以及边界可容许 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 与极小模型 $M(2,2m+3)$ 的经典自由性（classical freeness）。新结果包括：所有 Virasoro 极小模型及任意可容许能级 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 的分次一阶刚性（graded first-order rigidity）；$L_{-3/2}(\mathfrak{sl}_3)$ 及 Deligne 系列其他成员在给定假设下的经典自由性；边界可容许 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 的分次酉性（graded unitarity）蕴含极小模型 $M(2,2n+1)$ 的分次酉性。

Conclusion: LeanVOA 在不引入自设公理的前提下，实现了 VOA 理论从经典定理到新结果的大规模机器验证，并借助配套书籍建立形式化与教材之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We present LeanVOA, a Lean 4 library for the theory of vertex operator algebras (VOAs), together with a companion book that sets each formalized result next to its textbook statement. The library constructs the Moonshine module $V^\natural$ and formalizes Zhu's modular invariance, Huang's tensor categories with the Verlinde formula, the Gannon-Rayhaun classification of $c=1$ VOAs, and classical freeness of the boundary admissible $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ (Arakawa-Dai) and of the minimal models $M(2,2m+3)$ (van Ekeren-Heluani). It also contains new results: graded first-order rigidity of all Virasoro minimal models and of $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ at every admissible level; classical freeness of $L_{-3/2}(\mathfrak{sl}_3)$, and of the other members of the Deligne series under stated assumptions; and a proof that graded unitarity of the boundary admissible $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ implies that of the minimal models $M(2,2n+1)$. The library declares no axiom of its own.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Topological localization in unentangled random braids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05721)
*Daniil Fedotov,Pieter Hendrik Willem Van Der Hoek,Sergei Nechaev*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文重新解释辫状无纠缠随机线束的横向涨落：Serna 等观察到的对数增长被论证为暂态现象，足够长的聚合物将出现仅由集体拓扑约束导致、类似无序局域化的拓扑局域化（topological localization），并推测其随线数 n 呈现相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2015 年 Serna、Bunin 与 Nahum 的数值工作显示，辫（braid）中单条线的横向跨度 $\left<|x_N|\right>$ 随聚合物长度 $N$ 对数增长，且与固定在平行线介质中的情形定性不同，说明拓扑相互作用具有集体性。本文旨在澄清该对数增长的本质并给出更一般的解释。

Method: 同时采用数值与解析手段：在“局部自由（locally free）”近似下研究辫，并借助局部自由群（locally free group）渐近 Cayley 图上的布朗桥（Brownian bridges）解析分析来支撑局域化结论。

Result: 对数区间被判定为暂态（transient），$N\to\infty$ 时 $\left<|x_N|\right>$ 趋于有限值。该局域化不由淬火外无序（quenched external disorder）造成，而完全源自集体拓扑约束。进一步推测存在相变：线数少时（$n\sim 2\div5$）跨度代数增长，$N\gg1$ 时为常规标度 $\left<|x_N|\right>\sim N^{1/4}$；线数 $n$ 足够大时，长辫中每条线渐近局域化，$\left<|x_N|\right>\sim \mathrm{const}$。

Conclusion: 拓扑相互作用可通过纯集体机制产生局域化，且可能以线数为控制参数呈现从代数增长到局域化的相变；该结论基于局部自由近似与布朗桥解析，相变猜想尚待验证。

Abstract: In 2015, P. Serna, G. Bunin, and A. Nahum numerically investigated fluctuations in a bunch of unentangled random threads. They showed that fluctuations of an individual thread in a braid are qualitatively different from those of a thread embedded in a medium of fixed parallel lines. In particular, Serna et al. observed a logarithmic increase in the average transverse span, $\left<|x_N|\right>$, of a thread with polymer length, $N$. This behavior highlights the collective nature of topological interactions, in which each polymer fluctuates and constrains the motion of the others. Studying this problem both numerically and analytically, we propose the following interpretation: the logarithmic regime is transient, and sufficiently long polymers exhibit topological localization, where $\left<|x_N|\right>$ approaches a finite limit at $N\to\infty$. Unlike localization induced by quenched external disorder, this phenomenon arises solely from collective topological constraints. We study braids within a locally free approximation and support the localization by analytical investigation of Brownian bridges on the asymptotic Cayley graph of a locally free group. We further conjecture that topologically induced localization emerges as a phase transition: for a small number of threads, $n \sim 2÷5$, the span, $\left<|x_N|\right>$, grows algebraically, with the usual scaling $\left<|x_N|\right>\sim N^{1/4}$ at $N\gg 1$; by contrast, for sufficiently large $n$, each thread in a long braid ($N\gg 1$) asymptotically localizes, approaching $\left<|x_N|\right>\sim \mathrm{const}$.

</details>


### [97] [Thermodynamic signatures of the period-doubling route to chemical chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06037)
*Kyota Tamano,Keiji Saito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究可逆、充分混合化学反应网络中，振荡经倍周期分岔走向混沌时熵产生涨落的普适性质：孤立超临界倍周期分岔处扩散常数呈指数为1的单侧发散，混沌吸引子上单位体积熵方差在宏观极限下指数增长，速率等于两倍最大李雅普诺夫指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学反应网络可从稳定振荡经连续倍周期分岔（period doubling）演化至混沌（chaos），但混沌附近及混沌区内熵产生涨落（entropy-production fluctuations）的一般规律尚缺乏系统刻画，本文旨在揭示其普适标度行为与物理机制。

Method: 针对可逆、充分混合（well-mixed）反应网络，分析孤立超临界倍周期分岔（supercritical period-doubling bifurcation）附近的扩散常数行为，并在满足给定条件的混沌吸引子（chaotic attractor）上考察宏观极限下单位体积熵方差（entropy variance）的增长，进一步讨论涨落对体积（volume）的依赖及背后物理机制。

Result: 孤立超临界倍周期分岔处，扩散常数（diffusion constant）以指数1单侧发散；对满足条件的混沌吸引子，若先取宏观极限，单位体积熵方差呈指数增长，其增长率为2λ_max（λ_max为最大李雅普诺夫指数（largest Lyapunov exponent）），意味着扩散常数发散。

Conclusion: 混沌边缘与混沌区内的熵产生涨落表现出由倍周期分岔指数与最大李雅普诺夫指数控制的普适发散与指数增长行为，且涨落性质依赖取极限顺序与系统体积。

Abstract: Chemical reaction networks can evolve from stable oscillations to chaos through successive period doublings. We investigate general properties of entropy-production fluctuations near the onset of chaos and within the chaotic regime in reversible, well-mixed reaction networks. Near an isolated supercritical period-doubling bifurcation, we find a one-sided divergence of the diffusion constant with exponent one. For chaotic attractors satisfying the stated conditions, the entropy variance per unit volume grows exponentially when the macroscopic limit is taken first. Its exponential growth rate is $2λ_{\max}$, where $λ_{\max}$ is the largest Lyapunov exponent, implying a divergent diffusion constant. We also discuss the volume dependence of the fluctuations and the underlying physical mechanisms.

</details>


### [98] [Statistical mechanics and hierarchy of learnability in quantum codes: From generalized symmetries to non-Abelian topological orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06154)
*Hiromi Ebisu,Masazumi Honda*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文把拓扑稳定子码（topological stabilizer codes）中局域弱测量（local weak measurements）的贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）问题映射为具有广义对称性（generalized symmetries）的经典自旋模型，并利用对称性导出可学习性（learnability）的相关恒等式与层级关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域弱测量只能提供关于编码于量子码中的可观测量的含噪信息，需要刻画哪些非局域观测量（尤其是与分数化激发（fractionalized excitations）相关者）可从测量记录中被学习。

Method: 将贝叶斯推断问题映射到具有广义对称性的经典自旋模型；当波函数形变（wavefunction deformation）与高斯测量强度（Gaussian measurement strengths）重合时，复制模型（replicated model）获得扩大的对称性，据此导出测量记录平均的关联函数（correlators）间的精确恒等式，并结合洁净铁磁模型（clean ferromagnetic models）中的关联不等式。

Result: 得到可学习性的层级（hierarchies of learnability）：若两个观测量在长距离上仍可学习，则其乘积也必须可学习；在 rank-2 toric code 与 X-cube 模型（分别对应调制对称性（modulated symmetries）与子系统对称性（subsystem symmetries））中展示了这些关系，并推广至有限群量子双模型（quantum double models，含非阿贝尔情形），给出可学习性与关联间的精确关系及任意子（anyons）表示结构与融合规则（fusion rules）带来的额外约束。

Conclusion: 结果限制了可被同时学习的非局域信息类型，为局域弱测量下量子码的学习相图（learning phase diagrams）提供指导。

Abstract: Local weak measurements provide noisy information about observables encoded in a quantum code. We formulate the corresponding Bayesian inference problem for a broad class of topological stabilizer codes by mapping it to classical spin models with generalized symmetries. When the wavefunction deformation and Gaussian measurement strengths coincide, the replicated model acquires an enlarged symmetry. We use this symmetry to derive exact identities among correlators averaged over measurement records that quantify the learnability of nonlocal observables associated with fractionalized excitations. Together with correlation inequalities in the associated clean ferromagnetic models, these identities establish hierarchies of learnability: if two observables remain learnable at long distances, their product must also remain learnable. We demonstrate these relations in the rank-2 toric code and the X-cube model, whose classical descriptions exhibit modulated and subsystem symmetries, respectively. We further extend the analysis to finite group quantum double models, including non-Abelian ones. We derive exact relations between learnability and correlations in the corresponding classical models, and show that the representation structure and fusion rules of anyons impose further constraints under suitable positivity conditions. These results constrain which types of nonlocal information can be learned simultaneously, providing a guide to learning phase diagrams in quantum codes under local weak measurements.

</details>


### [99] [Brownian gyrator model with stochastic resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06322)
*Satya N. Majumdar,Gregory Schehr,Pascal Viot,Gleb Oshanin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究布朗回转器（Brownian gyrator, BG）在恒定速率随机重置（stochastic resetting）扰动下的稳态响应，解析与数值给出位置概率密度函数（probability density function, PDF）及其边缘分布和矩、局域概率流与涡度场，并量化平均角动量与平均角速度随重置速率的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布朗回转器是远离热力学平衡（far from thermodynamic equilibrium）的最小且具实验相关性的模型，其持续流具有非零涡度（vorticity）。作者希望考察该系统对一个本质上非平衡的扰动——随机重置——的响应，从而揭示两种具有不同空间对称性的非平衡机制之间的竞争。

Method: 采用解析与数值相结合的方法，考虑两类重置目标：平面中的固定点，以及圆周上随机选取的点；据此求解稳态位置概率密度函数及其边缘分布与矩，并刻画相应的局域概率流（probability currents）与涡度场。

Result: 稳态位置PDF及其边缘分布和矩表现出由重置引起的对重置速率的非单调依赖（non-monotonic dependence）；同时给出了局域概率流与涡度场，并考察了平均角动量（mean angular momentum）与平均角速度（mean angular velocity）对重置速率的依赖，数值分析了这两个可观测量的PDF。

Conclusion: 该分析完整量化了两种具有不同空间对称性的非平衡机制相互竞争所产生的复杂行为。

Abstract: The Brownian gyrator (BG) model provides a minimal yet experimentally relevant example of a system operating far from thermodynamic equilibrium, sustaining steady currents with nonzero vorticity and exhibiting stochastic rotational motion along elliptical orbits. In this paper, we study the response of the BG to a fundamentally non-equilibrium perturbation -- stochastic resetting at a constant rate -- either to a fixed point in the plane or to a randomly selected point on a circle. We determine analytically and numerically the steady-state position probability density function (PDF), together with its marginals and moments, which exhibit a resetting-induced non-monotonic dependence on the resetting rate. We also characterize the associated local probability currents and vorticity fields. Furthermore, we investigate the dependence of the mean angular momentum and mean angular velocity on the resetting rate, and numerically analyze the PDFs of these observables. This analysis fully quantifies the complex behavior arising from the competition between two non-equilibrium mechanisms with distinct spatial symmetries.

</details>


### [100] [Kardar--Parisi--Zhang density hydrodynamics in a coherent XXZ--ASEP Lindbladian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06369)
*Yansheng Liu,Daoguang Li,Kaixiang Lu,Mingdi Xu,Lei Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在具有不对称 Lindblad 跃迁的 XXZ 链中，尽管存在相干相互作用，其稳态电流仍呈 ASEP 形式；该电流的曲率与静态磁化率（static susceptibility）无需拟合参数即确定 KPZ 长度与振幅，且 Liouvillian 谱、实空间扩散与傅里叶弛豫均支持有限相干耦合下的 KPZ 标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干相互作用（coherent interactions）可在不改变稳态密度电流的前提下产生量子关联，因此该电流是否仍决定长波输运是一个动力学问题，需要定量检验。

Method: 研究带不对称 Lindblad 跃迁（asymmetric Lindblad hopping）的 XXZ 链，其稳态电流具有非对称简单排他过程（ASEP）形式但布居不自主演化；由电流曲率与静态磁化率确定 KPZ 长度与振幅，并结合可见密度（density-visible）的 Liouvillian 谱、实空间扩散与傅里叶弛豫进行分析，辅以与热库匹配的经典参照（reservoir-matched classical references）。

Result: 在所研究的有限相干耦合下，谱、实空间扩散与傅里叶弛豫均支持 Kardar–Parisi–Zhang（KPZ）标度，且无需拟合的长度或振幅转换因子；中心与中间稳态密度关联在该微观尺度上与 Prähofer–Spohn 分布一致，并分辨出有限时间修正；经典参照量化了边界效应，对称跃迁恢复扩散行为，约化不对称性给出预期的交叉（crossover）。

Conclusion: 结果将精确的非线性电流与物理密度所观测到的长波 KPZ 输运联系起来，适用于远离各向同性海森堡点（isotropic Heisenberg point）的相互作用开放量子链。

Abstract: Coherent interactions can generate quantum correlations without changing a stationary density current. Whether that current still determines long-wavelength transport is a dynamical question. We study an XXZ chain with asymmetric Lindblad hopping, whose stationary current has the asymmetric simple exclusion process (ASEP) form although its populations do not evolve autonomously. The current curvature and static susceptibility fix the leading Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) length and amplitude without a fitted length or amplitude conversion factor. The density-visible Liouvillian spectrum, real-space spreading, and Fourier relaxation support KPZ scaling at the finite coherent couplings studied. Central and intermediate stationary density correlations are consistent with the Prähofer--Spohn profile at this microscopic scale, with resolved finite-time corrections. Reservoir-matched classical references quantify boundary effects, while symmetric hopping recovers diffusion and reduced asymmetry produces the expected crossover. These results connect an exact nonlinear current to the observed long-wavelength KPZ transport of the physical density in an interacting open quantum chain away from the isotropic Heisenberg point.

</details>


### [101] [Marginal Operator Deformations and Pseudocriticality in Baxter--Wu Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06435)
*Hyun-Yong Lee,Naoki Kawashima,Nikolaos G. Fytas,Dong-Hee Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 弱一级相变可表现出模拟真实临界性的扩展赝临界标度区；稀Baxter–Wu模型的表观临界性源于边缘相关重整化群流，最终流入共存的一级相变区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期争论的稀Baxter–Wu模型（dilute Baxter–Wu model）是否真正具有临界性尚不明确，需澄清弱一级相变与真实临界性混淆的来源。

Method: 结合共形微扰理论（conformal perturbation theory）与对称性分辨的张量网络能级谱学（symmetry-resolved tensor-network level spectroscopy），将缓变耦合参数化为数值可及形式，并构建基于流的通用诊断工具。

Result: 稀与自对偶Baxter–Wu模型在边缘算符形变下的重整化群轨迹坍缩至稀四态Potts模型（dilute four-state Potts model）的多临界相图，揭示Potts点边缘相关与无关两侧的流结构。

Conclusion: 表观临界性由边缘相关重整化群流驱动，系统最终进入一级相变（first-order transition）与相共存区；所提流基诊断可泛化应用于弱一级相变判定。

Abstract: Weak first-order transitions can exhibit extended pseudocritical scaling regimes that mimic genuine criticality. We show that the long-debated apparent criticality of the dilute Baxter--Wu model arises from a marginally relevant renormalization-group flow, which ultimately drives the system into a first-order regime with phase coexistence. By integrating conformal perturbation theory with symmetry-resolved tensor-network level spectroscopy, we parameterize a slowly running coupling in a numerically accessible form and construct a generic flow-based diagnostic. The renormalization-group trajectories of the dilute and self-dual Baxter--Wu models under marginal operator deformations collapse onto the multicritical phase diagram of the dilute four-state Potts model, exposing the flow structure on both the marginally relevant and irrelevant sides of the Potts point.

</details>


### [102] [Steady states in asymmetric exclusion processes with reactions: Fluctuations and non-mean field behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06463)
*Rohn Chatterjee,Atri Goswami,Sudip Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文研究开阔几何中的完全非对称简单排他过程（totally asymmetric simple exclusion process, TASEP），引入体相中由过度拥挤引发的粒子产生（birth）与消亡（death）反应，并用平均场理论（mean-field theory, MFT）结合蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte-Carlo simulations, MCS）考察其定态与相共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规TASEP及相关模型在开放驱动系统中通常可用平均场理论较好描述，但构型依赖的体相反应对定态与关联的影响尚不清楚，论文旨在考察此类反应是否会使近邻关联（nearest-neighbour correlations）变得不可忽略。

Method: 在开边界TASEP中耦合由过度拥挤（overcrowding）触发的产生与消亡两类简单反应，采用平均场理论分析，并以大规模蒙特卡洛模拟作为补充与检验，数值量化关联效应。

Result: 定态一般具有空间依赖性（space-dependent），在部分参数选择下可出现相共存（phase coexistence）；与普通TASEP不同，构型依赖的体相反应使近邻关联在开放驱动系统中定量上变得重要，这一偏差被归因于MFT所忽略的关联效应。

Conclusion: 构型依赖的体相反应可显著削弱平均场理论在开放驱动TASEP中的适用性，近邻关联需被定量纳入；论文通过数值方法对该关联效应给出了量化。

Abstract: We study an totally asymmetric simple exclusion process (TASEP) in an open geometry. We couple TASEP with simple reactions in the form of production (birth) and degradation (death) due to over crowding taking place in the bulk. We investigate the steady states, which are generically space-dependent. Phase coexistences are also possible for some choices of the model parameters. We study the system by mean-field theories (MFT) supplemented by extensive Monte-Carlo simulations (MCS). Intriguingly, unlike oridinary TASEP and other related models, configuration-dependent bulk reactions in a TASEP model can make nearest-neighbour correlations quantitatively important even in an open driven system where mean-field theory would ordinarily be expected to provide a useful description. We attribute this to the correlation effects ignored in MFT. We numerically quantify this correlation effect.

</details>


### [103] [Mutual linearity in semi-Markov networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06490)
*Julian B. Voits,Ulrich S. Schwarz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文揭示随机网络（stochastic networks）中互线性（mutual linearity）源自一类全局仿射关系（affine relation）的满足，并将其推广至具有驻留时间记忆的半马尔可夫网络（semi-Markov networks）。研究发现半马尔可夫网络在占据统计与首达时间（first-passage times）统计之间存在根本性不对称，且结果可直接应用于随机重置（stochastic resetting）情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管随机网络微观复杂，却表现出与参数无关的普适性质，互线性即为其中一例；已有工作刻画了马尔可夫过程（Markov processes）在局部扰动下的成对线性关系，但该性质是否及如何推广到含记忆的半马尔可夫网络尚不清楚。

Method: 作者证明互线性在某一全局仿射关系被满足时成立，并据此分析半马尔可夫网络中一类广泛的局部扰动，其中网络状态具有驻留时间（dwell time）记忆；同时结合拉普拉斯空间（Laplace space）中的关系式与随机重置场景进行推导。

Result: 互线性在半马尔可夫网络的一类广泛局部扰动下仍然成立，但存在例外，说明该性质并非对半马尔可夫网络的所有扰动都普适；然而对首达时间而言这一限制消失，表明半马尔可夫网络在占据统计与首达统计之间存在不同于马尔可夫网络的基本不对称性。拉普拉斯空间关系在随机重置下获得物理解释，对应稳态占据（stationary occupations）与重置前到达目标的概率。

Conclusion: 互线性可由全局仿射关系导出，并可扩展至半马尔可夫网络的部分扰动类；半马尔可夫网络在占据与首达统计上的不对称性是其区别于马尔可夫网络的关键特征。随机重置为检验所预测仿射关系提供了自然的实验/理论场景。

Abstract: Despite their microscopic complexity, stochastic networks show a surprising degree of universal, parameter-independent properties. One example is mutual linearity, which has recently been characterized as a generic, pairwise-linear relation between observables of Markov processes under local perturbations, valid for both stationary dynamics and for transient dynamics in Laplace space. Here we show that mutual linearity arises if a certain global affine relation is satisfied. This allows us to demonstrate that mutual linearity also holds for a broad class of local perturbations in semi-Markov networks, which, unlike Markov networks, have memory in terms of the dwell time in the current state. However, exceptions exist, meaning that mutual linearity does not extend to all possible perturbations of semi-Markov networks. Strikingly, this restriction disappears for first-passage times. Thus, semi-Markov networks exhibit a fundamental asymmetry between occupation and first-passage statistics, different from Markov networks. Finally, we show that our results are directly applicable to stochastic resetting in semi-Markov networks, where the Laplace-space relations acquire a physical interpretation in terms of stationary occupations and the probability to reach a target before reset. This provides a natural setting in which the predicted affine relations can be probed.

</details>


### [104] [Dynamically Reentrant Skyrmion Phase in Oscillating Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06541)
*Z. Demir Vatansever,E. Vatansever,A. Vasilopoulos,N. G. Fytas,A. Berger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过蒙特卡洛模拟发现，二维Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用磁体在周期性振荡磁场下会出现无平衡对应物的动态重入斯格明子相，其特征是周期平均拓扑荷反号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究驱动拓扑物态（driven topological matter）中的一个核心开放问题：具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions）的二维磁体在周期驱动下的非平衡响应尚不清楚。

Method: 采用大规模蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations），对施加周期性振荡磁场的二维Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用磁体进行非平衡研究。

Result: 在有限的场振幅与偏置场（bias fields）窗口内发现动态重入斯格明子相（dynamically reentrant skyrmion phase），其位于常规斯格明子相与极化铁磁相（polarized ferromagnetic phase）之间；该相源于单个场周期内斯格明子产生与湮灭的不平衡，导致周期平均拓扑荷（period-averaged topological charge）反转；且仅存在于系统能跟随驱动的动态响应区，在更高驱动频率下被抑制。

Conclusion: 周期驱动可作为稳定并反转斯格明子拓扑（skyrmion topology）的非平衡控制参数（nonequilibrium control parameter）。

Abstract: We address a central open question in driven topological matter by investigating the nonequilibrium response of a two-dimensional magnet with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions subjected to a periodically oscillating magnetic field. Using extensive Monte Carlo simulations, we uncover a dynamically reentrant skyrmion phase that emerges within a finite window of field amplitudes and bias fields, sandwiched between the conventional skyrmion and polarized ferromagnetic phases. This phase has no equilibrium counterpart and originates from an imbalance between skyrmion creation and annihilation over a single field cycle, leading to a reversal of the period-averaged topological charge. We show that the re-entrant phase exists only in the dynamic response regime where the system can follow the drive, and is suppressed at higher driving frequencies. Our results establish periodic driving as a nonequilibrium control parameter for stabilizing and reversing skyrmion topology.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Representation theory and massive spectral zeta functions on spherical space forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05490)
*Victoria Martin,Allan Merino,Rahul Poddar,Simon Roby*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文用表示论方法（Peter-Weyl 定理与 Molien 级数）直接计算球空间形式 Γ\S³ 上 Laplace-Beltrami 算子的谱，并对偶数阶齐性商构造大质量标量动能算子谱 zeta 函数的亚纯延拓，得到闭式 zeta 正则化行列式，同时以 Jacobi theta 函数给出的热核迹作为独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是绕开传统微分方程或几何分析方法，直接从有限群 Γ ⊂ SO(4) 的表示论出发确定球空间形式上的谱数据，并为动能算子的泛函行列式提供可计算、可交叉验证的解析框架。

Method: 采用 Peter-Weyl 定理（Peter-Weyl theorem）与 Molien 级数（Molien series）计算 Γ\S³ 上 Laplace-Beltrami 算子（Laplace-Beltrami operator）的特征值及重数；对偶数阶齐性商构造谱 zeta 函数（spectral zeta function）的亚纯延拓（meromorphic continuation），并用 Jacobi theta 函数（Jacobi theta function）表达热核迹（heat trace）。

Result: 给出所有透镜空间（lens space）L(p,q) 与 SU(2) 各 ADE 类有限子群对应商的谱，涵盖齐性透镜空间、棱柱空间（prism space）及双四面体、双八面体、双二十面体群商（后者即 Poincaré 同调球）。动能算子的 zeta 正则化行列式（zeta-regularized determinant）以多重对数函数（polylogarithm）有限和形式闭式给出，对任意实质量成立，且仅通过群的阶与一组 twist 系数依赖于 Γ。

Conclusion: 热核迹的模性质将谱 zeta 函数与体积、闭测地线长度等几何数据相联系，并在大质量区给出以修正 Bessel 函数（modified Bessel function）表示的快收敛展开，为行列式提供独立检验；该框架可直接推广到高维流形商，留待后续工作。

Abstract: We study the spectral theory of the massive scalar kinetic operator on spherical space forms $Γ\backslash S^{3}$, where $Γ$ is a finite subgroup of $\mathrm{SO}(4)$ acting freely on $S^{3}$. Using the Peter-Weyl theorem and Molien series we compute the spectrum of the Laplace-Beltrami operator on $Γ\backslash S^3$ directly from representation theory, obtaining the eigenvalues and their multiplicities for all lens spaces $L(p,q)$ and for each ADE-class of finite subgroups of $\mathrm{SU}(2)$, which includes homogeneous lens spaces, prism spaces and quotients by the binary tetrahedral, octahedral and icosahedral groups, the last also known as the Poincaré homology sphere.
  For homogeneous quotients by subgroups of even order, we construct the meromorphic continuation of the spectral zeta function of the massive kinetic operator and obtain the general zeta-regularized determinant in closed form. The determinant is expressed as a finite sum of polylogarithms, valid for all real mass, and depends on $Γ$ only through its order and a finite set of parameters which we call twist coefficients fixed by the dimensions of the $Γ$-invariant subspaces.
  As a complementary approach, we express the heat trace of the kinetic operator on spherical space forms in terms of Jacobi theta functions, whose modular properties relate the spectral zeta function to geometric data, the volume and closed geodesic lengths of $Γ\backslash S^3$. We find that the spectral zeta function admits a rapidly convergent expansion in the heavy mass sector in terms of modified Bessel functions, and provides an independent check on the determinant.
  This framework extends to quotients of higher-dimensional manifolds directly, which we outline for future work.

</details>


### [106] [Rough-to-frozen transition in non-integrable dimer models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05516)
*Alessandro Giuliani,Vieri Mastropietro,Bruno Renzi,Fabio Toninelli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在非可积（non-integrable）六角格二聚体模型中严格证明了 Pokrovsky-Talapov 定律，即冻结相附近宏观高度剖面（limit shape）呈现 ε^{3/2} 型奇异性，并借助多尺度分析（构造性费米重整化群）揭示了指数 3/2 的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗糙相到冻结相（frozen phase/facet）的转变预期由 Pokrovsky-Talapov 定律支配，但此前仅在可精确求解的情形（如经典行列式型二维二聚体模型）中得到证明，非可积模型缺乏验证。

Method: 采用多尺度分析（multiscale analysis），即构造性费米重整化群（constructive fermionic Renormalization Group），研究六角格上格点间含弱 plaque 相互作用 的二维二聚体模型。

Result: 识别出两个具有不同标度性质（scaling properties）的区间：较小距离区间由有效二次色散关系（quadratic dispersion relation）刻画，另一区间则由 Luttinger 液体行为典型的有效线性色散关系刻画；由此解释了 Pokrovsky-Talapov 指数 3/2 的普适性，并与粗糙相中依赖相互作用的临界指数形成对比。

Conclusion: 作为构造的副产品，实现了相互作用二聚体模型标度极限（scaling limit）在距冻结相距离上的一致控制，从而在非可积情形下验证了 Pokrovsky-Talapov 定律。

Abstract: The transition from rough to frozen phases in models of random surfaces is expected to be governed by the Pokrovsky-Talapov law, which predicts a $\varepsilon^{3/2}$-type singularity of the macroscopic height profile (limit shape), as the distance $\varepsilon$ from the frozen phase (facet) is sent to zero. This has been proved so far only in exactly solvable cases, notably the classical, determinantal, 2D dimer model. In this paper we prove the validity of the Pokrovsky-Talapov law for a non-integrable 2D dimer model on the hexagonal lattice, whose Gibbs weight depends upon a weak plaquette interaction among dimers. Our proof is based on a multiscale analysis (constructive fermionic Renormalization Group), which allows us to identify two different regimes with distinct scaling properties and to explain the universality of the Pokrovsky-Talapov exponent $3/2$, in contrast with the interaction-dependent critical exponents characterizing the rough phase. The first regime, corresponding to smaller distances, is characterized by an effective quadratic dispersion relation, while the second comes with an effective linear dispersion relation, typical of Luttinger liquid behavior. As a byproduct of our construction, we control the scaling limit of the interacting dimer model uniformly in the distance from the frozen phase.

</details>


### [107] [Stroh--Hamiltonian Framework for Modeling Incompressible Poroelastic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05554)
*Kinza Arshad,Vincenzo Tibullo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文把 Stroh 六次形式（Stroh sextic formalism）视为典型哈密顿结构（canonical Hamiltonian structure），并将其推广至固体不可压缩且无流体压力梯度的 Biot 多孔弹性（Biot's poroelasticity）情形，推导出约束条件下的典型方程（canonical equations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stroh 形式在多层结构与时间简谐问题中因能自然嵌入物理约束而有效，但其与哈密顿结构的对应关系尚未用于多孔弹性问题；本文旨在填补这一空白，深化对固体不可压缩性与流固耦合（fluid-solid coupling）相互作用的理解。

Method: 基于 Stroh 六次形式与典型哈密顿结构的对应，将框架扩展至 Biot 多孔弹性，聚焦固体不可压缩、无流体压力梯度的约束情形，并在这些约束下推导典型方程。

Result: 获得约束条件下的典型方程，从而更清晰地刻画可逆多孔弹性行为（reversible poroelastic behavior）；该推导是本文的核心新意所在。

Conclusion: 框架假设完全排水条件（perfectly drained conditions）并忽略耗散效应（dissipative effects），故不适用于含能量损失或复杂流体动力学的现实场景；但为理解受约束多孔弹性系统奠定基础，并指向耗散与非线性（nonlinear）情形的后续扩展。

Abstract: The Stroh sextic formalism, developed by Stroh, offers a compelling framework for representing the equilibrium equations in anisotropic elasticity. This approach has proven particularly effective for studying multilayered structures and time-harmonic problems, owing to its ability to seamlessly integrate physical constraints into the analysis. By recognizing that the Stroh formalism aligns with the canonical Hamiltonian structure, this work extends its application to Biot's poroelasticity, focusing on scenarios where the solid material is incompressible and there is no fluid pressure gradient. The study introduces a novel Hamiltonian-based approach to analyze such systems, offering deeper insights into the interplay between solid incompressibility and fluid-solid coupling. A key novelty lies in the derivation of canonical equations under these constraints, enabling clearer interpretations of reversible poroelastic behavior. However, the framework assumes perfectly drained conditions and neglects dissipative effects, which limits its applicability to fully realistic scenarios involving energy loss or complex fluid dynamics. Despite this limitation, the work provides a foundational step toward understanding constrained poroelastic systems and paves the way for future extensions to more general cases, including dissipative and nonlinear regimes.

</details>


### [108] [When light travels with infinite speed: the electromagnetic loop circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05606)
*Graeme W Milton,Pierre Seppecher*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文在光速无穷大（真空介电常数与磁导率均为零）的理想极限下，构造了一类新型电磁电路——EM-loop电路，并证明其响应由一类新的对偶Herglotz函数（dual Herglotz functions）完全表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究出发点在于考察光速无穷大这一理想极限下的电磁行为，该极限下真空不支持磁场与位移场，从而可构造以高介电常数导线（E-wires）与高磁导率导线（M-wires）为基础的非常规电路结构。

Method: EM-loop电路由E-wire环与M-wire环通过线圈（coils）穿接构成，部分为终端环路（terminal loops），受外部施加的磁通量或位移场激励；通过从方程中消去内部环路（internal loops）的场，推导终端响应的解析表达。

Result: 响应函数由一类新的对偶Herglotz函数对（dual Herglotz functions）支配；通过对称为基本（fundamental）的一类特殊EM-loop电路进行叠加，可实现任意一对对偶Herglotz函数。

Conclusion: 由此获得EM-loop电路响应函数的完整表征（complete characterization），即任意对偶Herglotz函数对均可由基本EM-loop电路的叠加实现。

Abstract: Considering the ideal limit where the speed of light is infinite, or more precisely where the permittivity and permeability of the vaccuum are both zero, we construct a new type of electromagnetic circuit that we name an EM-loop circuit. These consist of wires having very high permittivity (E-wires) and wires having very high permeability (M-wires). These are threaded together by coils, the E-wire loops and M-wire loops. Some of the loops are terminal loops, pierced by an applied magnetic flux (for E-wire loops) or an applied displacement field (for M-wire loops) that are outside the vacuum since it does not support these fields. The response is the net flux of displacement field through a cross section of each E-wire terminal loop and the net magnetic flux through a cross section of each terminal M-wire loop. To calculate this response, the fields at the internal loops (those that are not terminal loops) are eliminated from the equations.It turns out that the response is governed by a new class of function pairs - dual Herglotz functions. Superpositions of a special class of EM-loop circuits, termed fundamental, allow us to realize any pair of dual Herglotz functions. Thus, we have obtained a complete characterization of the response functions of EM-loop circuits.

</details>


### [109] [A liquid vapor phase transition in quantum statistical mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05641)
*Francesco Baffioni,Tobias Kuna,Immacolata Merola,Errico Presutti*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了连续空间中一类量子粒子系统存在液-气相变（liquid-vapor phase transition），该系统为Lebowitz–Mazel–Presutti点粒子模型的玻尔兹曼统计（Boltzmann statistics）量子版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将已有经典连续点粒子模型（Lebowitz–Mazel–Presutti模型）中已知的液-气相变结论推广至量子框架，考察量子效应与玻尔兹曼统计下相变是否依然成立。

Method: 方法上，研究对象为连续空间（continuum）中的量子多粒子系统，采用玻尔兹曼统计（Boltzmann statistics），即以经典Lebowitz–Mazel–Presutti点粒子模型为出发点的量子对应版本，并据此进行相变证明。

Result: 摘要给出的核心结果为：该量子连续粒子系统中液-气相变（liquid-vapor phase transition）得到严格证明。

Conclusion: 结论表明，Lebowitz–Mazel–Presutti模型所刻画的液-气相变在玻尔兹曼统计的量子版本中依然成立，从而将经典结果拓展到量子连续系统情形。

Abstract: We prove a liquid vapor phase transition for a quantum system of particles in the continuum. The system is the quantum version with Boltzmann statistics of the point particles model introduced in by Lebowitz, Mazel and Presutti.

</details>


### [110] [Dynamical Signatures of Selective Gap Closing in Multi-Dimensional Monotone Quasiperiodic Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05686)
*Leonid Parnovski,Roman Shterenberg*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将多维单调准周期薛定谔算子（monotone quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators）的谱隙闭合判据转化为可执行的动力学协议，并用大规模数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱隙闭合判据此前停留在谱论层面，缺乏可直接观测的动力学判据，故需给出实验可实施的协议以在超导电路与光子平台上检验。

Method: 对非主标号（non-principal label）构造采样函数，使原点处主导本征函数权重为零；再用局部凸起（local bump）将残余权重推过零点，并考察原点至局域化中心关联子（correlator）的加权振荡时间平均高度。

Result: 该加权振荡时间平均的高度在谱隙闭合处出现极深极小值，而位点控制标号（site-control label）无特征且高出数个量级，判据稳健。

Conclusion: 该动力学签名可直接对应超导电路与光子平台，为谱隙闭合的实验探测提供可行方案。

Abstract: We translate the spectral gap-closing criterion for multi-dimensional monotone quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators into a concrete dynamical protocol and verify it by large-scale numerics. For a non-principal label we construct a sampling function whose leading eigenfunction weight at the origin vanishes; a local bump then drives the residual weight through zero. The height of a weighted oscillatory time average of the origin-to-localization-center correlator exhibits a deep minimum precisely at the closing, while the site-control label remains featureless and orders of magnitude larger. The signature is robust and maps directly onto superconducting-circuit and photonic platforms.

</details>


### [111] [Compatible Condensation and Generalized Gauging: Marked Transport and Wall Interchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06104)
*Youxuan Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在幺正 (2+1) 维缺陷实现中，给出了与指定可分离广义 gauging 操作相容的凝聚（condensation）形式化框架，并证明了一个包含父代数吸收的完整 Higgs 判据（Higgs criterion）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 gauging 与凝聚研究缺少对“与某个指定操作相容”的凝聚的系统刻画，尤其是当指定 unit 可吸收非平凡父任意子代数（parent anyon algebra）时，透明 unit 的强连通性（strong connectedness）只是特例。本文旨在补足这一缺口。

Method: 将相容性定义为完整凝聚代数（condensate algebra）的相干可逆穿越（coherent reversible crossing），并保留带电通道与标记凝聚界面；由复合网络导出输运凝聚、诱导操作与指定壁的交换方块（commuting square）。对连通 gauging 数据引入中心匹配（central matching）描述，并区分原始真空插入、unit 吸收后的真空分支与末相完整真空代数。

Result: 证明了包含指定 unit 所吸收父代数的完整 Higgs 判据；在非可逆子群 gauging（non-invertible subgroup gauging）的 toric-code 例子中，对固定操作分类了相容凝聚，给出同一父凝聚的两种不等价穿越并导致不同末相，并分析了一种纯凝聚操作。透明 unit 情形可回收反向凝聚输运（reverse-condensation transport）与真空重叠公式（vacuum-overlap formula），三条完整 Higgs 条件作为一般判据的推论出现。

Conclusion: 该框架将凝聚相容性统一为指定 gauging 操作下的代数穿越问题，把透明 unit 的已知结果作为特例纳入，并表明非透明 unit 吸收父代数会产生额外的末相真空算符与新末相，扩展了 Higgs 判据的适用范围。

Abstract: We formulate condensation compatible with a specified separable generalized gauging operation in a unitary (2 + 1)-dimensional defect realization. Compatibility is a coherent reversible crossing of the full condensate algebra, retaining charged channels and the marked condensation interface. The resulting composite network determines the transported condensate, the induced operation, and a commuting square of specified walls. For connected gauging data, compatibility admits a central matching description that retains the transported condensate and its algebra identification on the specified wall. The specified unit may absorb a nontrivial parent anyon algebra; strong connectedness appears only as the transparent-unit specialization. We distinguish original vacuum insertions, vacuum branches after unit absorption, and the full vacuum algebra of the final phase, and we prove a complete Higgs criterion that incorporates the parent algebra absorbed by the specified unit. In the toric-code example of a non-invertible subgroup gauging we classify the compatible condensations for the fixed operation, exhibit two inequivalent crossings of the same parent condensate that lead to different final phases, and analyse a pure condensation operation in which the specified unit absorbs the parent electric condensate and produces additional final vacuum operators. The transparent-unit case recovers reverse-condensation transport and the vacuum-overlap formula, and the three complete Higgs conditions appear as a corollary of the general criterion.

</details>


### [112] [The Quantum Stochastic Limit for Non-Markovian Driving Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06202)
*Tanner J. Reese*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将量子系统与其环境相互作用的随机极限（stochastic limit，即弱耦合极限）表述为一族参数化量子随机微分方程（quantum stochastic differential equations, QSDEs）解的极限，并在驱动过程可非马尔可夫、系统可无限维且哈密顿量无界的情形下证明收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有随机极限研究通常依赖马尔可夫驱动与较强谱假设，且仅保留在极限中被平均掉的快变哈密顿量；本文旨在放宽这些限制，使随机极限适用于非马尔可夫驱动、无限维无界系统和更一般的谱结构。

Method: 通过对驱动过程的 n 点函数（n-point functions）施加特定条件实现收敛性证明，并在玻色子福克空间（bosonic Fock space）上对湮灭、产生与规范算子（annihilation, creation, gauge operators）在充分混合条件下验证该条件；同时引入在极限中存活的慢变系统哈密顿量（slow-evolving system Hamiltonian），并放宽快变哈密顿量须为纯离散谱的要求以容许聚点。

Result: 在所设 n 点函数条件下证明参数化 QSDE 解收敛到随机极限；该条件在具充分混合性的玻色子福克空间算子类上得到验证；收敛模式为所有时间中心 n 点函数的收敛，强于随机极限中常用的半群收敛（semigroup convergence）。

Conclusion: 随机极限可在非马尔可夫驱动、无限维无界系统及含慢变哈密顿量的更一般框架下严格建立，且所得收敛强于传统半群意义下的收敛；但适用范围受 n 点函数条件与混合性假设约束。

Abstract: We express the stochastic limit (weak-coupling limit) of a quantum system interacting with its environment as the limit of solutions to a parametrized family of quantum stochastic differential equations (QSDEs). We prove the convergence of such solutions in the case where the driving processes may be non-Markovian and the system may be infinite-dimensional with unbounded Hamiltonian. We achieve this by imposing a particular condition on the n-point functions of the driving process which we verify for annihilation, creation, and gauge operators on a bosonic Fock space with sufficient mixing. Additionally, we include not only the usual fast-evolving system Hamiltonian which is averaged out in the limit, but also a slow-evolving system Hamiltonian which survives the limit. We also relax the requirement for the fast-evolving Hamiltonian to have a purely discrete spectrum allowing accumulation points under some circumstances. Moreover, our convergence mode for the processes is convergence of all time-centered n-point functions which is stronger than the typical semigroup convergence for the stochastic limit.

</details>


### [113] [Fluctuating Hydrodynamics of Quantum Fluids from the Nonlinear Fokker-Planck Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06485)
*Mingjian Gu,Zhaoyu Fei,Xiaoguang Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文以非线性 Fokker-Planck 方程（nonlinear Fokker-Planck equation, NFPE）为出发点，构建量子流体的涨落流体力学（fluctuating hydrodynamics），推导相应的随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics）与涨落定理（fluctuation theorems），并应用于费米与玻色系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为量子流体的动力学提供可实验验证的唯象描述，并检验非线性 Fokker-Planck 方程在刻画此类系统中的适用性。

Method: 从非线性 Fokker-Planck 方程出发建立量子流体的涨落流体力学框架，推导对应的随机热力学关系与涨落定理，并将该框架分别应用于理想费米气体（ideal Fermi gas）与玻色流体（bosonic fluids）。

Result: 对理想费米气体研究电导（electrical conduction），揭示电阻（resistance）正比于温度与费米面态密度（density of states at the Fermi surface）之积；对玻色流体分析表征临界慢化（critical slowing down）的弛豫时间（relaxation time），并识别出对应无相互作用、排斥相互作用与吸引相互作用三种区间的标度行为（scaling behaviors）。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子流体动力学提供了可实验验证的唯象描述，并表明非线性 Fokker-Planck 方程适用于刻画此类系统。

Abstract: Starting from the nonlinear Fokker-Planck equation (NFPE), we establish the fluctuating hydrodynamics for quantum fluids. We also investigate the corresponding stochastic thermodynamics and derive the associated fluctuation theorems. We then apply this framework to both fermionic and bosonic systems. For the ideal Fermi gas, we study the electrical conduction and reveal a resistance proportional to the product of temperature and the density of states at the Fermi surface. For bosonic fluids, we analyze the relaxation time characterizing the critical slowing down phenomenon and identify three distinct scaling behaviors corresponding to the non-interacting, repulsive interaction, and attractive interaction regimes. Overall, this work provides a experimentally verifiable phenomenological description for the dynamics of quantum fluids and demonstrates the applicability of the nonlinear Fokker-Planck equation in characterizing such systems.

</details>


### [114] [Spectral properties of twisted soft waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06558)
*Pavel Exner,David Spitzkopf*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非圆形截面扭曲直通道所描述的软波导（soft waveguide）模型，其对应带势的薛定谔算子（Schrödinger operator）。结果表明：紧支撑扭曲（compactly supported twist）使该算子变为次临界（subcritical），其性质可用 Hardy 型不等式（Hardy-type inequality）刻画；而周期扭曲（periodic twist）会抬高本质谱（essential spectrum），其局部减速还可能产生低于本质谱阈值的孤立特征值（isolated eigenvalues）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画通道几何扭曲（twist）如何影响相应薛定谔算子的谱性质，特别是次临界性与本质谱结构之间的联系。

Method: 采用的方法是将软波导建模为带势的薛定谔算子，其势对应非圆形截面的扭曲直通道，并借助 Hardy 型不等式来表述算子的次临界性质。

Result: 紧支撑扭曲使算子成为次临界，该性质以 Hardy 型不等式表达；周期扭曲则使本质谱上移，且其局部减速可能诱导出位于本质谱阈值以下的孤立特征值。

Conclusion: 扭曲的支撑结构（紧支撑与周期）对薛定谔算子的谱特性具有决定性影响：前者对应次临界性与 Hardy 型不等式，后者对应本质谱抬升及可能的孤立特征值生成。

Abstract: We discuss a model of a soft waveguide described by Schrödinger operator whose potential is a twisted straight channel of non-circular profile. We show that a compactly supported twist makes the operator subcritical, the property being expressed in terms of a Hardy-type inequality. On the other hand, a periodic twist raises the essential spectrum and its local slowdown may give rise to isolated eigenvalues below the essential spectrum threshold.

</details>


### [115] [A Counterexample to the Weak Gibbs and Regularity Conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06617)
*Marius Wesle*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个反例，否定弱吉布斯猜想（weak Gibbs conjecture）与正则性猜想（regularity conjecture）：在方格点阵上的自旋1（spin-one）平移不变最近邻相互作用的量子自旋系统中，高温强场内存在唯一的KMS态（KMS state），但它既不满足弱吉布斯性质也不满足正则性性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于KMS态的弱吉布斯性质与正则性是否存在普遍成立的一般性猜想（conjectures），本文旨在通过具体反例检验其正确性，从而界定这些猜想成立的范围。

Method: 构造一个方格点阵（square lattice）上的自旋1（spin-one）系统，其相互作用为平移不变（translation-invariant）的最近邻相互作用；在高温与强现场（on-site field）条件下分析其KMS态的结构与唯一性，并检验其是否具备弱吉布斯（weak Gibbs）与正则性（regularity）性质。

Result: 在该参数区域（高温、强现场）系统具有唯一的KMS态（KMS state），但该态不具有弱吉布斯性质（weak Gibbs property）与正则性（regularity property），从而构成对两项猜想的反例。

Conclusion: 弱吉布斯猜想与正则性猜想一般不成立；即便在相互作用简单（最近邻、平移不变）、态唯一且处于高温强场的情形下，也可能缺少上述性质，说明这些性质并非KMS态与热力学极限态的普遍特征。

Abstract: We provide a counterexample to the weak Gibbs and regularity conjectures. The example is a spin-one system on the square lattice with a translation-invariant nearest-neighbor interaction. At high temperatures and high on-site field strengths, it has a unique KMS state that does not have the weak Gibbs or regularity properties.

</details>


### [116] [Large particle number asymptotics for point-interacting bosons in the plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06669)
*Rupert L. Frank,Robert Seiringer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维（two dimensions）中 $N$ 个玻色子（bosons）经点相互作用（point interactions）耦合的 TMS 哈密顿量（TMS Hamiltonian），证明其基态能量（ground state energy）在大 $N$ 极限下呈 $-e^{CN}$ 的渐近行为，其中常数 $C$ 与 Ladyzhenskaya-Gagliardo-Nirenberg 不等式（Ladyzhenskaya-Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality）的最优常数（sharp constant）显式相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学文献曾基于平均场近似（mean-field approximation）的变体预测该指数型基态能量渐近行为，但缺乏严格数学证明；本文旨在给出该预测的严格数学论证。

Method: 以 TMS 哈密顿量描述二维点相互作用玻色子系统，通过严格分析推导大 $N$ 下基态能量的渐近展开，并借助 Ladyzhenskaya-Gagliardo-Nirenberg 不等式的最优常数确定显式常数 $C$。

Result: 证明基态能量满足 $E \sim -e^{CN}$（$N$ 充分大），且常数 $C$ 可由 Ladyzhenskaya-Gagliardo-Nirenberg 不等式的最优常数显式表达，与物理预测一致。

Conclusion: 该结果将物理学中基于平均场近似变体的启发式预测提升为严格定理，并揭示指数型基态能量增长率与 Ladyzhenskaya-Gagliardo-Nirenberg 最优常数之间的定量联系。

Abstract: We consider $N$ bosons in two dimensions interacting via point interactions, modeled by the TMS Hamiltonian. We prove that their ground state energy has the asymptotic behavior $-e^{C N }$ for large $N$, for an explicit constant $C$ related to the sharp constant in the Ladyzhenskaya-Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality. Such asymptotic behavior was predicted in the physics literature, based on a variant of the mean-field approximation.

</details>


### [117] [Concavity of Normalized Generalized Brézin--Gross--Witten Correlators and Its Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06675)
*Chenglang Yang,Xuhui Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文借助 BKP 可积性（BKP integrability）研究推广的 Brézin–Gross–Witten（generalized BGW）模型，证明归一化广义 BGW 关联函数在固定亏格与固定总次数下严格凹，并由此得到严格 Schur 凹性、上下界及普通 BGW 关联函数的严格单调性，从而解决 Guo–Norbury–Yang–Zagier 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广的 BGW 模型关联函数的结构性质（如凹性、单调性）此前缺乏严格论证，且 Guo–Norbury–Yang–Zagier 提出的关于普通 BGW 关联函数单调性的猜想尚未解决。

Method: 利用 BKP 可积性（BKP integrability），基于 Wang 与第一作者给出的 BKP 层级 tau 函数连通 n 点函数（connected n-point functions）公式，对归一化关联函数进行分析。

Result: 证明对固定亏格（genus）与固定总次数（total degree），归一化广义 BGW 关联函数严格凹（strictly concave）；并建立严格 Schur 凹性（strict Schur-concavity）及关联函数的上下界（upper and lower bounds）。

Conclusion: 进一步证明归一化普通 BGW 关联函数具有严格单调性（strict monotonicity），从而解决了 Guo–Norbury–Yang–Zagier 提出的猜想。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the generalized Brézin--Gross--Witten model using its BKP integrability. By virtue of the formula for connected $n$-point functions of tau-functions of the BKP hierarchy obtained by Wang and the first-named author, we prove that the normalized generalized BGW correlators are strictly concave for fixed genus and fixed total degree. As applications, we establish the strict Schur-concavity and derive upper and lower bounds for these correlators. We also prove the strict monotonicity of normalized ordinary BGW correlators, which resolves a conjecture by Guo--Norbury--Yang--Zagier.

</details>


### [118] [A local Yang-Baxter obstruction for the hard-square model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06780)
*Gabriel Ortega,Eliecer Peña-Ancavil*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究硬方块模型（hard-square model）能否沿Yang-Baxter方程（Yang-Baxter equation）的解析权重族（analytic family of weights）路径精确求解。作者刻画了不含隐藏高度（hidden heights）的硬方块高度字母表（height alphabets），证明恰有两个连通情形，并证明在非零活度下二者均不存在穿过硬方块的解析Yang-Baxter面权重族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维格点模型（two-dimensional lattice models）的许多精确解依赖于满足Yang-Baxter方程的解析权重族，硬六边形模型（hard-hexagon model）即由此求解，而相邻的硬方块模型尚无已知闭式解（closed form）。因此有必要考察硬方块模型能否纳入同一求解框架。

Method: 确定编码硬方块且不含隐藏高度的高度字母表，结果恰有两个连通情形：占据变量（occupation variables）本身，以及Andrews-Baxter-Forrester的四高度。随后检验是否存在经过硬方块的解析Yang-Baxter面权重（face weights）族。

Result: 硬六边形在两种编码上均可积（integrable），但在非零活度（nonzero activity）下，两种编码上都不存在穿过硬方块的解析Yang-Baxter面权重族。原因在于排除规则（exclusion rule）的差异：对硬方块而言，若干Yang-Baxter方程化为多项式恒等式（polynomial identities），迫使辅助权重（auxiliary weights）为零。

Conclusion: 硬方块模型的闭式解并未被排除，但沿此路径的求解必须离开这些编码，或超越穿过该模型的单一解析权重族。含隐藏高度的编码仍有待研究。

Abstract: Many exact solutions of two-dimensional lattice models rest on an analytic family of weights satisfying the Yang-Baxter equation. The hard-hexagon model was solved in this way, while the neighbouring hard-square model has no known closed form. We determine the height alphabets that encode hard squares without hidden heights, and there are exactly two connected ones, the occupation variables themselves and the four heights of Andrews, Baxter and Forrester. Hard hexagons is integrable on both, but no analytic Yang-Baxter family of face weights passes through hard squares at nonzero activity on either. The difference lies in the exclusion rule, which for hard squares turns a few Yang-Baxter equations into polynomial identities that force auxiliary weights to vanish. A closed form for hard squares is not excluded, but a solution by this route would have to leave these encodings or go beyond a single analytic family through the model. Encodings with hidden heights remain open.

</details>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Hyperbolic Nanocavities for Single-Molecule Vibrational Strong Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06240)
*Stavros Athanasiou,Olivier J. F. Martin,Francisco J. García-Vidal,Johannes Feist*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上证明，在横向限制双曲声子极化激元（hyperbolic phonon polaritons, HPhPs）的六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）纳米腔中可实现单分子振动强耦合（single-molecule vibrational strong coupling）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hBN中HPhPs可在中红外实现极端亚衍射场限制，分子集合体与HPhP的集体振动强耦合已在扩展平板中实现，但单分子尺度仍具挑战；实现单分子强耦合可为研究真空电磁场对分子动力学与化学反应性的影响提供平台。

Method: 结合全波电磁仿真（full-wave electromagnetic simulations）与少模量子化方法（few-mode quantization approach），分析hBN纳米腔中横向限制的HPhP共振特性，并用HPhP射线轨迹（ray trajectories）解释共振频率及其随腔纵横比的调谐。

Result: 纳米腔支持丰富的非对称共振，显著增强局部谱密度；识别出品质因子（quality factor）约600、有效模式体积（effective mode volume）|V_eff| ~ 10^-7 λ^3 的共振，足以与偶极矩约1 D的振动跃迁实现单分子强耦合。

Conclusion: 双曲纳米腔（hyperbolic nanocavities）是单分子振动强耦合的有前景平台，并为振动动力学的局域调控与分子能量流动开辟新途径。

Abstract: Hyperbolic phonon polaritons (HPhPs) in low-loss anisotropic materials such as hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) provide extreme sub-diffractional field confinement in the mid-infrared, making them attractive for enhancing the interaction of light with molecular vibrations. Collective vibrational strong coupling between molecular ensembles and HPhPs has already been demonstrated in extended slabs. Reaching this regime with a single molecule, however, remains a major challenge, and would provide a platform for investigating how vacuum electromagnetic fields influence molecular dynamics and chemical reactivity. Here, we show theoretically that single-molecule vibrational strong coupling can be reached in hBN nanocavities that laterally confine HPhPs within a finite volume. Combining full-wave electromagnetic simulations with the few-mode quantization approach, we find that these nanocavities support a rich set of asymmetric resonances that strongly enhance the local spectral density, with frequencies that follow from simple HPhP ray trajectories and can be tuned through the cavity aspect ratio. We identify resonances with quality factors of about $600$ and effective mode volumes of $|V_{\mathrm{eff}}| \sim 10^{-7}λ^{3}$, sufficient for single-molecule strong coupling with vibrational transitions with dipole moments on the order of $1\,\mathrm{D}$. These results establish hyperbolic nanocavities as a promising platform for single-molecule vibrational strong coupling and open new routes toward the local control of vibrational dynamics and molecular energy flow.

</details>


### [120] [Directional Control of Chiral Polymer Emission Dissymmetry by Plasmonic Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06400)
*Debapriya Pal,Louis Minion,David Reger,Jessica Wade,Matthew J. Fuchter,A. Femius Koenderink*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本研究首次将有机手性聚合物薄膜（organic chiral polymer thin films）与平面手性等离激元超表面（planar chiral plasmonic metasurfaces）集成，实现对手性发光方向性、强度与手性的协同调控，并在固定分子立体化学条件下观测到随方向反转的发光手性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆偏振（circularly polarized, CP）发光在下一代光子与光电子技术中至关重要，但同时控制其方向性、强度与手性仍是主要挑战；传统薄膜架构对CP发光的角分布控制有限，且手性发光常强烈依赖角度，而超表面研究多集中于非手性发光体。

Method: 将有机手性聚合物薄膜与平面手性等离激元超表面（chiral plasmonic metasurfaces）相结合，并采用角分辨斯托克斯傅里叶偏振测量（angle-resolved Stokes Fourier polarimetry）表征手性发光的方向性与角依赖特性。

Result: 超表面耦合产生定向发光，近法向（near-normal）不对称因子（dissymmetry factor）超过0.7，并出现随方向反转的发光手性（emission handedness），且分子立体化学保持固定。

Conclusion: 手性聚合物薄膜与手性等离激元超表面的集成可增强手性光-物质相互作用（chiral light-matter interactions），并同时调控手性发光的方向性与角依赖行为，为手性光源设计提供新途径。

Abstract: Circularly polarized (CP) emission is central to next-generation photonic and optoelectronic technologies, yet simultaneously controlling its directionality, intensity, and handedness remains a major challenge. Advances in molecular and materials design have enabled strong chiral light-matter interactions and large dissymmetry between opposite CP states. However, conventional thin-film architectures provide limited control over the angular distribution of CP emission and often exhibit strongly angle-dependent chiral emission. Metasurfaces, on the other hand, offer powerful control over emission directionality and polarization, but have predominantly been explored with intrinsically achiral emitters. For the first time, we integrate organic chiral polymer thin films with planar chiral plasmonic metasurfaces to enhance chiral light-matter interactions while controlling the directionality and angular dependence of chiral emission. Using angle-resolved Stokes Fourier polarimetry, we show that metasurface coupling produces directional emission, near-normal dissymmetry factors exceeding 0.7, and, remarkably, direction-dependent reversal of the emission handedness at fixed molecular stereochemistry.

</details>


### [121] [Photonic and Electronic Interaction of TMD-Hybridized ZnO Nanowire Lasers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06740)
*Moritz Willems,Edwin Eobaldt,Alexander Zaunick,Aurelia Eberhard,Maximilian Tomoscheit,Lukas Raam Jäger,Xiao Chen,Preeti Sharma,Nicolas Tancogne-Dejean,Antony George,Andrey Turchanin,Silvana Botti,Giancarlo Soavi,Carsten Ronning*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究通过将同一根 ZnO 纳米线激光器（nanowire laser）依次与 WS₂、MoS₂ 单层及不同厚度 MoS₂ 杂化，揭示过渡金属硫族化物（TMD）通过倏逝场吸收（evanescent absorption）与空穴转移（hole transfer）双重机制抑制激光发射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体纳米线激光器（semiconductor nanowire laser）是光子集成电路（photonic integrated circuits）中有前景的片上相干光源，但集成过程不可避免地使其与其他材料接触并因杂化（hybridization）而改变性能，因此需厘清纳米线与二维材料间的光子与电子耦合。

Method: 采用确定性纳米操纵（deterministic nanomanipulation）将同一根 ZnO 纳米线先与 WS₂ 单层、再与 MoS₂ 单层杂化，并扩展到双层、三层及体相 MoS₂；结合有限差分本征模（Finite-Difference Eigenmode）模拟分析倏逝场吸收，并用含界面偶极的密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算界面能带对齐。

Result: 单层杂化后激光阈值（lasing threshold）升高、高泵浦功率密度下发射被强烈猝灭（quenching）、模式包络蓝移（blueshift）；与双层、三层及体相 MoS₂ 杂化后激光完全被抑制。模拟显示 TMD 吸收部分倏逝场且吸收具波长依赖性，DFT 预测理想 ZnO(10<span style="text-decoration:underline">1</span>0)/TMD 界面为 type-I 对齐，中等程度偶极减弱时转为 type-II，两种情形下空穴均从纳米线转移至 TMD 并离开增益介质（gain medium）。

Conclusion: 倏逝吸收与空穴转移共同抑制激光发射，二者在纳米线激光器与二维材料集成时均须纳入考量。

Abstract: Semiconductor nanowire lasers are promising on-chip coherent light sources for photonic integrated circuits, but integration inevitably places them in contact with other materials, thereby changing their properties upon hybridization. In this study, we examine the photonic and electronic coupling between single ZnO nanowire lasers and TMDs. Using deterministic nanomanipulation, we first hybridized the same nanowire first with a WS$_2$ monolayer and then with a MoS$_2$ monolayer. The respective lasing threshold rises, the emission is strongly quenched at high pump power densities, and the mode envelope blueshifts. Upon hybridization with bilayer, trilayer, and bulk MoS$_2$, lasing is fully suppressed. Finite-Difference Eigenmode simulations show that the TMD absorbs part of the evanescent field. Because this absorption is wavelength dependent, it also removes modes from the low-energy flank of the envelope, which accounts for small difference between the two monolayers, while the blueshift itself follows the increase of the carrier density at threshold. Density functional theory calculations that include the interface dipole predict a type-I alignment at the ideal ZnO(10$\bar{1}$0)/TMD interface, which turns into type~II for a moderately reduced dipole, as expected for hydroxylated facets in air. In both cases, holes are transferred from the nanowire into the TMD and removed from the gain medium. Evanescent absorption and hole transfer thus jointly suppress lasing, and both must be accounted for when integrating nanowire lasers with 2D materials.

</details>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [CMS ECAL Phase-2 upgrade: characterisation and stability analysis of a three-stage low-voltage power-conversion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06807)
*Liv Anke Richter,Günther Dissertori,Werner Lustermann,Krzysztof Stachon*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文针对CMS电磁量能器桶部前端电子学三级低压电源变换系统的级联稳定性与启动冲击问题，采用奈奎斯特判据和分流注入阻抗测量进行评估，并对第三级板卡采用错峰使能方案抑制浪涌电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型强子对撞机高亮度升级（High-Luminosity upgrade）要求更换CMS电磁量能器桶部前端电子学，其三级低压电源变换系统（power-conversion system）的级联动力学（cascade dynamics）需加以表征以确保稳定运行，同时启动过程中的浪涌电流（inrush current）可能触发过流保护。

Method: 在两个级间接口处基于奈奎斯特判据（Nyquist criterion）评估三级变换器之间的相互作用；采用经校验的分流注入（shunt-injection）装置配合Bode 100频率响应分析仪（frequency-response analyser）进行带载原位阻抗测量（in-situ impedance measurement），以检验平均变换器模型（averaged converter models）的准确性；并通过开通研究定位浪涌电流来源。

Result: 在所评估的配置下两个接口均保持稳定，模型给出的距临界点最小距离分别为0.76和0.79；开通研究确定第三级（Stage 3）电容充电是触发第二级（Stage 2）过流保护的主要原因；两种第三级板卡变体通过板内与板间错峰使能变换器，使实测双塔电流低于5 A，处于8.0 A保护阈值之内。

Conclusion: 测量结果验证了平均变换器模型的有效性，表明该三级电源系统在所评估配置下具有足够的稳定裕度，且错峰使能策略可有效抑制启动浪涌电流、避免误触发过流保护。

Abstract: As part of the High-Luminosity upgrade of the Large Hadron Collider, the front-end electronics of the Compact Muon Solenoid electromagnetic calorimeter barrel are being replaced. A three-stage low-voltage power-conversion system supplies the upgraded electronics. As the cascade dynamics require characterisation to ensure stable operation, interactions between the three converter stages are assessed at their two interfaces using the Nyquist criterion. A validated shunt-injection setup using a Bode 100 frequency-response analyser provides in-situ impedance measurements under load to assess the accuracy of the averaged converter models. Results show that both interfaces are stable over the evaluated configurations, with modelled minimum distances to the critical point of 0.76 and 0.79, respectively. A turn-on study identifies capacitor charging at Stage 3 as the main cause of inrush currents triggering Stage 2 overcurrent protection. Two Stage 3 board variants stagger converter enables within and between boards, keeping the measured two-tower current below 5 A, within the 8.0 A protection threshold.

</details>
