### [123] [Exact thermal correlators at vanishing momenta and the black hole singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05473)
*Kaustubh Singhi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全息共形场论（holographic CFT）中与 AdS 黑膜（AdS black brane）对偶的标量主算子（scalar primary）的热两点与三点函数，并在零外部动量下给出对算符维度精确的闭式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于高阶热关联函数的 WKB 与鞍点（saddle-point）预测缺乏精确检验；本文旨在提供可精确计算的两点与三点函数基准，以验证这些半经典预测。

Method: 在体（bulk）中通过三次标量相互作用（cubic scalar interaction）计算树图级三点函数，并辅以二维 CFT 的直接计算进行对照验证。

Result: 零外部动量下树图结果可写成闭式且对算符维度精确，并在二维情形由直接 CFT 计算复现；其大维度展开（large-dimension expansion）包含编码从视界到黑洞奇点径向固有时（proper time）的次领头指数项。

Conclusion: 结果构成对既有 WKB 与鞍点预测的精确检验，证实了固有时特征，同时修正了所预测的渐近形式。

Abstract: We study thermal two- and three-point functions of scalar primaries in holographic CFTs dual to AdS black branes. The three-point function arises from a cubic scalar interaction in the bulk. At vanishing external momenta, the tree-level results can be written in closed form and are exact in the operator dimensions. We reproduce them through direct CFT calculations in two dimensions. Their large-dimension expansions contain subleading exponential terms encoding the radial proper time from the horizon to the black hole singularity. These results provide an exact test of earlier WKB and saddle-point predictions for higher-point thermal correlators, confirming the proper-time signature while correcting the predicted asymptotic form.

</details>


### [124] [Satisfying Freed--Witten quantization on smooth divisors in Calabi--Yau orientifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05480)
*Mudit Jain*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在带全纯 O3/O7 对合的 Calabi–Yau 三流形上，选取公共体场 $B_{2,+}^{\star}=\tfrac12[R]$ 可使每个光滑连通除子的全二维闭链格满足 Freed–Witten 周期量子化（period quantization），模去挠元；关键在于证明不变除子上 $η_D^\star=\tfrac12([R]+[D])|_D$ 的整周期性并可用 $σ$-odd 世界体通量补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个除子上的 D-膜所要求的多条 Freed–Witten 量子化条件必须由同一个体 $B$-场同时满足，而此前缺少在整体二维闭链格上统一实现的构造，故需研究公共背景的可行性与通量选取方式。

Method: 在光滑 Calabi–Yau 三流形 $X$ 与全纯 O3/O7 对合 $σ$ 下取 $B_{2,+}^{\star}=\tfrac12[R]$（$R$ 为总 O7 除子），逐除子独立选取满足 orientifold 关系的世界体通量；对集合不变除子计算 $η_D^\star$ 在 $σ$-偶、$σ$-奇及混合类上的周期并进行通量修正；对交换除子对与零通量情形给出兼容性判据（compatibility test）。

Result: $η_D^\star$ 在全部 $σ$-偶整二维类（含继承类与自有类）上周期为整数，在纯 $σ$-奇类上周期为零，故非整周期仅可能出现在既非偶也非奇的类上；选取适当 $σ$-奇世界体通量可使其全部整化。交换除子对中一侧通量量子化自动导致其 orientifold 像量子化；在 $h^{2,0}_-(D)=0$ 的不变除子（含所有光滑刚性不变除子）上所得通量为 $(1,1)$ 型并具有本原代表。当 $h^{1,1}_-(X)\leq1$ 时构造出满足全部继承相交周期的公共体背景，更大 $h^{1,1}_-(X)$ 则由二元兼容性判据判定公共平移是否存在。

Conclusion: 单一半整数体 $B$-场可统一实现除子族上的 Freed–Witten 量子化，其可行性归结为对合下类分解与通量选取的组合条件；所给判据明确了公共背景与零通量解在何种上同调条件下存在。

Abstract: Freed--Witten quantization conditions on several divisors wrapped by D-branes must be satisfied with one bulk $B$-field. On a smooth Calabi--Yau threefold $X$ with a holomorphic O3/O7 involution $σ$, we show that the common background $B_{2,+}^{\star}=\tfrac12[R]$, where $R$ is the total O7 divisor, permits Freed--Witten period quantization on the full two-cycle lattice of \emph{every smooth connected divisor}, modulo torsion. The worldvolume fluxes are chosen individually and obey the orientifold relation between each divisor and its image. The central step concerns setwise invariant divisors $D$: we prove that the bulk Freed--Witten class $η_D^\star=\tfrac12([R]+[D])|_D$ has integer periods on every $σ$-even integral two-cycle class, including inherited and autochthonous classes. Its periods on purely $σ$-odd classes vanish, so any remaining non-integer periods occur only on classes that are neither $σ$-even nor $σ$-odd. We show how to choose $σ$-odd worldvolume fluxes on these invariant divisors that make all periods integral. For exchanged divisor pairs, quantizing the flux on one member automatically quantizes its orientifold image. On invariant divisors with $h^{2,0}_-(D)=0$, the constructed flux is of type $(1,1)$ and admits a primitive representative; this includes every smooth rigid invariant divisor. We also study zero worldvolume flux on invariant divisors. For $h^{1,1}_-(X)\leq1$, we construct common bulk backgrounds satisfying every inherited intersection period on all such divisors. A binary compatibility test determines whether a common shift exists for larger $h^{1,1}_-(X)$. Non-inherited period tests then determine whether the resulting backgrounds allow zero flux on the full worldvolume lattices.

</details>


### [125] [Goldstone theory of gluon saturation in high energy QCD: From color glass to color superfluid condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05498)
*V. P. Nair,Raju Venugopalan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文通过正确处理凝聚体对整体对称性的破缺，推广了色玻璃凝聚（Color Glass Condensate, CGC）框架，指出彩色Goldstone有效场论及长时标上的"色超流（color superfluid）"新物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能Regge渐近下胶子过密导致胶子饱和，表现为尺度1/Q_S的非微扰半经典团块构型；需扩展CGC框架以刻画凝聚体引起的整体对称性破缺及其动力学。

Method: 仔细处理凝聚体对整体对称性的破缺，构造彩色Goldstone有效场论（EFT），并研究其与共线胶子模式的耦合及对静态激波散射框架的修正。

Result: 色整体子群的破缺给出Goldstone EFT，其衰变常数f_G=√N Q_S（N=O(1/α_S)为相空间占据数）；彩色Goldstone与共线胶子耦合导致凝聚体耗竭，在由f_G控制的时间尺度上改变CGC静态激波散射。

Conclusion: 在更长时标上CGC EFT表现为一种新型色超流（color superfluid）；外部探针中ω≤α_S Q_S的低频模式对超流动力学敏感，对深度非弹性散射和超相对论核碰撞有可观测影响。

Abstract: In the high energy Regge asymptotics of QCD, the rapid overpopulation of gluons leads to gluon saturation, characterized by nonperturbative semi-classical lumpy configurations of size $1/Q_S$, where $Q_S\gg Λ_{\rm QCD}$ is a dynamical semi-hard saturation scale. We discuss here a generalization of the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) framework describing this phenomenon that is obtained by carefully treating the breaking of global symmetries by the condensate. In particular, we show that the breaking of a global subgroup of color results in a Goldstone effective field theory (EFT) whose dynamics is characterized by a Goldstone decay constant $f_G = \sqrt{N} Q_S$, where $N=O(1/α_S)$ is the phase space occupancy of the condensate, which is large for $α_S(Q_S)\ll 1$. Further, the coupling of these colored Goldstones to collinear gluon modes leads to a depletion of the condensate, thereby modifying the static shockwave scattering framework of the CGC on timescales controlled by $f_G$. The CGC EFT on these longer time scales is a novel color superfluid. Low frequency modes with $ω\leq α_S Q_S$ in an external probe of the CGC are sensitive to superfluid dynamics, with interesting physical consequences for deeply inelastic scattering and ultrarelativistic nuclear collisions.

</details>


### [126] [Schwinger Effect with a Transversal Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05530)
*Santiago Christiansen Murguizur,Daniela D'Ascanio,Pablo Pisani,Ulises Wainstein-Haimovichi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究边界对电场中粒子对产生（pair production）的影响，重点处理电场法向分量情形，并给出瞬时电脉冲在平直边界下的显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当电场相对边界存在法向分量（normal component）时，产生的粒子会与边界发生反射（reflection），使问题比纯切向场（tangential fields）情形显著复杂，需专门求解。

Method: 针对空间均匀且垂直于平直表面的瞬时电脉冲（instantaneous electric pulse），推导该情形的显式解析解，并与边界项分离。

Result: 粒子对产生包含两部分：体贡献（bulk contribution），其来源包含反射模式（reflected modes）的干涉；以及纯边界项（purely boundary term）。

Conclusion: 进一步给出由任意边界（arbitrary boundary）所限定区域内、无旋电脉冲（irrotational electric pulse）所致总粒子产生量的表达式。

Abstract: We study the effect of a boundary on pair production by an electric field. In particular, we are interested in the case in which the electric field has a normal component with respect to the boundary. This is essentially more complex than pure tangential fields due to the reflection of created particles against the boundary. In this article we provide the explicit solution for the case of an instantaneous electric pulse which is spatially uniform and normal to a flat surface. We show that pair production exhibits a bulk contribution that accounts for the interference of reflected modes, in addition to a purely boundary term. To conclude, we give an expression for the total particle production due to an irrotational electric pulse in a region delimited by an arbitrary boundary.

</details>


### [127] [Complex phase diagram and thermodynamic supercriticality of five-dimensional four-charge AdS black holes in $STU-W^2U$ gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05624)
*Di Wu,Xiao-Dan Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维 $STU-W^2U$ 规范超引力中四电荷静态 AdS 黑洞在固定电荷下的复相结构与热力学超临界行为，借助解析延拓追踪 spinodal 奇点由实变复的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示临界相变与超临界热力学之间的连续联系，深化对多电荷超引力黑洞热力学相行为的理解。

Method: 固定四个物理电荷，对固定电荷热力学关系作解析延拓（analytic continuation），追踪两个实 spinodal 奇点穿越临界点后成为复共轭对，并构建复相图。

Result: 发现类范德瓦尔斯（Van der Waals-like）一阶小/大黑洞相变终止于临界点；spinodal 温度呈现特征的 3/2 近临界标度（near-critical scaling）；由复 spinodal 实温度投影得到 Widom 型跨接线（Widom-type crossover line），其随压强升高逐渐偏离实热容极大值轨迹；高压下实热容极值合并消失，而复奇点持续存在。

Conclusion: 复奇点结构为临界相变与超临界热力学之间提供了连续联系，为多电荷超引力黑洞的热力学相行为提供了新见解。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the complex phase structure and thermodynamic supercriticality of static four-charge anti-de Sitter (AdS) black holes in five-dimensional $STU-W^2U$ gauged supergravity, with all four physical electric charges held fixed. A Van der Waals-like first-order small/large black hole phase transition is found to terminate at a critical point. By analytically continuing the fixed-charge thermodynamic relations, we trace the two real spinodal singularities through criticality into a complex-conjugate pair in the supercritical region. The corresponding spinodal temperatures exhibit the characteristic $3/2$ near-critical scaling, connecting the subcritical temperature splitting with the imaginary parts of the supercritical complex temperatures. We construct the associated complex phase diagram and identify a Widom-type crossover line from the real-temperature projection of the complex spinodals. This line progressively deviates from the locus of real heat-capacity maxima as the pressure increases. At higher pressures, the real heat-capacity extrema eventually merge and disappear, while the complex spinodal singularities persist. Together, these results reveal a continuous connection between critical phase transitions and supercritical thermodynamics through complex singularity structures, providing further insight into the thermodynamic phase behavior of multicharge supergravity black holes.

</details>


### [128] [Chiral edge modes from lattice Chern-Simons theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05663)
*Gordon W. Semenoff*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明具有连续时间与空间方格点阵的 level-k Abel 格点 Chern-Simons 理论在空间柱面上量子化后，约化为精确局域于两个边界的手征边缘激发（chiral edge excitations），并携带两个相反手征、相互对易的格点 U(1) Kac-Moody 代数实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作关注格点化拓扑场论（topological field theory）在非平凡空间拓扑（柱面）上的量子化行为，特别是其边缘自由度（edge degrees of freedom）能否在格点框架下被精确描述并与连续理论预期一致。

Method: 研究对象为连续时间、空间取方格点阵的 level-k Abel 格点 Chern-Simons 理论；在平面上与环面上该理论是拓扑的，本文将其在空间柱面上量子化，并构造相应的代数和 Fock 空间表示。非紧致情形用 Fock 空间表示，紧致情形用有限 Weyl 代数（finite Weyl algebra）表示。

Result: 量子化后理论约化为局域于两个边界的无质量手征边缘模式，分别携带相反手征的 level-k U(1) Kac-Moody 代数的格点版本，且两者相互对易；此外发现该理论对 Wilson 环（Wilson loops）存在自然的 framing，并呈现正确的 framing anomaly。

Conclusion: 结果表明格点 Chern-Simons 理论能够精确复现边缘手征代数结构与 framing 反常（framing anomaly），为格点拓扑场论中边界物理的严格处理提供了依据。

Abstract: It is shown that a level $k$ Abelian lattice Chern-Simons theory with continuum time and a spatial square lattice, which is a topological field theory on the latticized plane and torus, when quantized on a spatial cylinder reduces to chiral edge excitations, exactly localized on the two boundaries and carrying two commuting lattice versions of the level-$k$ $U(1)$ Kac-Moody algebra of opposite chirality. The algebras are represented on a Fock space in the noncompact theory and by a finite Weyl algebra in the compact one. We also observe that the theory has a natural framing of Wilson loops which exhibits the correct framing anomaly.

</details>


### [129] [Static and dynamical Casimir effects in a wedge within the worldline formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05768)
*César Daniel Fosco,Bruno Cardin Guntsche*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文运用世界线（worldline）表述研究限制在张角为α的楔形区域中、并带有可径向振荡半透明圆弧的无质量标量场，将有效作用量按圆弧位移R作幂次展开，得到楔形Casimir能与圆弧贡献的闭合形式，并推导出移动圆弧导致成对产生（pair creation）的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带可移动半透明圆弧边界的楔形区域中标量场的真空能（Casimir energy）与动力学效应，需要一个能系统处理任意强度δ势与边界位移的框架，并借此将楔形几何的已知结果与移动镜面的粒子产生问题统一起来。

Method: 采用世界线表述（worldline formulation），介质信息全部编码于非相对论点粒子传播子（propagator）中；对模拟圆弧的δ势取任意强度λ，将有效作用量按圆弧径向位移R展开至二阶，并把决定in-out有效作用量虚部的形状因子约化为涉及各角扇区径向Green函数的一圈（one-loop）表达式；电磁情形通过分解为TE与TM标量处理。

Result: 零阶得到含Dirichlet、Neumann及混合壁的楔形Casimir能，以及任意λ下圆弧贡献的闭式表达；证明世界线被积函数逐点等于曲率耦合ξ=1/4时应力量张量的能量密度，并借此（用维数正规化）重现楔形及Dirichlet圆弧封闭楔形的已知结果。二阶给出移动圆弧成对产生概率的形状因子。窄楔极限下各角扇区表现为质量由角量子化设定的场耦合于移动δ镜面；Dirichlet圆弧情形下外部区域谱趋近平面有质量镜面，内部区域类似锥形腔。此外给出理想导体、理想磁导体及混合壁楔形的电磁Casimir能。

Conclusion: 世界线方法配合传播子已知结果，可高效处理含半透明/可移动边界的楔形场论问题，并自然衔接Casimir能与动态粒子产生两类现象；该方法还可推广至电磁场以给出各类壁条件下楔形的Casimir能。

Abstract: We study a real massless scalar field in $d+1$ dimensions confined to a wedge of opening angle $α$, in the presence of a semitransparent circular arc that may oscillate radially. Within the worldline formulation, and for an arbitrary strength $λ$ of the $δ$-potential that models the arc, we expand the effective action in powers of the radial displacement $R$ of the arc. In this formulation, all the information about the media is contained in the propagator of a non-relativistic point particle, so that known results for that propagator can be used directly. At zeroth order we obtain the Casimir energy of the wedge, with Dirichlet, Neumann or mixed walls, and the contribution of the arc in closed form for arbitrary $λ$. We show that the worldline integrand is, point by point, the energy density obtained from the energy-momentum tensor of the scalar field with curvature coupling $ξ=1/4$; with this identification, and using dimensional regularization for the integrated quantities, we recover the known results for the wedge and for the wedge closed by a Dirichlet arc. At second order in $R$, we reduce the form factor that determines the imaginary part of the in-out effective action, and hence the probability of pair creation by the moving arc, to a one-loop expression involving the radial Green's functions of each angular sector. In the narrow-wedge limit, each angular sector behaves as a field with a mass set by the angular quantization, coupled to a moving $δ$-mirror; for a Dirichlet arc, the pair creation spectrum of the exterior region approaches that of a planar massive mirror, while the interior region behaves as a tapered cavity. Finally, by decomposing the electromagnetic field into TE and TM scalars, we obtain the Casimir energy of wedges with perfectly conducting, perfectly permeable and mixed walls.

</details>


### [130] [Partially Massless Condensates and Reentrant NUT/Bolt Phase Transitions in Conformal Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05787)
*Cristóbal Corral,Felipe Diaz,Borja Diez,Eleftherios Papantonopoulos,Per Sundell*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究在弱化AdS边界条件下非爱因斯坦（non-Einstein）Bach-flat Taub-NUT/Bolt瞬子，发现其可具有非零的部分无质量响应并对偶于带涡量的完美流体，同时显著改变热力学相结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作主要限于爱因斯坦情形下的Taub-NUT/Bolt瞬子；本文旨在将分析推广到非爱因斯坦、Bach-flat 背景，以考察Fefferman–Graham展开次领头阶系数所带来的部分无质量响应（partially massless response）及其全息与热力学后果。

Method: 在弱化AdS边界条件下构造非爱因斯坦Bach-flat Taub-NUT/Bolt瞬子，其中Fefferman–Graham展开（Fefferman–Graham expansion）的次领头阶系数非零并作为对偶共形场论中部分无质量响应的源；同时分析全息应力张量（holographic stress tensor）、热力学相结构，并研究共形耦合试探标量（conformally coupled probe scalar）在正则Bach-flat NUT背景上的负模。

Result: 发现非爱因斯坦Taub-Bolt分支可在零源下支持非零的部分无质量响应，并保持squashed共形边界的全部对称性；其全息应力张量仍对应带涡量（vorticity）的完美流体（perfect fluid）。额外全息自由度使常规NUT/Bolt相变消失或转为重入相变（reentrant transitions），且非爱因斯坦Bolt可具有负的热力学体积（thermodynamic volume）。

Conclusion: 非爱因斯坦Bach-flat瞬子拓展了Taub-NUT/Bolt全息物理的图景：部分无质量响应与额外全息自由度丰富了热力学相结构，并允许负热力学体积等新现象；同时正则Bach-flat NUT背景上共形耦合试探标量不存在负模。

Abstract: We study non-Einstein Bach-flat Taub-NUT/Bolt instantons with weakened AdS boundary conditions. In particular, the next-to-leading-order coefficient in the Fefferman--Graham expansion is nonvanishing, and it sources the partially massless response in the dual conformal field theory. As in the Einstein case, the holographic stress tensor of these instantons represents a perfect fluid with vorticity. Remarkably, we identify a non-Einstein Taub-Bolt branch supporting a nonvanishing partially massless response at zero source, while preserving the full symmetry of the squashed conformal boundary. The additional holographic degrees of freedom enrich the thermodynamic phase structure, allowing the conventional NUT/Bolt transition to disappear or give way to reentrant transitions. We further show that non-Einstein Bolts can possess negative thermodynamic volume and establish the absence of negative modes of a conformally coupled probe scalar on regular Bach-flat NUT backgrounds.

</details>


### [131] [Gluon and Faddeev--Popov ghost propagators in rotating background: An eigenfunction approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05789)
*Benjamín García-Contreras,Luis A. Hernández,R. Zamora*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在费曼规范（Feynman gauge）下，用本征函数方法构建了刚性旋转柱坐标系中的自由胶子与Faddeev–Popov鬼场传播子（propagator），并给出无边界与有限半径圆柱两种情形下的谱表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在旋转几何（rotating geometries）中开展微扰QCD计算提供自洽框架，需先获得旋转坐标系下胶子与鬼场传播子的显式构造，以保留角动量、极化与边界效应。

Method: 从二次规范固定QCD作用量出发导出动力学算符，采用本征函数方法与本征谱表示；胶子场用圆极化基（circular polarization basis）对角化矢量波动算符，并在满足因果条件|Ω|R<1的有限半径圆柱上对胶子施加相对边界条件（relative boundary conditions）、对鬼场施加Dirichlet条件。

Result: 胶子传播子出现极化相关的径向Bessel函数阶数移动；无径向边界时显式恢复惯性笛卡尔坐标下的标准Minkowski传播子，表明柱基下非对角张量分量源于几何因素；有限圆柱下获得含Dirichlet与Neumann本征值的离散横谱及传播子谱表示。

Conclusion: 该工作为旋转几何中的微扰QCD计算（如关联函数与辐射修正研究）提供了保留角动量、极化与边界效应的自洽基础框架。

Abstract: We construct the free gluon and Faddeev--Popov ghost propagators in rigidly rotating cylindrical coordinates using an eigenfunction approach within the Feynman gauge. Starting from the quadratic gauge-fixed QCD action, we derive the corresponding kinetic operators and obtain their spectral representations. For the gluon field, a circular polarization basis diagonalizes the vector wave operator, leading to polarization-dependent shifts in the orders of the radial Bessel functions. In the absence of radial boundaries, we explicitly recover the standard Minkowski propagator in inertial Cartesian coordinates, showing that the off-diagonal tensor components in the cylindrical basis have a geometrical origin. We then extend the construction to a finite-radius cylinder satisfying the causality condition $|Ω|R<1$, imposing relative boundary conditions on the gluon field and Dirichlet conditions on the ghosts. The resulting discrete transverse spectra involve both Dirichlet and Neumann eigenvalues for the gluon modes, from which we obtain explicit spectral representations of the propagators. Our results provide a consistent framework for perturbative QCD calculations in rotating geometries, retaining the angular-momentum, polarization, and boundary effects relevant to future studies of correlation functions and radiative corrections.

</details>


### [132] [On phases of 4d $Spin/SO$ Yang-Mills theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06058)
*Ryo Hamachika,Takahiro Nishinaka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用混合规范理论（mixed gauge theory）技术，给出了 $Spin(N)/SO(N)_\pm$ Yang-Mills 理论在低温下随 $\theta$-参数变化的相图，并证明其与中心/CP 混合反常（center/CP mixed anomaly）一致，且与软破缺超对称 Yang-Mills 的相图完全吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $Spin(N)/SO(N)_\pm$ Yang-Mills 理论低温柔性相图的动机在于检验中心/CP 混合反常（center/CP mixed anomaly）对 $\theta$-依赖相结构的约束，并与软破缺超对称理论的结果进行对照。

Method: 采用混合规范理论（mixed gauge theory）技术，对偶数 $N\geq 4$ 的 $Spin(N)/SO(N)_\pm$ Yang-Mills 理论构造与中心/CP 混合反常相一致的低温 $\theta$-相图。

Result: 所得相图与由小 gaugino 质量软破缺的 $Spin(N)/SO(N)_\pm$ 超 Yang-Mills 理论相图完全一致；对于 $Spin(4k)$（$k\geq2$）仅出现两个相，而 $Spin(4)$ 情形包含受 $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ 中心对称性（center symmetry）保护的四个 SPT 相；对于 $Spin(4k+2)$（$k$ 为正整数），相图包含受 $\mathbb{Z}_4$ 中心对称性保护的四个 SPT 相。

Conclusion: 低温柔性相图按 $N$ 的模 4 结构分类：$Spin(4k)$ 与 $Spin(4k+2)$ 分别呈现不同的相数与中心对称性保护的 SPT 相，且整体结果与混合反常及软破缺超对称对应一致。

Abstract: We study the low-temperature phase diagrams of $Spin(N)/SO(N)_\pm$ Yang-Mills theories as functions of the $θ$-parameter. In particular, for every even integer $N\geq 4$, we find a phase diagram consistent with the center/CP mixed anomaly, via the technique of mixed gauge theory. The resulting phase diagrams are in perfect agreement with the phase diagrams of $Spin(N)/SO(N)_\pm$ super Yang-Mills theory softly broken by a small gaugino mass. In the case of $Spin(4k)$ Yang-Mills for an integer $k\geq 2$, only two phases appear in the phase diagram, while in the $Spin(4)$ case the phase diagram contains four SPT phases protected by the $\mathbb{Z}_2\times \mathbb{Z}_2$ center symmetry. In the case of $Spin(4k+2)$ Yang-Mills for a positive integer $k$, the phase diagram contains four SPT phases protected by the $\mathbb{Z}_4$ center symmetry.

</details>


### [133] [Spectral flow in AdS$\mathbf{_3}$ revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06295)
*Nathan McStay,Ronald Reid-Edwards,Sean Seet*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视谱流（spectral flow）与 worldsheet 上 \(G\)-丛（\(G\)-bundles）相关的旧观点，提出通过线缺陷网络（network of line defects）系统计算 WZW 模型中谱流顶点算子关联函数的新方法，并在 \(SL(2,\mathbb{R})\) 情形下将树级 \(n\) 点函数化为 \((n+1)\) 点函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有观点认为 WZW 模型中的谱流可与 worldsheet 上的 \(G\)-丛相联系，但缺乏系统计算谱流顶点算子关联函数的方法；同时 \(SL(2,\mathbb{R})\) WZW 模型（与 AdS\(_3\) 弦理论相关）尚未被完全求解，需要推进其求解进程。

Method: 将谱流与 \(G\)-丛（\(G\)-bundles）关联，通过系统计算线缺陷网络（network of line defects）的贡献来计算群 \(G\)、能级 \(k\) 的 WZW 模型中谱流顶点算子的关联函数；并针对 \(G=SL(2,\mathbb{R})\)，在树级（tree level）将一般 \(n\) 点函数改写为 \(n\) 个未谱流顶点算子加一个退化插入（degenerate insertion）的 \((n+1)\) 点函数。

Result: 对 \(SL(2,\mathbb{R})\)，树级谱流顶点算子的 \(n\) 点函数可解释为瞬子背景（instanton background）中的未谱流 \(n\) 点函数；该 \((n+1)\) 点函数在特定时空坐标选取下满足与未谱流 \(n\) 点函数相同的整体时空共形不变性（global spacetime conformal invariance）约束，且结果与文献中的 bootstrap 结果一致。

Conclusion: 作者猜想该 \((n+1)\) 点函数可重写为未谱流算子的 \(n\) 点函数，并给出支持性证据；该工作为完全求解 \(SL(2,\mathbb{R})\) WZW 模型提供了下一步。

Abstract: We revisit an old idea that spectral flow in WZW models can be associated with certain $G$-bundles over a worldsheet. This allows us to introduce a novel method for computing correlators of spectrally-flowed vertex operators in WZW models with group $G$ and level $k$, by systematically computing the contribution from a network of line defects. Specialising to the case of $G=SL(2, \mathbb{R})$, as is relevant to AdS$_3$ string theory, we demonstrate that generic $n$-point functions of spectrally-flowed vertex operators at tree level can be rewritten in terms of an $(n+1)$-point function of $n$ unflowed vertex operators and one degenerate insertion. This is to be interpreted as an unflowed $n$-point function in an instanton background. Moreover, we conjecture that this $(n+1)$-point function can be recast as an $n$-point function of unflowed operators and we provide evidence for this. We demonstrate that the $(n+1)$-point function satisfies the same constraints from global spacetime conformal invariance as does an unflowed $n$-point function for a specific choice of spacetime coordinates that we determine. We verify that our results agree with bootstrap results in the literature. This provides the next step towards fully solving the $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ WZW model.

</details>


### [134] [A dynamical Einstein-Proca-dilaton holographic model and exotic hybrid meson resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06411)
*Bruno Toniato,David Dudal,Hudson Almeida,Roldao da Rocha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了全动力学（fully dynamical）的 Einstein-Proca-dilaton（EPD）全息模型（holographic model），用于描述 $J^{PC}=1^{-+}$ 混合态介子（hybrid meson），并通过引入体（bulk）中与混合态流对偶的大质量矢量场的反作用（backreaction），得到自洽引力背景及谱学与有限温度性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 AdS/QCD 研究多采用非反作用（non-backreacted）的固定背景，可能无法准确刻画奇异混合态介子的谱学与介质内行为；本文旨在通过包含反作用的全动力学方法提升与实验及有限温度物理的一致性。

Method: 求解耦合的 EPD 系统以获得自洽引力背景与混合态共振质量谱（mass spectrum）；软墙极限（soft-wall limit）用于对照线性 Regge 行为（linear Regge behavior），并通过黑洞热力学（black hole thermodynamics）与谱函数（spectral functions）研究禁闭-解禁闭相变（confinement-deconfinement transition）附近的有限温度区域，将 Hawking-Page 温度固定为 $T_c \sim 150\,\mathrm{MeV}$。

Result: 软墙极限重现线性 Regge 行为；引入反作用后给出定量移动（quantitative shifts），改善与实验候选态的吻合。有限温度下，与非反作用模型不同，动力学背景预测在解禁闭起始附近即出现混合态的强烈抑制（strong suppression）。

Conclusion: 结果表明引力反作用对奇异混合态介子的真空谱学（vacuum spectroscopy）与介质内性质（in-medium properties）均有可观测影响，凸显了 AdS/QCD 中采用动力学自洽方法（dynamical self-consistent approach）的必要性。

Abstract: A fully dynamical Einstein-Proca-dilaton (EPD) holographic model is developed for the exotic $J^{PC}=1^{-+}$ hybrid meson, incorporating the backreaction of the massive vector field dual to the hybrid current in the bulk. Solving the coupled EPD system yields a self-consistent gravitational background and the mass spectrum of hybrid resonances. While the soft-wall limit reproduces linear Regge behavior, the inclusion of backreaction yields quantitative shifts that improve agreement with experimental candidates. The finite-temperature regime is approached by studying black hole thermodynamics and spectral functions across the confinement-deconfinement transition, fixing the Hawking-Page temperature to $T_c \sim 150 \, \mathrm{MeV}$. Unlike non-backreacted AdS/QCD models, the dynamical background predicts a strong suppression of hybrid states already near the onset of deconfinement. These results demonstrate that gravitational backreaction has measurable consequences for both vacuum spectroscopy and in-medium properties of exotic hybrid mesons, emphasizing the relevance of a dynamical self-consistent approach in AdS/QCD.

</details>


### [135] [Striped phases of N=4 SYM at finite density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06421)
*Alex Buchel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在有限R荷密度下构造了强耦合N=4超对称Yang–Mills理论的空间调制相。通过在三维环面紧致化并使用非等背景荷，隔离出不稳定电荷密度模，并以STU模型中的条纹黑膜非线性分支作为其静态阈值，该分支熵高于同能量同守恒荷的均匀态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合规范理论中电荷输运不稳定性缺乏明确的终点描述，因此需要构造不稳定模演化所对应的空间调制相，以刻画该不稳定性的可能归宿。

Method: 在三维环面（three-torus）上紧致化N=4超对称Yang–Mills理论并施加非等背景荷，以隔离不稳定的电荷密度模；将该模的静态阈值作为种子，在STU模型中构造条纹黑膜（striped black branes）的非线性分支。

Result: 条纹态在与均匀分支连续连接的同时，具有高于同等能量与守恒荷下均匀态的熵，且熵超出始于调制振幅的四阶项。

Conclusion: 这些条纹黑膜解构成电荷输运不稳定性（charge-transport instability）的候选终点。

Abstract: We construct spatially modulated phases of strongly coupled $\mathcal N=4$ supersymmetric Yang--Mills theory at finite $R$-charge density. Compactification on a three-torus and unequal background charges isolate an unstable charge-density mode. Its static threshold seeds a nonlinear branch of striped black branes in the STU model. The striped states have higher entropy than homogeneous states with the same energy and conserved charges. They connect continuously to the homogeneous branch, with an entropy excess that begins at fourth order in the modulation amplitude. These solutions provide a candidate endpoint of the charge-transport instability.

</details>


### [136] [Quasi-Normal Modes and thermalization in integrable Quantum Field Theories from the Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06473)
*Zoltan Bajnok,Romuald A. Janik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种热力学贝特拟设（Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz, TBA）框架，用于描述可积量子场论（integrable quantum field theory）中的热化（thermalization）过程，并将趋近热平衡时的指数衰减与振荡解释为全息（holography）框架下黑洞微扰准正规模（quasi-normal mode, QNM）频率的类似物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可积量子场论中趋近热平衡的动力学（指数阻尼与振荡）缺乏系统描述，而全息图像中黑洞微扰的准正规模提供了可类比的直觉，据此推测存在相应的 TBA 表述。

Method: 借助全息直觉与热格林函数（thermal Green's function）的一般性质论证该 TBA 表述的存在性，并对 QNM TBA 施加非标准解析结构（nonstandard analytical structure）；通过与可积形状因子展开（form-factor expansion）比较确定其余要素，并猜想 QNM TBA 等价于对推迟两点关联函数热形状因子展开中久期项（secular terms）的全阶重求和（all-order resummation）。

Result: 将理论预言与非零温度下 Ising CFT 的 E8 形变（E8 deformation）的张量网络模拟（tensor network simulation）结果进行比较。

Conclusion: QNM TBA 框架被猜想亦可适用于描述趋向广义流体力学（Generalized Hydrodynamics, GHD）的非平衡（out-of-equilibrium）过程。

Abstract: We propose a Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz (TBA) approach for describing thermalization in integrable quantum field theories. The exponential damping and oscillations when approaching thermal equilibrium can be understood as the analogs of quasi-normal mode (QNM) frequencies of black hole perturbations within the framework of holography. Holographic intuitions and some general properties of thermal Green's functions motivate the existence of such a TBA formulation, as well as lead to the imposition of a nonstandard analytical structure on the QNM TBA, while comparison with integrable form-factor expansions fixes the remaining ingredients. We conjecture the QNM TBA to be equivalent to performing an all-order resummation of secular terms in a thermal form factor expansion of retarded 2-point correlation functions. We compare the predictions with tensor network simulations of the E8 deformation of Ising CFT at nonzero temperature. We conjecture that the QNM TBA framework should also be applicable for describing out-of-equilibrium approach to Generalized Hydrodynamics (GHD).

</details>


### [137] [Higher Integrability, Generalized Symmetries, and Partially Topological Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06507)
*Luigi Alfonsi,Leron Borsten,Mehran Jalali Farahani,Hyungrok Kim,Martin Wolf,Charles A. S. Young*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在非洛伦兹高次半全纯Chern–Simons理论中，建立高阶Lax可积性与广义对称性之间的经典联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是揭示高阶Lax可积性（higher Lax integrability）与广义对称性（generalized symmetries）在非洛伦兹（non-Lorentzian）高次规范理论中的经典对应关系。

Method: 在 $M\times\mathbb CP^1$ 上的非洛伦兹高次半全纯Chern–Simons理论中，由运动方程（equations of motion）得到 $M$ 上谱依赖的平坦高次联络（spectrally dependent flat higher connection），其Laurent系数组织为无穷守恒量层级；并构造与Lax联络相关的谱族经典 $(d_M-2)$-形式对称线缺陷（symmetry line defects），建立保作用量形变律（action-preserving deformation law），给出规范不变探针（gauge-invariant probes）。

Result: 得到了由Lax联络Laurent系数构成的无穷守恒量层级，以及作用于规范不变探针并产生非平凡变换的谱族线缺陷与其保作用量形变律。

Conclusion: 结果表明非洛伦兹高次半全纯Chern–Simons理论中存在Lax可积性与广义对称性之间的经典联系，且该对称性可通过规范不变探针被非平凡地探测。

Abstract: We exhibit a classical link between higher Lax integrability and generalized symmetries in a non-Lorentzian higher semi-holomorphic Chern--Simons theory on $M\times\mathbb CP^1$. The equations of motion determine a spectrally dependent flat higher connection on $M$ whose Laurent coefficients organise an infinite hierarchy of conserved quantities. We construct a spectral family of classical $(d_M-2)$-form symmetry line defects associated with the Lax connection, establish an action-preserving deformation law, and exhibit suitable gauge-invariant probes on which the induced transformations act nontrivially.

</details>


### [138] [Form Factors in AdS from the Worldsheet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06679)
*Luis F. Alday,Clément Virally,Xinan Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文利用世界面自举（worldsheet bootstrap）方法，通过匹配极点结构（pole structure）确定AdS形状因子（form factors）的领头曲率修正（leading curvature corrections），并以't Hooft环与巨引力子（giant gravitons）为例给出结果，且获超对称局域化（supersymmetric localization）验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究描述闭弦（closed strings）与D膜（D-branes）散射的AdS形状因子，旨在平直空间极限（flat-space limit）附近确定其领头曲率修正，并借助CFT数据（CFT data）约束结果。

Method: 假设形状因子可表示为多重多对数函数（multiple polylogarithms）的世界面积分表示，将其极点结构与CFT数据匹配以施行世界面自举；研究对象为4d N=4 SYM中对偶于D1膜的1/2-BPS 't Hooft环与对偶于D3膜的一对巨引力子。

Result: 't Hooft环情形下自举完全确定形状因子，并通过超对称局域化的非平凡检验；巨引力子情形初始留下五个自由系数，其中一个可由局域化固定，而要求缺陷通道（defect channel）在该阶不出现新算符（operators）可固定全部五个系数，并重现局域化约束。

Conclusion: 在所加假设下得到唯一的D3膜形状因子，超对称局域化提供独立检验，表明缺陷通道无非微扰新算符的假设与局域化约束一致。

Abstract: We study AdS form factors describing closed strings scattering off D-branes. Assuming that these form factors admit worldsheet integral representations in terms of multiple polylogarithms, we determine their leading curvature corrections around the flat-space limit by matching their pole structure to CFT data. We focus on two examples of defects in 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM: a $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS 't Hooft loop, dual to a D1 brane, and a pair of giant gravitons, dual to a D3 brane. For the 't Hooft loop, the worldsheet bootstrap completely fixes the form factor, and the result passes nontrivial checks from supersymmetric localization. For the giant gravitons, the bootstrap initially leaves five free coefficients, one of which can be fixed using localization. Remarkably, imposing instead that no new operators appear in the defect channel at this order fixes all five coefficients and reproduces the localization constraint. Under this additional assumption, we therefore obtain a unique D3-brane form factor, with supersymmetric localization providing an independent check.

</details>


### [139] [5-brane webs and generalised symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06794)
*Mohammad Akhond,Federico Carta,Sung-Soo Kim,Futoshi Yagi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从5-brane网（5-brane webs）出发，提出直接判定五维量子场论1-形式对称性（1-form symmetry）的微观方案，基于D3-brane与(p,q)弦的BPS Wilson线及其通过弦连接（string junctions）、希格斯化（Higgsing）和Hanany-Witten跃迁实现的屏蔽，并推广至含O7、O5定向面（orientifold planes）的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5-brane网已能良好刻画连续0-形式对称性，但其中高阶形式对称性（higher-form symmetries）受到的关注较少，缺乏从5-brane网直接提取1-形式对称性的微观判定方法。

Method: 以D3-brane与(p,q)弦的组态表示BPS Wilson线，用弦连接、希格斯化和Hanany-Witten跃迁实现局域算符对其的屏蔽；对无定向面的网，该构造重现基于渐近7-brane荷的Smith标准形（Smith normal form）判据，并扩展至含O7^-、O7^+与O5定向面的网。

Result: 无定向面时与Smith标准形判据一致；O7^-面可将解析后的7-brane荷纳入Smith标准形，O7^+面则提出有效荷方案并在拉格朗日与非拉格朗日实例中检验；含O5面时半D3-brane组态重现Spin规范理论的中心对称性（centre symmetries）及其被物质场破缺的行为。

Conclusion: O5面系统的1-形式对称性不能仅由渐近数据确定，还可能依赖定向面附近膜组态的局域结构。

Abstract: 5-brane webs provide a powerful geometric description of five-dimensional quantum field theories, encoding data such as symmetries and dualities. Although the extraction of continuous 0-form symmetries from these systems is well understood, their higher-form symmetries have received less attention. We develop a microscopic prescription for determining 1-form symmetries directly from 5-brane webs. BPS Wilson lines are represented by configurations of D3-branes and $(p,q)$ strings, while their screening by local operators is realised through string junctions, Higgsing, and Hanany-Witten transitions. For webs without orientifold planes, this construction reproduces the Smith normal form prescription based on asymptotic 7-brane charges. We extend the analysis to webs containing O7- and O5- planes. For O7$^-$-planes, the resolved 7-brane charges can be incorporated into the Smith normal form, whereas for O7$^+$-planes we propose an effective charge prescription and test it in both Lagrangian and non-Lagrangian examples. For webs with O5-planes, configurations of half-D3-branes reproduce the centre symmetries of Spin gauge theories and their breaking by matter. Crucially, we find that the 1-form symmetry of an O5-plane system is not determined by asymptotic data alone, but can depend on the local structure of the brane configuration near the orientifold.

</details>


### [140] [Exact Splitting of Amplitudes and One-Loop Integrands: A Classical Origin of Zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06810)
*Yang Li,Laurentiu Rodina,Zhi-Zhen Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于隐零点（hidden zeros）的递推关系，可在一般运动学下重构完整的树级散射振幅与单圈被积函数，其形式为普适的精确分裂式结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有振幅构造方法多依赖特定零点或特定理论，缺少在一般运动学下同时适用于树级振幅与单圈被积函数的统一递推框架，且隐零点的物理来源尚待阐明。

Method: 构造以隐零点为核心的递推关系，将树级振幅与单圈曲面被积函数（surface integrands）统一表示为完整树级函数的乘积之和，并在立方标量理论、非线性 sigma 模型（nonlinear sigma model）及逆弦 KLT 函数（inverse string Kawai–Lewellen–Tye functions）中确立该结构；同时由运动方程推导树级分裂公式。

Result: 该递推在立方标量理论、非线性 sigma 模型与逆 KLT 函数中均成立；树级分裂公式可由运动方程导出，隐零点出现在两个流（currents）之积满足运动方程之时，偏离零点处的修正可重构完整振幅；并证明逆 KLT 是 Tr(φ³) 在保持隐零点的非导数高阶相互作用下的唯一延拓。

Conclusion: 隐零点构成树级振幅与单圈被积函数重构的普适组织原则，并唯一刻画逆 KLT 这一理论延拓，为散射振幅的递推构造提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We introduce a recursion based on hidden zeros that reconstructs complete tree level scattering amplitudes and one-loop integrands at generic kinematics. The recursion takes a universal exact-splitting form: both tree amplitudes and one-loop surface integrands are expressed as sums of products of complete tree functions, with the chosen zero conditions manifest in every term. We establish this structure for cubic scalar theory, the nonlinear sigma model, and inverse string Kawai--Lewellen--Tye (KLT) functions. We derive the tree splitting formula from the equations of motion, showing that hidden zeros arise when the product of two currents solves the equations of motion, while corrections away from the zero reconstruct the full amplitude. We further show that inverse KLT is the unique extension of $\mathrm{Tr}(φ^3)$ with higher-point non-derivative interactions that preserves hidden zeros.

</details>


### [141] [Anomaly effective actions for 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ superconformal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06842)
*Pantelis Panopoulos,Ioannis Papadimitriou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在分量形式下系统分析了四维 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超共形场论的 Wess-Zumino 反常有效作用量，通过求解 Wess-Zumino 一致性条件并在旧最小超引力框架下构造局部泛函，得到可重现共形超引力度规泛函全部反常的广义 Wess-Zumino 作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作已分别讨论陪集构造、共形代数规范化及含 U(1) 对称性的非超对称共形场论 Wess-Zumino 作用量，但四维 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超共形场论的分量形式 Wess-Zumino 反常有效作用量尚缺乏完整分析，且其与共形超引力泛函反常的关系需要澄清。

Method: 先回顾陪集构造（coset construction）、共形代数规范化及二/四维非超对称情形；随后重审 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 共形超引力（conformal supergravity）的超共形反常，求解 Wess-Zumino 一致性条件（consistency conditions）至引力微子（gravitino）二阶；再引入手征补偿多重态（chiral compensator multiplet），在不固定陪集对称性的前提下识别局部超庞加莱子代数，并据此计算一般 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超共形场论耦合共形超引力的 Wess-Zumino 作用量。

Result: 所得作用量按构造重现 Weyl、R 对称性与 S-超对称陪集反常，但其在未破缺庞加莱超对称下的变换与共形超引力泛函的对应反常不一致；存在一个陪集不变的旧最小场（old-minimal fields）局部泛函，将其加入后可得到重现共形超引力泛函全部反常的广义 Wess-Zumino 作用量。

Conclusion: 该局部泛函同时可用于确定旧最小超引力的超 Weyl 反常与 Wess-Zumino 作用量，从而给出已知超空间（superspace）Wess-Zumino 作用量的分量版本。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive analysis of the Wess-Zumino anomaly effective action of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ superconformal field theories in components. After reviewing the coset construction of the Wess-Zumino action in the presence of an unbroken subgroup, the gauging of the conformal algebra and the Wess-Zumino effective action for non-supersymmetric conformal field theories with a U(1) symmetry in two and four dimensions, we revisit the superconformal anomalies of $\mathcal{N}=1$ conformal supergravity. Solving the Wess-Zumino consistency conditions we determine both the bosonic and fermionic superconformal anomalies in a two-parameter family of schemes, to quadratic order in the gravitino. Turning on a chiral compensator multiplet and identifying a local super-Poincaré subalgebra of the conformal supergravity algebra without gauge-fixing the coset symmetries, we employ the coset construction and the superconformal anomalies in order to evaluate the Wess-Zumino action for generic $\mathcal{N}=1$ superconformal field theories coupled to conformal supergravity. By construction, this action reproduces the Weyl, R-symmetry, and S-supersymmetry coset anomalies, but its transformation under the unbroken Poincaré supersymmetry differs from the corresponding anomaly of the conformal supergravity functional. We show that there exists a local functional of the coset-invariant old-minimal fields that, when added to the Wess-Zumino action, results in a generalized Wess-Zumino action that reproduces all anomalies of the conformal supergravity functional. The same local functional enables us to determine the super-Weyl anomalies and the Wess-Zumino action of old-minimal supergravity, providing a component version of the known superspace Wess-Zumino action.

</details>


### [142] [Conformal field theory from automorphic forms on a quantum group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06907)
*Dalimil Mazac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文用非交换几何（noncommutative geometry）语言重构二维共形场论（2d CFT）：将中心荷 $c=1+6(b+b^{-1})^2>1$ 的幺正玻色型 2d CFT 等价表述为带 $\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})\times\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})$ 作用的量子测度空间（quantum measure space）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为二维共形场论建立非交换几何表述，从而沟通 Virasoro 共形自举（conformal bootstrap）与范畴化方法、冯·诺依曼代数（von Neumann algebraic）方法这两类研究进路。

Method: 引入 $\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})$ 冯·诺依曼代数量子群（von Neumann algebraic quantum group）的自守形式（automorphic forms）；将量子测度空间定义为带权（weight）的冯·诺依曼代数，并研究其上的保权作用。

Result: 在该词典下，Virasoro 准原初算子（primary operators）对应量子自守形式，其标度维数（scaling dimensions）对应量子 Laplace 算子的谱；Virasoro crossing 方程对应量子测度空间上乘法的结合性（associativity）；紧致理论对应 type III$_1$ 因子；Liouville CFT 实现为 $\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})$ 经左右平移作用于自身。

Conclusion: Virasoro 边界条件（boundary conditions）对应带单个 $\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})$ 作用的量子测度空间，边界谱离散时即紧致双曲曲面（compact hyperbolic surfaces）的非交换对应物；该框架兼容含与不含扩展手征代数（extended chiral algebras）的模型。

Abstract: We reformulate two-dimensional conformal field theory (2d CFT) in the language of noncommutative geometry. To this end, we introduce automorphic forms for the von Neumann algebraic quantum group $\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})$. A quantum measure space is a von Neumann algebra equipped with a weight. We study such spaces carrying a weight-preserving action of $\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})$. We argue that every unitary bosonic 2d CFT with central charge $c=1+6(b+b^{-1})^2>1$ is equivalent to a quantum measure space with an action of $\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})\times\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})$. In this dictionary, Virasoro primary operators are quantum automorphic forms and their scaling dimensions correspond to the spectrum of a quantum Laplace operator. The Virasoro crossing equations express associativity of multiplication on the quantum measure space. For compact theories, the algebra is a type III$_1$ factor. Liouville CFT is realized as $\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})$ acting on itself by left and right translations. Virasoro boundary conditions correspond to quantum measure spaces with an action of a single copy of $\mathrm{SL}^+_b(2,\mathbb{R})$. When the boundary spectrum is discrete, these are noncommutative counterparts of compact hyperbolic surfaces. The formulation accommodates models both with and without extended chiral algebras. It bridges the Virasoro conformal bootstrap with categorical and von Neumann algebraic approaches to 2d CFT.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Photon-Counting Readout via Electro-Optic Microwave-to-Optical Transduction for Axion Haloscopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05444)
*Jing Wu,Raphael Cervantes,Doga Murat Kurkcuoglu,Zheshen Zhang,Silvia Zorzetti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于电光微波-光转换器（electro-optic microwave-to-optical transducer）的光子计数（photon-counting）方案，用于轴子（axion）暗物质搜寻，理论模型表明在过耦合（overcoupled）微波腔下可实现相对量子极限零拍探测（quantum-limited homodyne detection）5–10倍的扫描速率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子暗物质搜寻需要兼具高灵敏度与高扫描速率（scan rate）的探测方案，以高效覆盖参数空间；而传统Sikivie型光晕镜（haloscope）在5–12 GHz范围内腔体体积缩小会严重降低可达扫描速率。

Method: 在传统Sikivie haloscope（轴子在浸于磁场的谐振腔内转换为微波光子）基础上，构建基于电光微波-光转换器的光子计数方案，并在实际实验约束下建立完整理论模型，分析最优工作区间。

Result: 模型显示最优工作区间为过耦合微波腔，以牺牲峰值转换效率（transduction efficiency）换取转换器噪声降低；采用现有器件已实现的参数并结合优化的外部耦合率（extrinsic coupling rates），扫描速率相对量子极限零拍探测提升5–10倍，而品质因子（quality factors）、耦合率与热管理（thermal management）的改进可将提升推至50倍以上。

Conclusion: 该方案在9 GHz处完成定量模型标定，并可良好适配5–12 GHz范围内的各类haloscope，表明电光光子探测（electro-optical photon detection）可作为轴子暗物质搜寻的高速探测技术。

Abstract: Axion dark matter searches require highly sensitive detection schemes with high scan rate to efficiently explore the parameter space. Building upon the conventional Sikivie haloscope, where axions convert into microwave photons inside a magnetic-field-immersed resonant cavity, we present a photon-counting scheme based on an electro-optic microwave-to-optical transducer. Through a comprehensive theoretical model under realistic experimental constraints, we find that the optimal operating regime employs an overcoupled microwave cavity, trading peak transduction efficiency for reduced transducer noise. Furthermore, combining parameters already demonstrated in existing devices, in a device with optimized extrinsic coupling rates, would yield a scan-rate enhancement of 5-10 relative to quantum-limited homodyne detection. Improvements in quality factors, coupling rates, and thermal management push this enhancement beyond 50. While our quantitative model is benchmarked at 9 GHz, the scheme adapts well to haloscopes across the 5-12 GHz range, where shrinking cavity volumes severely degrade the achievable scan rate. These results demonstrate the potential of electro-optical photon detection as a high-speed detection technique for axion dark matter searches.

</details>


### [144] [Top Spin and CP from Equivariant Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05463)
*Henning Bahl,Marco Menen,Antoine Petitjean,Tilman Plehn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出利用洛伦兹等变Transformer（Lorentz-equivariant transformer）L-GATr-slim提升强子顶夸克的极化分析能力，并通过加入赝标量通道（pseudoscalar channel）学习与解释最优可观测量及其CP性质，在部分子层次的WBF-Higgs产生与含探测器效应的顶夸克对产生中进行了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋关联（spin correlations）与CP性质的测量是精确LHC计划（precision-LHC program）中重要且具挑战性的任务，需要提升对强子顶夸克的自旋分析能力（spin analyzing power）并构建可解释的最优可观测量（optimal observables），以增强对CP性质的检验能力。

Method: 采用洛伦兹等变变换器（Lorentz-equivariant transformer）L-GATr-slim，首先用于提升强子顶夸克的自旋分析能力；随后在其架构中加入赝标量通道（pseudoscalar channel），以学习并解释最优可观测量及其CP性质。演示对象包括部分子层次（parton level）的WBF-Higgs产生，以及包含探测器效应（detector effects）的顶夸克对产生。

Result: L-GATr-slim显著提升了强子顶夸克的自旋分析能力；加入赝标量通道后，模型能够学习并解释最优可观测量及其CP性质，并在WBF-Higgs产生（部分子层次）与顶夸克对产生（含探测器效应）两类过程中得到验证。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹等变变换器结合赝标量通道，可作为开展自旋关联与CP性质测量的有效且可解释的工具，适用于精确LHC相关物理过程的建模与分析。

Abstract: Measurements of spin correlations and CP are an exciting and challenging part of the precision-LHC program. We first show how L-GATr-slim, as a Lorentz-equivariant transformer, significantly boosts the spin analyzing power for hadronic top quarks. We then show how adding a pseudoscalar channel to its architecture allows us to learn and interpret optimal observables and their CP properties. We demonstrate this for WBF-Higgs production at parton level and top pair production including detector effects.

</details>


### [145] [Indirect probes of heavy neutral leptons through the dipole portal at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05501)
*Natascia Vignaroli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究FCC-ee上马约拉纳重中性轻子（Majorana heavy neutral leptons, HNLs）规范不变偶极门户（dipole portal）的间接探针，通过圈图匹配修正计算其对Z极点可观测量的影响，并给出偶极系数量级约0.14 TeV⁻²的投影灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接探测偶极门户受限于耦合强度，而虚HNL通过双偶极相互作用圈图修正电弱规范玻色子传播与轻子耦合，可提供不依赖直接产生的间接检验途径，故需系统评估FCC-ee的间接探测能力。

Method: 在FCC-ee框架下计算由两个偶极相互作用圈图诱导的有限匹配修正（finite matching corrections）及其对Z极点观测量（Z-pole observables）的影响；对实、味对齐（flavour-aligned）耦合，局域响应由四种耦合位移组合刻画，并分析独立局域紫外相互作用（ultraviolet interactions）的可重现性与假设依赖性。

Result: 在HNL质量1 TeV、偶极主导且独立紫外系数在2倍HNL质量处取零的假设下，FCC-ee借助改进的标准模型（Standard Model）预言可探测小至0.14 TeV⁻²的等号、同号超荷与弱偶极系数；该灵敏度与HL-LHC同号双轻子（same-sign dilepton）搜寻相当，更大质量可在有效理论（effective theory）有效域内探测更小的偶极系数，辐射希格斯衰变可带来适度改进。

Conclusion: 偶极解释依赖于对独立紫外相互作用的假设，额外紫外贡献可削弱或增强灵敏度；FCC-ee的间接探针在1 TeV处与HL-LHC互补，且所用法则（FeynRules、FeynArts、UFO模型）已公开。

Abstract: We study indirect probes of the gauge-invariant dipole portal of Majorana heavy neutral leptons (HNLs) at FCC-ee. Virtual HNLs modify electroweak gauge-boson propagation and lepton couplings through loops with two dipole interactions. We calculate the resulting finite matching corrections and their effects on $Z$-pole observables. For real, flavour-aligned couplings, the local response is specified by four combinations of coupling shifts. Independent local ultraviolet interactions can reproduce all four, making the dipole interpretation dependent on assumptions about those interactions. With improved Standard Model predictions, FCC-ee can probe equal, same-sign hypercharge and weak dipole coefficients as small as $0.14\,\mathrm{TeV}^{-2}$ at an HNL mass of 1 TeV. This projection assumes dipole dominance, with independent ultraviolet coefficients set to zero at twice the HNL mass. We quantify how additional ultraviolet contributions can weaken or strengthen the sensitivity. At 1 TeV, the projected reach is competitive with HL-LHC same-sign dilepton searches for Majorana HNLs, while larger masses allow smaller dipole coefficients to be probed within the effective theory's domain of validity. Radiative Higgs decays offer a modest further improvement. The FeynRules, FeynArts and UFO models used in this analysis are publicly available at \href{https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23667622}{doi:10.5281/zenodo.23667622}.

</details>


### [146] [Proton generalized parton distributions from electromagnetic form factors: A theoretically constrained phenomenological reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05635)
*A. Capelo-Astudillo,T. Aguilar,M. Conde-Correa,A. Duenas-Vidal,P. G. Ortega,J. Segovia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出质子非极化手征偶（chiral-even）领头扭度（leading-twist）广义部分子分布（GPDs）H^q与E^q的、受理论约束的现象学重构，并在零斜度（skewness）下用中子与质子电磁形状因子标定价夸克分布，随后推广到非零斜度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个理论自洽、且可由电磁数据约束的GPD基线（elastic baseline），用于厘清哪些信息由电磁数据确定、哪些依赖额外假设，并为后续核子-共振态跃迁GPD研究提供参照。

Method: 零斜度下将价夸克分布与味依赖的指数型剖面（exponential profiles）结合，同时拟合质子与中子电磁形状因子的现象学表示；施加严格的价夸克与反常磁矩（anomalous magnetic moment）归一化、低标度条件B^{u+d}(0)=0、端点行为（endpoint behavior）以及完整的高斯碰撞参数正定性（impact-parameter positivity）约束；非零斜度通过归一化双分布（double distributions）延拓，并引入独立D项（D-term）补全Mellin多项式。

Result: 所得参数化能合理描述电磁形状因子及其斜率，同时揭示在所选拟设（ansatz）下严格GPD自洽性与低Q^2观测量精确重现之间的张力；文中检验了电磁与能量-动量张量形状因子、碰撞参数分布、正定性、多项式性（polynomiality）及若干力学性质。

Conclusion: 分析区分了电磁数据约束的信息与支配斜度及力学部分的额外假设，提供了一个透明的弹性基线，可服务于未来核子-共振态跃迁GPD的研究。

Abstract: We present a theoretically constrained phenomenological reconstruction of the unpolarized, chiral-even, leading-twist proton generalized parton distributions $H^q$ and $E^q$. At zero skewness, valence-quark distributions are combined with flavor-dependent exponential profiles calibrated simultaneously against phenomenological representations of the proton and neutron electromagnetic form factors. The construction imposes exact valence and anomalous-magnetic-moment normalizations, the low-scale condition $B^{u+d}(0)=0$, the required endpoint behavior, and the complete Gaussian impact-parameter positivity bound. The resulting parametrization provides a reasonable description of the electromagnetic form factors and their slopes while revealing the tension between strict GPD consistency and a precision reproduction of all low-$Q^2$ observables within the adopted ansatz. We extend the distributions to nonzero skewness using normalized double distributions and introduce an independent $D$-term to complete the relevant Mellin polynomials. We examine electromagnetic and energy--momentum-tensor form factors, impact-parameter distributions, positivity, polynomiality, and illustrative mechanical properties. The analysis distinguishes information constrained by electromagnetic data from additional assumptions governing skewness and the mechanical sector, thereby providing a transparent elastic baseline for future studies of nucleon-to-resonance transition GPDs.

</details>


### [147] [Born spreading with consistent shower starting scales in MC@NLO and FxFx merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06769)
*Rikkert Frederix,Paolo Torrielli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在 MadGraph5_aMC@NLO 中，通过重新构造与 Born 级量一致的事件起始标度（starting scales），解决了 MC@NLO、FxFx 合并（merging）与 MC@NLO-Δ 中 Born spreading 的偏置问题，使其得以公开发布；在 W+jets（含至多两个 NLO 喷注）的 FxFx 合并样本中，负权重比例下降且预言不变，事件生成速度提升约六倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负权重（negative weights）是 LHC 上 NLO 事件产生与部分子簇射（parton shower）匹配的主要代价，会显著增加达到给定统计精度所需的事件数；Born spreading 可降低 MC@NLO 中负权重 S 事例的比例，但其无偏性依赖特定条件，而此前 MadGraph5_aMC@NLO 的起始标度不满足该条件，阻碍了其公开发布。

Method: 论证 Born spreading 无偏以及 MC 抵消项（MC counterterms）能重现首次簇射发射，均要求传给部分子簇射的 S 事例仅依赖 Born 级量，与辐射相空间积分方式无关；据此为普通 MC@NLO、FxFx 合并与 MC@NLO-Δ 给出一致的新起始标度构造，并研究 MC 抵消项的修正形式。

Result: 新的起始标度构造满足上述条件，使 Born spreading 得以公开发布并适用于合并样本；在 FxFx 合并的 W+jets 产生（至多两个 NLO 喷注）中，Born spreading 降低合并后的负权重比例而不影响物理预言，事件样本生成速度约为原实现的六倍。

Conclusion: Born spreading 的无偏性与 MC 抵消项对首次发射的正确重现共享同一条件，即 S 事例仅依赖 Born 级量；满足该条件的一致起始标度构造使 Born spreading 可用于普通 MC@NLO 与合并样本，兼顾负权重抑制与计算效率。

Abstract: Negative weights are a major cost in NLO event generation matched to parton showers at the LHC, as they increase the number of events needed for a given statistical precision. Born spreading reduces the fraction of negative-weight $\mathbb S$ events in MC@NLO by redistributing the Born contribution over the radiation variables. It introduces no bias only if everything that is passed to the parton shower together with $\mathbb S$ events depends on Born-level quantities alone. We show that the same condition is needed for the MC counterterms to reproduce the first shower emission, independently of how the radiation phase space is integrated. In MadGraph5_aMC@NLO, the $\mathbb S$-event starting scales did not fully satisfy this condition, which so far prevented the public release of Born spreading. We present a new and consistent construction of the starting scales for ordinary MC@NLO, FxFx merging and MC@NLO-$Δ$, with which Born spreading is now publicly available, including for merged samples. We also study modified formulations of the MC counterterms, and FxFx-merged $W$+jets production with up to two jets at NLO, for which Born spreading reduces the fraction of negative weights after merging without affecting the predictions, and the event samples are generated six times faster than with the former implementation.

</details>


### [148] [Cross-Moment Consistency and the Fifth Adler Coefficient in Hadronic Tau Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06824)
*Gauhar Abbas,Debabina Biswas,Pratham Dadhich,Vartika Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出，四个已知 Adler 系数（Adler coefficients）对首个被忽略的五阶项系数 c_{5,1} 的约束能力有限；多权重检验实质等价于单一绝对容差，因此 ±24 量级的分辨率与条件性估计 248±80 均不构成对 c_{5,1} 的精确确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 inclusive hadronic τ 衰变（inclusive hadronic τ decays）的固定阶（fixed-order）分析中，未知系数 c_{5,1} 控制首个被省略的五阶项，其取值直接影响理论误差评估；本文旨在厘清仅凭四个已知 Adler 系数究竟能对该系数提供多强的检验。

Method: 证明对任意归一化多项式权重（normalized polynomial weight），固定阶 Adler-to-moment 映射（Adler-to-moment map）为对角元为单位值的三角映射；据此分析多权重相对检验（multi-weight relative test）的退化结构，并对三种代表性权重给出数值分辨率，同时与 contour-improved 检验及 Padé/Shanks 缺陷（defects）的符号约束进行比较。

Result: 三角映射意味着共同的 Adler 系数误差以相同绝对幅度进入所有矩，多权重相对检验退化为单一绝对容差；三种权重下 10% 检验仅将 c_{5,1} 分辨到约 ±24，而最接近的 contour-improved 检验弱一个数量级以上。此外，保持直接 Padé/Shanks 缺陷观测符号的任何延拓（continuation）要求 c_{5,1} ≥ 378.03，故接近 280 的取值需额外结构。

Conclusion: 应用于简化 Beneke–Jamin 型纯幂次拟设（pure-power ansatz）的校准—证伪协议（calibration-and-falsification protocol）给出条件性估计 c_{5,1}=248±30(struct)±74(method)=248±80；在物理阶数下其中心值在代数上与精确四参数拟合（four-parameter fit）一致，故该协议仅增加可证伪的可容许性检验与方法不确定度，而非新的系数信息。文末同时澄清常被引用的 Beneke–Jamin 值 283 系总宽度 FOPT 的线性外推。

Abstract: The unknown coefficient $c_{5,1}$ controls the first omitted fifth-order term in fixed-order analyses of inclusive hadronic $τ$ decays. We determine what the four known Adler coefficients can actually test. For any normalized polynomial weight, the fixed-order Adler-to-moment map is triangular with unit diagonal. Hence one common Adler-coefficient error reaches every moment with the same absolute size, and a multi-weight relative test reduces to one absolute tolerance. For three representative weights, a ten-percent test resolves $c_{5,1}$ only to about $\pm24$; the closest contour-improved test is weaker by more than an order of magnitude. Moreover, any continuation that preserves the observed sign of the direct Padé/Shanks defects gives $c_{5,1}\geq378.03$, so values near $280$ require additional structure. A calibration-and-falsification protocol applied to a simplified Beneke--Jamin-type pure-power ansatz gives the conditional estimate $c_{5,1}=248\pm30\,(\mathrm{struct})\pm74\,(\mathrm{method})=248\pm80$. At the physical order its central value coincides algebraically with the exact four-parameter fit; the protocol adds a falsifiable admissibility test and a method uncertainty, not new coefficient information. We also clarify that the often-quoted Beneke--Jamin value $283$ is a linear extrapolation of total-width FOPT te

</details>


### [149] [Parton wave functions, density matrices and entropy in DGLAP evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05432)
*S. Bondarenko,M. Ouchen,A. Prygarin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文将Kharzeev-Levin提出的部分子多重数熵（parton multiplicity entropy）与强子多重数数据进行比较，指出由部分子密度计算的熵并非唯一确定，需依赖指定分辨模式（resolved modes）。作者借助Lipatov波函数的单部分子密度矩阵（one-parton density matrix）推导两类熵，证明所谓热态熵最强只能是实际熵的上界，并给出满足Lindblad演化的DGLAP对应关系及可检验预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分子多重数熵被用于刻画深度非弹性散射（deep inelastic scattering）探测区域与质子剩余部分之间的纠缠，但其由部分子密度计算，而密度并不固定部分子的量子态（quantum state），因此该熵的物理含义与唯一性存疑，需要澄清其定义并给出可检验的表述。

Method: 由Lipatov波函数构造单部分子密度矩阵，计算具有有限平均数与熵的分辨模式对应的两种熵；讨论相容热态（thermal state）在固定单部分子矩阵下使熵最大化；并证明未记录发射平均后的态在有限多个受调控模式上服从保概率的Lindblad演化，其占据数在领先对数精度下重现含虚项的领头阶DGLAP。

Result: 由密度计算的熵仅是特定分辨模式下的最大值，即实际熵的上界，仅在特定纯态子系统下才度量纠缠；几何多重数熵对应于所有发射部分子共享同一模式的情形。在横向动量强有序、光锥规范（light-cone gauge）与稀薄占据条件下，占据数方程可由独立热 πληθ近似闭合，并出现来自算符代数的Bose与Pauli因子。

Conclusion: 提出在HERA或LHC检验该近似：将快度窗内由归一化强子阶乘矩（factorial moments）推断的模式数与同一窗口的横向模式数比较。该检验可判断基于密度的熵上界近似的有效性。

Abstract: Parton multiplicity entropy, proposed by Kharzeev and Levin as entanglement between the region probed in deep inelastic scattering and the proton's remainder, was compared with hadron multiplicity data. It is computed from parton densities, which do not fix the partons' quantum state. We show that a one-parton density matrix from Lipatov's wave functions determines two entropies for resolved modes with finite mean number and entropy. Its trace is the mean parton number, and normalization gives a sampled parton's entropy. A compatible thermal state, if it exists, maximizes entropy at fixed one-parton matrix. This maximum equals the actual entropy only for that thermal state and measures entanglement only for a specified subsystem of a pure state. The geometric multiplicity entropy follows if all emitted partons share one mode. An entropy computed from densities becomes such a maximum, an upper bound on the actual entropy, only for specified resolved modes. We also show that the state averaged over unrecorded emissions, assumed incoherent across scales, obeys probability-preserving Lindblad evolution on finitely many regulated modes with the resolution scale if emission and no-emission terms match. The occupations reproduce leading-order DGLAP, with virtual terms, at leading logarithmic accuracy as the cutoffs on momentum fractions are removed. The reduction holds for strongly ordered transverse momenta in light-cone gauge and dilute occupations, including those of newly resolved modes. Approximating momentum and helicity modes by independent thermal populations closes the occupation equation and gives this bound's evolution, with Bose and Pauli factors from the operator algebra. We propose testing this approximation at HERA or the LHC. The test compares the mode number inferred from normalized hadron factorial moments in a rapidity window with the window's transverse mode count.

</details>


### [150] [The Cosmology of Hawking Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05747)
*Abdelaziz Hussein,Gordan Krnjaic,Sarunas Verner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 原初黑洞（primordial black hole, PBH）因能量密度相对辐射增长而普遍引发早期物质主导时期，其霍金蒸发（Hawking evaporation）产生的加速类轴子粒子（axion-like particle, ALP）衰变为光子后可影响多项宇宙学观测；据此对大爆炸核合成、宇宙微波背景、大尺度结构和X射线观测量给出新限制，并指出若霍金轴子（Hawking axion）在复合时期（recombination）仍保持相对论性，将贡献ΔN_eff≃0.05–0.11，或可被未来CMB探测检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBH本质为非相对论性，其宇宙学能量占比相对辐射持续增长，即使初始丰度极小，宇宙也普遍会经历一段早期物质主导时期（early matter domination），直至PBH蒸发恢复热大爆炸；该时期产生的加速ALP群体具有可观测的宇宙学与天体物理效应，值得系统约束。

Method: 考虑PBH主导时期通过霍金蒸发产生一批加速的ALP，这些“霍金轴子”随后变为非相对论性并衰变为光子；通过其对大爆炸核合成（big bang nucleosynthesis, BBN）、宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）、大尺度结构（large-scale structure, LSS）和X射线观测量的影响，对其参数空间施加新限制，并估算其在复合时期仍相对论时对ΔN_eff的贡献。

Result: 得到该类粒子在BBN、CMB、LSS及X射线观测量上的新约束；若霍金轴子在复合时期保持相对论性，则依母体PBH质量不同，贡献ΔN_eff≃0.05–0.11，处于未来CMB探测的可观测范围。

Conclusion: PBH主导时期产生的霍金轴子可作为连接PBH物理与标准宇宙学探针的有效通道，多观测量联合限制其性质；其ΔN_eff贡献为未来CMB实验提供了潜在检验目标。

Abstract: Since primordial black holes (PBH) are always non-relativistic, their cosmological energy fraction grows relative to that of radiation. Thus, for even a tiny initial PBH abundance, the universe generically enters an epoch of early matter domination, which ends when the PBH evaporate to restore the hot big bang. We consider the possibility that a PBH dominated era yields a population of boosted axion-like particles (ALPs) produced from Hawking evaporation. Eventually these ``Hawking axions" become non-relativistic and decay to photons, which can affect various cosmological and astrophysical observables. Here we place new constraints on these particles through their impact on big bang nucleosynthesis, cosmic microwave background, large-scale structure, and X-ray observables. Furthermore, if Hawking axions remain relativistic through recombination, then depending on the progenitor PBH mass, they contribute $ΔN_{\rm eff}\simeq0.05$-$0.11$, which may be observable with future CMB probes.

</details>


### [151] [From lattice QCD thermodynamics to an effective quasiparticle description of gluonic matter at finite temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06128)
*Cheng-Gang Guo,La-Yuan Peng,Zhen-Yan Lu,Marco Ruggieri,Shu-Peng Wang,Zhi-Jun Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于格点数据构建纯SU(3)杨–米尔斯物质在有限温度下的准粒子描述，将温度依赖的有效胶子质量作为热力学参数，在保持热力学一致性的前提下重建压强、能量密度、熵密度、相互作用度量与声速平方，并研究近$T_c$的非共形行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在保持热力学一致性（thermodynamic consistency）的前提下，用格点（lattice）热力学数据刻画胶子激发谱的介质修正，并区分有效热力学参数与物理胶子质量。

Method: 采用格点约束的准粒子（quasiparticle）图像：由$0.70\le T/T_c\le5$范围的格点熵密度（entropy density）确定温度依赖的有效胶子准粒子质量，并仅用一个压强归一化条件重建背景贡献。

Result: 重建的压强、能量密度、熵密度、相互作用度量（interaction measure）与声速平方与格点结果符合；近$T_c$时有效质量快速变化，相互作用度量在$T/T_c\simeq1.09$出现显著极大值，声速平方被强烈压低，背景贡献亦达极大；高温下相互作用度量下降，声速平方趋近共形极限（conformal limit）。每激发能量比$E/n_g$在$T/T_c\simeq1.21$取得约$964~\mathrm{MeV}$的极小值。

Conclusion: 结果表明可将格点热力学映射为有效胶子激发谱（effective gluonic excitation spectrum）并保持热力学一致性，近$T_c$的相关特征刻画了转变区对共形行为的显著偏离。

Abstract: We develop a lattice-constrained quasiparticle description of pure SU(3) Yang--Mills matter at finite temperature. The temperature-dependent effective gluon quasiparticle mass is determined from the lattice entropy density over $0.70\leq T/T_c\leq5$. It is interpreted as an effective thermal parameter encoding medium modifications of gluonic excitations, rather than as a physical gluon mass. With this mass fixed, we reconstruct the background contribution required for thermodynamic consistency, using a single pressure-normalization condition. The resulting pressure, energy density, entropy density, interaction measure, and squared speed of sound are then compared with available lattice results. Near $T_c$, the rapid variation of the effective mass is accompanied by a pronounced maximum in the interaction measure at $T/T_c\simeq1.09$, a strong suppression of the squared speed of sound, and a maximum in the background contribution. These correlated features characterize the substantial departure from conformal behavior in the transition region. At higher temperatures, the interaction measure decreases and the squared speed of sound approaches the conformal limit. We also examine the energy-per-excitation ratio $E/n_g$, which has a minimum of approximately $964~\mathrm{MeV}$ at $T/T_c\simeq1.21$. This ratio provides a measure of the excitation scale within the quasiparticle description. Overall, our results show how lattice thermodynamics can be mapped onto an effective gluonic excitation spectrum while maintaining thermodynamic consistency.

</details>


### [152] [Quantum field theory treatment of the spin-flavor precession of Dirac neutrinos with diagonal magnetic moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06643)
*Maxim Dvornikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用量子场论（quantum field theory, QFT）方法描述狄拉克中微子（Dirac neutrino）的自旋-味进动（spin-flavor precession），利用精确包含磁矩相互作用的大质量中微子传播子推导了磁场下 ν_{eL}→ν_{μL} 的跃迁概率，并与标准量子力学（quantum mechanical, QM）方法的预言进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子力学方法对中微子自旋-味进动的描述可能不完备，本文旨在以量子场论框架重新处理该过程，考察磁矩相互作用对跃迁概率的影响并评估对量子力学结果的修正可能性。

Method: 将中微子质量本征态视为虚粒子，磁矩矩阵在质量基下取对角形式；整体过程（发射、传播与探测）的中微子贡献包含于大质量中微子传播子中，采用近期导出的、精确计入磁矩相互作用的传播子进行计算。

Result: 在磁场影响下推导出 ν_{eL}→ν_{μL} 过程的跃迁概率，并将其与标准量子力学方法的预言进行对比，二者结果存在可比性，差异体现量子场论修正。

Conclusion: 量子场论方法为自旋-味进动提供了系统描述，并简要讨论了由量子场论结果导出对量子力学表达式修正的可能性。

Abstract: We apply the quantum field theory (QFT) approach to describe the spin-flavor precession of Dirac neutrinos. The magnetic moments matrix of these neutrinos is taken to be diagonal in the mass basis. Neutrino mass eigenstates are considered to be virtual particles in the QFT approach. The neutrino contribution to the matrix element of the overall process, which includes the emission, the propagation and the detection of neutrinos, is contained in the propagators of massive neutrinos. Using the recently derived propagator of a massive neutrino, which exactly accounts for the magnetic moment interaction, we derive the transition probability of the process $ν_{e\mathrm{L}}\toν_{μ\mathrm{L}}$ under the influence of a magnetic field. The result is compared with the prediction of the standard quantum mechanical (QM) approach. We briefly discuss the possibility to derive the QFT corrections to the QM expressions.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [A Fully Polynomial Approximation Algorithm for the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05446)
*Dylan Herman,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Junhyung Lyle Kim,Guneykan Ozgul*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出分组式 IAMP（GEG-IAMP），首次在 N 与 1/ε 上均为多项式工作量、并具备有限维定量保证的条件下近似 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick（SK）最大能量；在维度不小于 K ε^{-2} log²(e/ε) log(K/α) 时以概率 1-α 给出 (1-ε) 近似，工作量 O(N²/ε)、存储 O(N²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作（Montanari 的增量近似消息传递 IAMP 与 AMS 的零温表述）仅在固定精度下达到 C(ε)N² 的计算量，其复杂度对精度非多项式，且缺乏有限维（finite-size）定量保证；论文旨在实现同时对 N 与 1/ε 多项式的总工作量并给出显式维度阈值。

Method: 在 AMS 相同假设下提出 GEG-IAMP：以 Reeves 的耦合构造（coupling construction）替代 Onsager 校正项，并将 Euler-Maruyama 步分组（grouping）为 O(1/ε) 次矩阵-向量乘积（matrix-vector products）；另对 AMS IAMP 给出相对保证（relative guarantee）的分析。

Result: GEG-IAMP 在 N ≥ K ε^{-2} log²(e/ε) log(K/α) 时以至少 1-α 的概率返回 (1-ε) 近似，工作量 O(N²/ε)、存储 O(N²)；对 AMS IAMP 证明在 O(N² ε^{-3} log²(e/ε)) 工作量下的相对保证，但其充分维度随精度呈指数增长。

Conclusion: GEG-IAMP 是首个在 N 与 1/ε 上多项式工作量且具有限维保证的 SK 近似算法；在低于维度阈值时返回固定配置，从而给出加性误差与维度无关的渐近完全多项式随机近似方案（AFPRAS）。

Abstract: Montanari's incremental approximate message passing (IAMP) algorithm [FOCS 2019; SIAM J. Comput., 2025] and the zero-temperature formulation of El Alaoui, Montanari and Sellke (AMS) [Ann. Probab., 2021] find a $(1-\varepsilon)$ approximation to the maximum Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (SK) energy in $C(\varepsilon)N^2$ operations for fixed accuracy, with probability tending to one as $N\to\infty$, under their respective full-support and access-model assumptions. However, it was unknown whether this approximation could be achieved with work polynomial in both $N$ and $1/\varepsilon$, together with quantitative guarantees at finite dimension. Under the same assumptions as AMS, we introduce grouped IAMP with exact Gaussian query outputs (GEG-IAMP), which is the first SK approximation algorithm with total work polynomial in $N$ and $1/\varepsilon$ and a finite-size guarantee above a dimension threshold polynomial in $1/\varepsilon$ and $\log(1/α)$. More precisely, for every $α\in(0,1/2)$, it returns a $(1-\varepsilon)$ approximation with probability at least $1-α$ whenever $N\ge \mathsf{K}\varepsilon^{-2}\log^2(e/\varepsilon)\log(\mathsf{K}/α)$, using $O(N^2/\varepsilon)$ work and $O(N^2)$ storage. GEG-IAMP replaces the Onsager correction by Reeves's coupling construction [arXiv:2508.10782] and groups Euler-Maruyama steps into $O(1/\varepsilon)$ matrix-vector products. We also prove a relative guarantee for AMS IAMP with $O(N^2\varepsilon^{-3}\log^2(e/\varepsilon))$ work but a sufficient dimension exponential in inverse accuracy. By returning a fixed configuration below its dimension threshold, GEG-IAMP also provides an asymptotic fully polynomial randomized approximation scheme (AFPRAS), with an additive error independent of dimension.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [How Rich is the Rich-Club? Super Rich-Club Organization in Complex Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06756)
*Lucía S. Ramirez,Laia Barjuan,M. Ángeles Serrano*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究联合分析富人俱乐部系数（rich-club coefficient）与富集系数（enrichment coefficient），揭示核心组织（core organization）的三种模式，并提出“超级富人俱乐部”（Super Rich-Club）组织形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富人俱乐部系数只衡量高度节点连接是否高于随机期望，无法反映其内部平均度是否高于原网络，因此需引入富集系数补充刻画核心组织的内部连通性。

Method: 作者同时计算富人俱乐部系数与富集系数，后者刻画随节点数递减而累积的高阶子图（subgraph）内部平均度是否大于原网络，并在真实网络与合成网络上应用该框架。

Result: 结果识别出三种核心组织模式：有富人俱乐部但无富集、有富集但无富人俱乐部、以及二者兼具的超级富人俱乐部组织；后者中高度节点形成内聚且异常致密的核心，支持高效通信。

Conclusion: 跨结构尺度刻画核心组织需同时考虑相对密度（relative density）与内部连通性（internal connectivity），真实系统中存在更强的核心组织形式，并影响网络结构、支配动力学过程的谱性质（spectral properties）与通信能力（communicability）。

Abstract: The rich-club coefficient is used in complex networks to quantify the tendency of progressively higher-degree nodes to be more densely connected than expected by chance. However, it does not capture whether high degree nodes towards the core are actually more interconnected than the original network. Here, we jointly analyze the rich-club coefficient and an enrichment coefficient, which quantifies whether the internal average degree of progressively higher-degree subgraphs is greater than that of the original network, despite the decreasing number of nodes. We apply this framework to real and synthetic networks. Our results reveal three distinct regimes of core organization: rich-club organization without enrichment, enrichment without rich-club organization, and a combination of both, which we call Super Rich-Club organization. In the latter regime, high-degree nodes form cohesive and unusually dense cores that support efficient communication. Our results show that characterizing core organization across structural scales requires considering both relative density and internal connectivity, revealing a stronger form of core organization in real systems with implications for network structure, spectral properties that govern dynamical processes, and communicability.

</details>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Near linear mixing of random-cluster Glauber dynamics on random regular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05794)
*Zhangsong Li*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究局部树状图（locally treelike graphs）上低温度随机簇模型（random-cluster model）的采样问题，证明在随机 d-正则图（random d-regular graph）上，对所有固定 q>2 与 p>p_s，随机簇 Glauber 动力学的混合时间为 O(n^{1+o(1)})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机簇模型推广了独立伯努利渗流（independent Bernoulli percolation）并与铁磁 Ising/Potts 自旋系统密切相关；此前对所有 p>p_s 成立的采样结果仅覆盖 q≳log d，作者希望将适用范围扩展至整个 q>2 区间以验证理论预测。

Method: 仅依赖 d-正则树上的空腔消息（cavity messages）收缩不足以覆盖全部 q>2 区域；受文献 [LS26] 启发，作者构建了精细的自举论证（bootstrap argument），证明在宏大耦合（grand coupling）下分歧（disagreement）的收缩性。

Result: 在随机 d-正则图上，对所有固定 q>2 与 p>p_s，随机簇 Glauber 动力学以 O(n^{1+o(1)}) 时间混合，从而在随机正则图上证实了该预测。

Conclusion: 该工作将全 p>p_s 区间的快速混合结果从 q≳log d 推广到所有 q>2，验证了随机正则图上低温度随机簇模型的易采样性预测；论文同时说明证明的核心思路由 GPT-6 与 Pharos 系统生成，后由作者精炼简化。

Abstract: We study the random-cluster model on locally treelike graphs at low temperatures. This is a model of dependent percolation parametrized by an edge probability $p \in (0,1)$ and a clustering weight $q \in [1,\infty)$, generalizing independent Bernoulli percolation ($q=1$) and closely related to the classical ferromagnetic Ising and Potts spin systems at integer $q$. For $q>2$, at parameter $p$ below the tree uniqueness threshold $p_{\mathsf u}(q,d)$, it is expected that sampling is easy and local Markov chains mix rapidly on all graphs with degree bounded by $d$. On typical graphs, the same is predicted at $p>p_{\mathsf s}(q,d)$, where $p_{\mathsf s}(q,d)$ is a second uniqueness transition point on the $d$-regular wired tree. Our result confirms this prediction for random regular graphs, showing that the random-cluster Glauber dynamics mix in time $O(n^{1+o(1)})$ on a random $d$-regular graph for all fixed $q>2$ and $p>p_{\mathsf s}$.
  For comparison, the best prior sampling results for these models that held for all $p>p_{\mathsf s}$ required $q\gtrsim \log d$ \cite{BGPZ26}. The key difficulty is that simply utilizing the contraction of cavity messages on $d$-regular trees does not suffice to cover the entire regime $q>2$. To address this issue, motivated by \cite{LS26}, we develop a careful bootstrap argument to show the shrinkage of disagreement under grand coupling. The core ideas of the proof were generated using GPT-6 and the Pharos system; the proof was then refined and simplified by the author.

</details>


### [156] [Gaussian free field limit for the Airy$_β$ line ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05852)
*Vadim Gorin,Lingfu Zhang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明 Airy_β 线系综的中心化高度函数在空间与时间变量重整化后，对任意 β>0 收敛于半平面上的高斯自由场（Gaussian free field, GFF）；β 的依赖仅体现为场按 √β 的标度变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Airy_β 线系综是随机矩阵与随机曲面模型中的普适对象，其大尺度涨落结构是否具有与 β 无关的共形不变性（conformal invariance）是核心问题；此前对一般 β>0 的收敛性尚缺乏证明。

Method: 研究 Airy_β 线系综 Laplace 变换各阶矩的渐近展开（由作者与 Xu 的前期工作建立），据此对中心化高度函数作空间与时间重整化并取极限。

Result: 中心化高度函数收敛到半平面 GFF，其共形结构涉及空间坐标变换 x↦√(−x)，且 β 依赖被隔离为场的 √β 重标度。

Conclusion: Airy_β 线系综在大尺度下具有与 β 无关的高斯自由场极限（除整体 √β 因子外），说明其普适涨落由 GFF 共形结构刻画。

Abstract: We prove that the centered height function of the Airy$_β$ line ensemble converges for each $β>0$, under rescaling of space and time variables, towards the Gaussian free field in the half-plane. The conformal structure for GFF involves the spatial change of coordinate $x\mapsto \sqrt{-x}$. The $β$--dependence is isolated in the field rescaling by $\sqrtβ$. For the proofs we study the asymptotic expansions of the moments of Laplace transforms of the Airy$_β$ line ensemble developed in our previous work with Xu.

</details>

### [157] [Discrete Backbone Elasticity and Curvature-Dependent Torsion Statistics in Proteins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06363)
*Yiquan Wang,Mingyu Bian,Jiayuan Ma*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: 该文将离散Hasimoto标架输运与相邻弯曲失配联系起来，推导出曲率乘积型扭转相互作用与六参数广义von Mises密度，作为仅依赖局部Cα几何的基线模型，显著降低密度损失、扭转符号误差与螺旋比例误差，并经消融与轨迹测试界定局部弹性解释的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗粒化蛋白质构象动力学模型以Cα几何描述规则螺旋、有序转角与无序构象的概率，但尚不清楚其中多少序结构源于局部链弹性，因此需要检验局部弹性能解释的边界。

Method: 将离散Hasimoto标架输运（discrete Hasimoto frame transport）与相邻弯曲失配耦合，推导曲率乘积扭转相互作用及六参数广义von Mises密度（generalized von Mises density）；在ATLAS数据库194条链上拟合、在1744条非冗余序列蛋白上测试，并做消融、圆形矩投影对照参考生成模型，以及各向异性刚度与两弹性态扩展。

Result: 基线将负对数密度损失从1.490降至1.160 nats，扭转符号误差从12.26降至2.15个百分点，螺旋比例误差从21.84降至10.41个百分点；扩展后测试损失降至0.790 nats、螺旋误差降至1.65个百分点，并在肽Fs、(AAQAA)3与微型蛋白HP35的72条轨迹上改善预测。残余角误差与共享弯折处的原子环境相关；引入双线性耦合Cα–N取向角基本只是重参数化，其新增系数函数平方范数的96.7%–99.6%落在原有六函数张成空间内。

Conclusion: 结果将离散Hasimoto弹性与统计骨架势（statistical backbone potentials）联系起来，并明确了局部Cα几何解释力的适用范围与局限。

Abstract: Coarse-grained models of protein conformational dynamics use $\mathrm{C}_α$ geometry to describe the probabilities of regular helices, ordered turns and disordered conformations. To test how much of this order arises from local chain elasticity, we connect discrete Hasimoto frame transport to neighboring bending mismatch, deriving a curvature-product torsion interaction and a six-parameter generalized von Mises density. Fitted on 194 chains from the ATLAS molecular dynamics database and tested on 1744 sequence-nonredundant proteins, this baseline reduces negative log-density loss from 1.490 to 1.160 nats, torsion-sign error from 12.26 to 2.15 percentage points, and helix-fraction error from 21.84 to 10.41 points. Ablations and circular-moment projections against reference generative models motivate extensions to anisotropic stiffness and two partition-function-weighted elastic states, further lowering test loss to 0.790 nats and helix error to 1.65 points. The fixed hierarchy also improves predictions across 72 trajectories of peptides $\mathrm{Fs}$ and $(\mathrm{AAQAA})_3$ and miniprotein $\mathrm{HP35}$ spanning varied starting conformations and force fields. Remaining angular errors in these trajectories are associated with atomic context at shared bends, as bend-matched peptide frames adopt opposite torsion signs with distinct hydrogen-bond patterns and a proline-containing $\mathrm{HP35}$ turn remains at negative torsion. Introducing a bilinearly coupled $\mathrm{C}_α$--$\mathrm{N}$ orientation angle largely reparameterizes the existing bend-dependent harmonics, with an empirical $L^2$ Gram projection placing 96.7\% to 99.6\% of its added coefficient-function squared norm within the original six-function span. These results link discrete Hasimoto elasticity to statistical backbone potentials and clarify the scope and limits of local $\mathrm{C}_α$ geometry.

</details>
