# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Near-Quantum-Limited Readout for the Princeton Axion Search using a sub-GHz Kinetic Inductance Traveling Wave Parametric Amplifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06761)
*Joe Wiedemann,Roman Kolevatov,Jianyang Fu,Joelle-Marie Begin,Nate Otto,Felix St-Amour,Lyman Page,Henry LeDuc,Peter Day,Saptarshi Chaudhuri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并表征了一种工作在 300–700 MHz 的动能电感行波参量放大器（kinetic inductance traveling-wave parametric amplifier, KI-TWPA），增益超过 30 dB，泵浦功率仅数百纳瓦（nanowatt），系统噪声为 2.5–3.5 个量子（quanta）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参量放大器（parametric amplifier）是 1 GHz 以上近量子极限（near-quantum-limited）测量的主力器件，但 1 GHz 以下的放大器发展相对有限，亚 GHz 精密测量缺乏合适工具。

Method: 采用动能电感行波参量放大器（KI-TWPA）结构，使其工作在 300–700 MHz 频段并以数百纳瓦的泵浦功率驱动；使用变温噪声源（variable-temperature noise source）测量系统噪声，并表征增益漂移（gain drift）、压缩点（compression point）、不同工作温度下的放大器噪声、带输入环行器（circulator）的读出以及磁场不敏感性等性能。

Result: 在 300–700 MHz 范围内实现超过 30 dB 的增益，泵浦功率为数百纳瓦；系统噪声为 2.5–3.5 个量子。器件在增益漂移、压缩点、温度相关噪声、环行器读出及磁场不敏感性方面完成了与轴子（axion）搜寻相关的性能表征。

Conclusion: 该器件为亚 GHz（sub-GHz）信号的精密测量提供了通用新工具，可应用于半导体自旋量子比特（spin qubit）读出、超导探测器阵列（superconducting detector array）以及超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的新物理搜寻。

Abstract: Parametric amplifiers are a workhorse for near-quantum-limited measurements above 1 GHz. However, amplifier development has been more limited below 1 GHz. We present a kinetic inductance traveling-wave parametric amplifier (KI-TWPA) operating over 300-700 MHz, with gain exceeding 30 dB and pump powers of a few hundred nanowatts. The system noise performance, as measured by a variable-temperature noise source, is 2.5-3.5 quanta. This device is a versatile new tool for precision measurements of sub-GHz signals, with applications including readout of semiconductor spin qubits, superconducting detector arrays, and searches for new physics beyond the Standard Model. We characterize aspects of the amplifier performance relevant to axion searches, including gain drift, compression point, amplifier noise at various operating temperatures, readout with an input circulator, and magnetic field insensitivity.

</details>


### [159] [Spectral measures and sharpness of quantum speed limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05462)
*Ivica Smolić*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在若干标准正交化时间界（orthogonalization time bounds）下，使量子速度极限（quantum speed limits）取等号的谱测度（spectral measure）唯一，为两个狄拉克测度（Dirac measures）之和，并刻画了逼近这些界的谱测度族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子速度极限在特定能量约束下界定了量子态演化至希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）某子空间所需的最短时间，但达到该极限所需初始向量的谱结构尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 针对若干标准正交化时间界，分析初始向量对应的谱测度取得等号的充要结构；进而约束任意逼近这些界的谱测度族，并构造相对于勒贝格测度（Lebesgue measure）为纯绝对连续（purely absolutely continuous）与纯奇异连续（purely singular continuous）的谱测度实例；同时借助演化算符的数值域（numerical range）讨论其几何直观。

Result: 使界取等号的唯一谱测度为两个狄拉克测度之和；并给出可任意逼近这些界的纯绝对连续与纯奇异连续谱测度构造。

Conclusion: 量子速度极限的饱和由高度集中的离散谱结构决定，而该极限可被谱型差异显著的测度任意逼近，表明极限的普适性与其饱和条件的刚性形成对比；演化算符数值域提供了相应的几何解释。

Abstract: Quantum speed limits bound the time required for a given state to evolve into a given subspace of the Hilbert space under specific energy constraints. We prove that for several standard orthogonalization time bounds, the unique spectral measure associated with the initial vector that saturates the bound is a sum of two Dirac measures. Furthermore, we constrain the families of spectral measures that come arbitrarily close to these bounds and construct examples with measures which are purely absolutely continuous and purely singular continuous with respect to the Lebesgue measure. As a complementary perspective, we discuss the geometric intuition behind quantum speed limits provided by the numerical range of the evolution operator.

</details>


### [160] [Raychaudhuri-Ehlers Kinematics on Quantum State-Space: Caustics and Extrinsic Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05499)
*Neellohit Karmakar,Suraj S. Hegde,Aryan Bhatia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用Raychaudhuri-Ehlers方程研究量子态空间上测地线族的普适几何行为，证明复结构导致潮汐各向异性，并将几何结果用保真度磁化率与量子Fisher信息解释，进而以横场XY自旋链给出精确多体结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学具有内在几何性：纯态构成弯曲复流形，Schrödinger演化定义其上的Hamiltonian流。几何可给出与微观实现无关的量子态空间流约束，但此类普适行为尚待系统刻画。

Method: 采用Raychaudhuri-Ehlers方程分析量子态空间中的测地线族，刻画潮汐各向异性、剪切（shear）与方向选择性聚焦；对受物理参数约束的基态流形，将潮汐曲率分解为依赖Berry曲率的环境潮汐与外在弯曲贡献，并在横场XY自旋链上作精确计算。

Result: 当Hilbert空间维度N≥3时，完整点发射测地线束必然产生剪切并在有限距离发生方向选择性聚焦；复结构导致曲率的潮汐各向异性。XY链中基态乘积结构使环境潮汐（含Berry曲率增强）被外在曲率精确抵消；零场Ising点给出有限尺寸精确结果，各向异性定向运动的剪切源完全为外在的；热力学极限下铁磁相内测地线族出现重复的A2折叠突变（fold catastrophe）。

Conclusion: 该框架将普适的态空间效应与物理约束效应分离，并在典型多体系统中揭示其后果，为量子态空间几何提供了信息论与多体层面的统一解读。

Abstract: Quantum mechanics is inherently geometric in that the pure states form a curved complex manifold and the Schrödinger evolution defines a Hamiltonian flow on this space. Geometry identifies constraints on flows on quantum state-space that hold independently of their microscopic realization. We uncover such universal behavior in families of geodesic paths on the quantum state-space using the Raychaudhuri-Ehlers equations. We show that the complex structure leads to tidal anisotropy in the curvature, and for Hilbert space dimension $N\geq3$, complete point-emitted geodesic bundles necessarily develop shear and undergo direction-selective focusing at finite distance. We give these geometric results an information-theoretic interpretation in terms of fidelity susceptibility and quantum Fisher information. For ground-state manifolds constrained by physical parameters, we resolve the tidal curvature into Berry-curvature-dependent ambient tides and contributions from extrinsic bending within the full ambient state space. We further relate extrinsic bending to variations in the unitary generator of motion along intrinsic geodesics. We present exact many-body geometric results for the transverse-field XY spin chain. The product structure of the ground state leads to an exact cancellation of the ambient tide, including its Berry-curvature enhancement, by extrinsic curvature contributions. At the zero-field Ising point, we obtain exact results at finite size and show that the shear source for anisotropy-directed motion is entirely extrinsic relative to the full many-body state-space. For the metric in the thermodynamic limit, the geodesic families exhibit repeated $A_2$ fold catastrophes inside the ferromagnetic phase. Our framework thus separates universal state-space effects from those of physical restrictions and exposes their consequences in a paradigmatic many-body system.

</details>


### [161] [Toward a Quantum Information Foundation for Neutron Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05466)
*Michael J. Landry,Yongqiang Cheng,Daniel Pajerowski,Joshua J. Turner,Mingda Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将中子散射（neutron scattering）表述为量子测量（quantum measurement），以散射振幅作为作用于材料态上的Kraus算符，统一描述强度、相干散射、干涉测量与纠缠见证（entanglement witness），并考察该框架向非线性性质（如纯度）的扩展边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子散射是量子材料（quantum materials）的核心探测手段，但散射实验原则上能提取哪些量子信息（quantum information）仍不清晰，需要建立统一的量子信息描述框架。

Method: 将散射振幅视为Kraus算符以形式化中子散射为量子测量；对两个独立制备样品推导探针介导测量概率的一般结构，并分析自旋投影守恒（spin-projection-conserving）动力学下低阶散射的分辨能力，辅以理想化单自旋协议。

Result: 该框架统一涵盖传统强度、相干散射、干涉测量及基于量子Fisher信息（quantum Fisher information）的纠缠见证；在自旋投影守恒动力学下，低阶散射无法分辨确定纯度（purity）所需的某些多体相干（many-body coherence），而实现适当两拷贝（two-copy）操作的理想化单自旋协议可借助探针介导交换获取纯度信息。

Conclusion: 研究确立了中子散射的量子信息描述，并界定了传统测量与干涉测量可触及的信息范围，明确了向非线性量子态性质扩展的边界。

Abstract: Neutron scattering is a central probe of quantum materials, yet what quantum information can, in principle, be extracted from a scattering experiment remains unclear. Here we formulate neutron scattering as a quantum measurement, with the scattering amplitudes acting as Kraus operators on the material state. This provides a unified description of conventional intensity, coherent scattering, interferometric measurements, and entanglement witnesses, including those based on quantum Fisher information. We then ask how far this connection can be extended to nonlinear properties of a quantum state. For two independently prepared samples, we derive the general structure of probe-mediated measurement probabilities and show that, for spin-projection-conserving dynamics, low-order scattering cannot resolve certain many-body coherences required to determine purity. Conversely, an idealized single-spin protocol shows how probe-mediated exchange can access purity when the appropriate two-copy operation is realized. Our results establish a quantum-information description of neutron scattering and delineate the information accessible through conventional and interferometric measurements.

</details>


### [162] [Krylov meets Fock: Complementary lenses on quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05495)
*Giuseppe Di Giulio,Dimitrios Patramanis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将Krylov空间的粒子跳跃图像推广到Fock基，提出“Fock态晶格（FSL）”框架，用于以更一般的（可能高维的）晶格描述一般量子动力学，并在多模Jaynes-Cummings与Dicke模型中与Krylov方法作对比，发现Dicke模型展宽标度可探测超辐射相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov空间中的量子动力学可表示为粒子在由Krylov基矢构成的链上跳跃，但这一图像局限于链状结构；作者希望将其推广到Fock基，从而以更一般的、可能更高维的晶格刻画一般动力学。

Method: 以Fock态作为格点构造Fock态晶格（FSL），引入多种态展宽（spread）度量，并将其在FSL与Krylov空间之间相互比较；随后应用于量子光学中的两个光-物质相互作用模型：多模Jaynes-Cummings模型与Dicke模型。

Result: 当FSL不是一条链时，其对应动力学可提供对Krylov空间分析的互补洞察，并在某些情形下具有更强的解析可控性；例如获得了Dicke模型在热力学极限下FSL展宽度量的解析结果，并确定其时间标度可探测超辐射相变（superradiant phase transition）。

Conclusion: FSL为超越链状Krylov图像的一般量子动力学提供了更一般的晶格描述框架，与Krylov分析互补；在Dicke模型中其展宽标度可作为超辐射相变的可解析探针，显示出该构造在量子光学模型中的实用价值。

Abstract: Quantum dynamics in Krylov space can be represented by a particle hopping on a chain whose sites are the Krylov basis vectors. The goal of this work is to extend this idea to the Fock basis, which in turn allows the description of a generic dynamics in terms of a more general, possibly higher-dimensional lattice. This construction has been dubbed the Fock state lattice (FSL), since its sites are identified with Fock states. To analyze the dynamics on the FSL we introduce various measures of the spread of a state, comparing them among themselves and with their Krylov counterparts. We apply our analysis to two models of light-matter interaction studied in quantum optics: the multi-mode Jaynes-Cummings and the Dicke models. We find that, whenever the FSL is not a chain, the corresponding dynamics provides insights complementary to the Krylov space analysis and, in some instances, greater analytical control. For example, we obtain analytical results for the FSL spread measures of the Dicke model in the thermodynamic limit and determine that their scaling in time detects the superradiant phase transition.

</details>


### [163] [Quantum error correcting codes from quiver moduli: Grassmannians, cyclic monodromy, and argyle collision divisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05526)
*Steven Rayan,Evan Sundbo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于图解场论（diagrammatic field-theory）框架，在射影直线（projective line）上的扭曲循环箭图与argyle箭图模空间（quiver moduli）中构造精确有限维量子纠错码；并证明最大Kostka重数为最优逻辑维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续第一作者近期提出的量子纠错图解场论方案（arXiv:2607.08911），旨在从箭图模空间的代数几何结构中构造可精确解码的有限维量子码，并确定其在单值噪声模型下的最优维数与唯一性。

Method: 利用节点标记循环Hitchin映射（cyclic Hitchin map）的正则纤维记录谱零点在带次数箭头间的分配，所得Hilbert空间为Young置换模（Young permutation module）；根单值（root monodromy）作用于其Specht因子并保持重数空间无噪声。进一步通过量子化argyle主空间，将循环片空间识别为张量幂的权扇区（weight sector）。

Result: 证明最大Kostka重数为纠正整个单值代数的最优子空间码维数，即使在跨同型块的码中亦然；对平衡三循环，划分(a,b,c)的重数为1+min(a−b,b−c)，最优逻辑维数为t+1且由(2t,t)块唯一达到；t=1时给出六片qubit的显式码字，并计算碰撞宇称秩及高秩Grassmannian示例。

Conclusion: 单值噪声模型内的所有信道均可通过重置Specht因子恢复编码态；但交换的右作用生成完整逻辑代数，产生单值模型之外、观测足迹统计不变却需不同恢复方案的正确错误。研究还给出跨分裂类型（splitting types）恢复的条件性判据。

Abstract: Building on aspects of a recent diagrammatic field-theory programme for quantum error correction put forward by the first-named author (arXiv:2607.08911), we construct exact finite-dimensional codes from twisted cyclic and argyle quiver moduli on the projective line. A regular fibre of the node-labelled cyclic Hitchin map records the ways to distribute simple spectral zeroes among the arrows with prescribed degrees. The Hilbert space spanned by these allocations is a Young permutation module. Root monodromy acts on its Specht factors and leaves their multiplicity spaces noiseless. Resetting the Specht factor therefore recovers the encoded state for every channel in the monodromy noise model. We prove that the largest Kostka multiplicity is the optimal dimension of a subspace code correcting the full monodromy algebra, even among codes spanning several isotypic blocks. Quantizing an argyle master space recovers the cyclic sheet space as a weight sector of a tensor power, with the identification preserving monodromy. For the balanced three-cycle with $t$ zeroes per arrow, the multiplicity of a partition $(a,b,c)$ of $3t$ is $1+\min(a-b,b-c)$. The optimal logical dimension is thus $t+1$, attained uniquely by the block indexed by $(2t,t)$. At $t=1$ we give explicit codewords for a qubit on six sheets. The commuting right action generates its full logical algebra, yielding errors outside the monodromy model that leave the measured footprint statistics unchanged yet require different recoveries. We also compute collision-parity ranks, give a higher-rank Grassmannian example, and derive a conditional criterion for recovery across splitting types.

</details>


### [164] [Using topology to reveal all mutually unbiased bases in dimension six](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06917)
*Mateo Cárdenes Wuttig,Joseph Tindall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用复Hadamard矩阵空间的拓扑结构，证明六维空间中所有相互无偏基三元组均无法扩展为第四基，从而完整分类六维MUBs集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六维中是否存在四个相互无偏基（mutually unbiased bases, MUBs）这一问题悬置三十余年，此前仅有已知的三元组。该研究旨在解决这一长期未决问题并完成六维MUBs集合的分类。

Method: 借助描述MUBs之间转换的复Hadamard矩阵（complex Hadamard matrices）空间拓扑，提出新的拓扑结果：除Tao形式外，所有六阶复Hadamard矩阵构成连通空间。连通性将三元组分类化为有限个相互重叠的四维相位球（phase balls），并用区间算术（interval arithmetic）验证这些球覆盖整个空间。

Result: 证明每个相互无偏三元组或属于一个双参数族，或为由Fourier矩阵构成的孤立三元组；两者均不能扩展为第四基，故不存在四个MUBs。对球中每一对MUBs，进一步确定了所有可扩展的第三基。

Conclusion: 六维成为首个所有MUBs集合均被完整分类的非素数幂维度，同时证实了此前OpenAI关于不存在第四个基的证明结论。

Abstract: Mutually unbiased bases (MUBs) describe complementary quantum measurements: certainty in one basis gives no information about the others. In dimension six, triples of MUBs are known, but whether a fourth basis could exist remained unresolved for over three decades. OpenAI recently reported a proof that none exists. Here we use the topology of the space of complex Hadamard matrices, which describe transitions between MUBs, to characterize all sets in dimension six. We prove that every mutually unbiased triple belongs to a two-parameter family or is a single isolated triple built from the Fourier matrix. Since none of them extend to a fourth basis, this also proves that no four MUBs exist. Our proof rests on a new topological result that all order-six complex Hadamard matrices except Tao's form a connected space. Connectedness reduces the classification of triples to finitely many overlapping four-dimensional phase balls. We certify with interval arithmetic that these balls cover the space and for every pair of MUBs represented in a ball, we then determine all third bases that extend it. Dimension six is now the first non-prime-power dimension in which all sets of MUBs are classified.

</details>


### [165] [Non-compact Kogut-Susskind gauge theories: 1D lattice models with exact ${\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ gauge invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06771)
*Carlos Fulgado-Claudio,Torsten V. Zache*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在格点上重新构建 Kogut-Susskind（KS）规范理论框架以处理非紧致规范群（non-compact gauge group），以一维 SL(2,ℝ) 为范例，展示了精确规范不变性的保持、与紧致情形的差异，并给出可积模型及物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KS 构造此前主要针对紧致李群（compact Lie group）；广义相对论（general relativity）的一阶表述涉及非紧致洛伦兹群（Lorentz group），因此有必要将 KS 格点哈密顿量框架推广到非紧致规范群，并先以一维简化情形切入。

Method: 采用自下而上（bottom-up）方法，在 1D 格点上构造 SL(2,ℝ) 的 KS 哈密顿量；引入正半群（positive semigroup）SL⁺(2,ℝ) 限制、借助额外合成维度（synthetic dimension）上的单费米子自由度实现静态电荷（static charges），并进一步推广到动力学费米物质（dynamical fermionic matter）。

Result: 发现非紧致情形与紧致群存在本质差异：规范场 Casimir 算符（Casimir operators）具有连续谱，物质场出现无穷维表示（infinite-dimensional representations）。纯规范理论经 SL⁺(2,ℝ) 限制后可复现 Jackiw-Teitelboim（JT）引力量子化的已知结果；含静态电荷时模型仍精确可积（exactly integrable），含动力学费米子时得到相互作用的 1D SL(2,ℝ) KS 规范理论。

Conclusion: 该相互作用模型可解释为由弦（strings）依次连接电荷构成的链，弦张力依相对连续 SL(2,ℝ) 通量（fluxes）的符号将物质向左或向右牵引；在 SL(2,ℝ) 电荷强自相互作用极限下该图像严格成立，并可对约化的两体问题（two-body problem）解析求解。

Abstract: The Kogut-Susskind (KS) formulation of gauge theories constructs quantum many-body Hamiltonians with gauge invariance under a compact Lie group exactly implemented on a spatial lattice. Here, we revisit this construction for a non-compact gauge group. Although motivated in part by theories like general relativity whose first-order formulation involves the non-compact Lorentz group as a local symmetry, we focus on simpler cases in one spatial dimension akin to the Schwinger model. In a bottom-up approach, we show how exact gauge invariance under the simplest non-compact Lie group ${\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ can be retained exactly on a 1D lattice, and highlight the differences that arise with respect to compact gauge groups, including a continuous spectrum of the Casimir operators of the gauge fields and the emergence of infinite-dimensional representations of the matter fields. For the pure gauge theory, we discuss how a consistent restriction to the positive semigroup ${\rm SL}^{+}(2,\mathbb{R})$ reproduces well-known results from the quantization of Jackiw-Teitelboim (JT) gravity. Motivated by the constraint structure of general dilaton gravity, we introduce static charges coupled to the non-compact gauge field, which can be realised by single-fermion degrees of freedom living in an additional, synthetic dimension. With static charges our lattice model remains exactly integrable, but when extending to dynamical fermionic matter, we arrive at an interacting 1D ${\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ KS gauge theory. We give an interpretation of this model as a chain of charges sequentially connected by strings whose tension pulls matter left or right depending on the sign of the relative continuous ${\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ fluxes. This picture becomes exact when the involved ${\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ charges are strongly self-interacting, where we solve the reduced two-body problem analytically.

</details>


### [166] [Probing gravity at megahertz frequencies with levitated rotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06893)
*Matteo Fadel,Andrea Vinante*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出近场引力实验方案：用悬浮微转子调制质量四极矩，另一转子以力矩方式探测引力潮汐场，将兆赫兹信号下变频至数赫兹，可在约1小时内探测2 MHz、2×10^-25 Nm的力矩，从而把引力耦合非瞬时性（场弛豫时间）的约束提升至10^-8 s量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近场引力相互作用通常被视为瞬时（instantaneous），但实验检验仅达1.7 kHz，对应约10^-5 s量级的界限；因此需要将检验频率提升至更高频段以更严格地限制引力耦合的非瞬时偏离。

Method: 设计近场实验：悬浮微转子（levitated microrotor）调制质量四极矩（mass quadrupole），第二个转子作为机械本振（mechanical local oscillator）通过力矩测量引力潮汐场（gravitational tidal field），将兆赫兹信号下变频（downconvert）至数赫兹频段；并在固定几何下论证灵敏度与转速、积分时间无关。

Result: 在磁悬浮NdFeB转子可达参数下，2 MHz信号产生2×10^-25 Nm力矩，可在10 mK下不到一小时内探测；对场弛豫时间（field relaxation time）的约束可达10^-8 s量级，较现有界限改善三个数量级以上。附带得到转子作为可调高频引力波探测器（gravitational-wave detector）的噪声等效应变约10^-22 Hz^-1/2（兆赫兹频段）。

Conclusion: 该方案可在不牺牲灵敏度的情况下将引力瞬时性检验拓展至兆赫兹频段，显著收紧对引力耦合弛豫时间的限制，并可作为高频引力波探测的可行平台。

Abstract: The near-field gravitational interaction between masses is always thought of as instantaneous, an assumption experimentally tested only up to 1.7 kHz, where measurements yield a bounds at the $10^{-5}$ s level. We propose a near-field experiment reaching up to megahertz frequencies, in which a levitated microrotor modulates a mass quadrupole and a second rotor measures the resulting gravitational tidal field as a torque. Because the detector rotor acts as a mechanical local oscillator, the megahertz signal is downconverted to a convenient band of a few hertz, and since at fixed geometry neither the torque nor the integration time depends on the rotation rate, higher frequencies are reached at no cost in sensitivity. With parameters within reach of magnetically levitated NdFeB rotors, a signal at 2 MHz yields a torque of $2\cdot 10^{-25}$ Nm, detectable in under an hour at 10 mK. Treating a possible departure from instantaneous gravitational coupling through a field relaxation time, the measured torque could constrain it at the $10^{-8}$ s level, improving on the existing bounds by more than three orders of magnitude. As a byproduct of our analysis, we derive the sensitivity of a rotor operated as a tunable high-frequency gravitational-wave detector, finding that a noise-equivalent strain around $10^{-22}$ Hz$^{-1/2}$ could be achievable in the megahertz band.

</details>


### [167] [Entanglement Entropy in Non Relativistic Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06774)
*Bertrand Giraud,Robi Peschanski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文数值研究两个非相对论性（non relativistic）粒子对撞时纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）的建立过程，发现在不同物理情境下纠缠熵的时间演化趋势与数值高度相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究由含局域有限程势（local finite range potentials）的普通薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation）驱动、初态为两个独立波包（wave packet）乘积的碰撞粒子之间，纠缠熵如何随时间建立，并检验其结果是否依赖具体物理情境。

Method: 采用数值方法求解薛定谔方程，考察低能下的势垒隧道效应（tunnel effect）与镜像效应（mirror effect）、更高能量情形以及吸引势与排斥势等多种情形，并计算波函数、密度矩阵（density matrix）与纠缠熵。

Result: 尽管计算所得波函数与密度矩阵随模型描述的物理情境显著不同，纠缠熵随时间上升的趋势与数值却出乎意料地彼此相似。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵的建立过程对具体物理细节表现得不敏感，呈现出跨情境的普适性相似特征。

Abstract: We study numerically the build-up of entanglement entropy between two colliding, non relativistic particles, that are driven by the plain Schrödinger equation containing local, finite range potentials, and whose initial state is the product of two independent wave packets. Different situations are considered, ranging from a tunnel and mirror effect at low energy in front of a barrier, to higher energies, and also attraction rather than repulsion. While the calculated wave functions and density matrices significantly depend on the physical situations described by our models, the build-up trends and values in time of entanglement entropy look surprisingly alike one another.

</details>


### [168] [Noise-Induced Topology in Chiral Quantum Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05471)
*Alexandre Chaduteau,Derek K. K. Lee,Frank Schindler,Abhinav Prem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示退相位噪声（dephasing noise）不会抹除手性量子行走（chiral quantum walk）的输运不对称性，反而使其获得拓扑鲁棒性：密度分布随时间增长的偏度（skewness）出现，有限链中出现无漂移的趋肤效应（skin effect）。噪声平均动力学可映射为相互作用Lindbladian（Lindbladian），其晚期弛豫模式形成带点隙（point gap）的能带，绕数（winding number）决定不对称方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为噪声破坏量子相干性（quantum coherence）并洗掉依赖干涉的输运现象，手性量子行走中由磁通引起的路径干涉不对称被认为会被噪声消除。论文旨在检验最常见的在位能量随机涨落（即退相位）对手性输运不对称性的实际影响，并给出与直觉相反的机制解释。

Method: 研究格点上手性量子行走在退相位噪声下的噪声平均动力学，并将其刻画为相互作用（一般不可积）的Lindbladian；通过解析可处理的形式分析晚期弛豫模式、密度分布的偏度与中心位置，并考虑多子格情形及含噪声横档跃迁（rung hopping）的通量梯（flux ladder）。

Result: 退相位噪声使不对称性拓扑鲁棒：密度剖面偏度随时间增长而中心固定；有限链晚期弛豫模式堆积于一侧边缘，形成无常规非厄米趋肤效应（non-Hermitian skin effect）漂移的趋肤效应。多子格时，仅靠退相位强度即可驱动拓扑相变（topological phase transition）并反转不对称；含噪横档跃迁的梯上出现横档相干性的漂移，形成无经典对应的量子棘轮（quantum ratchet）。

Conclusion: 控制晚期弛豫的模式是噪声诱导相互作用的束缚态，构成具有点隙的能带；该能带在无噪声及后选择（postselection）情形下均不存在，其绕数确定不对称方向。相关效应可在合成晶格（synthetic lattices）、超导量子比特（superconducting qubits）与光晶格（optical lattices）等平台实现。

Abstract: Noise typically destroys quantum coherence and washes out transport phenomena that rely on interference. Chiral quantum walks are a case in point: a particle hopping on a lattice threaded by a magnetic flux can spread asymmetrically due to interference between paths, an asymmetry that noise is expected to erase. Remarkably, we find that the most ubiquitous noise -- random fluctuations of on-site energies (dephasing) -- instead renders this asymmetry topologically robust. The density profile develops a skewness that grows in time while its center stays fixed, and in a finite chain the late-time relaxation modes pile up at one edge: a skin effect lacking the drift of conventional non-Hermitian skin effects. With multiple sites per unit cell, the dephasing strength alone can cause a topological phase transition, reversing the asymmetry. On a flux ladder with noisy rung hopping, the noise causes a drift of rung coherences, a quantum ratchet with no classical counterpart. Underlying these effects, the noise-averaged dynamics is an interacting (generically non-integrable) Lindbladian that remains analytically tractable. The modes governing late-time relaxation are bound states of the noise-induced interaction and form a band with a point gap, absent both without noise and under postselection, whose winding number fixes the direction of the asymmetry. These effects are within reach of several experimental platforms, including synthetic lattices, superconducting qubits, and optical lattices.

</details>


### [169] [Zero noise extrapolation of transport coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05481)
*Hansveer Singh,Pieter Claeys,Sarang Gopalakrishnan,Anushya Chandran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出，当噪声违反守恒律时，常规零噪声外推（ZNE）无法估计守恒量的输运系数；作者提出在含噪一维非可积自旋链中通过长波模式衰减率的外推获得零噪声极限下的自旋输运系数，并论证普适的非线性流体力学修正会导致对噪声强度 γ 的非解析依赖，如有效自旋扩散系数按 γ^{1/2} 趋于无噪值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ 设备常通过不同噪声强度 γ 下的测量做零噪声外推（zero-noise extrapolation, ZNE），但当噪声破坏守恒律时，这类方案无法估计守恒量的输运系数，因而需要一种适用于含噪体系的外推框架。

Method: 以含噪一维非可积自旋链（noisy, non-integrable spin chains）为对象，利用守恒量傅里叶模式（Fourier mode）波矢趋于零时衰减率消失的性质：由领头动量依赖的系数定义无噪体系的输运系数及含噪体系的有效系数，并分析其对 γ→0 的解析性；同时以数值方法检验解析预测。

Result: 核心结论是流体力学（hydrodynamics）的普适非线性修正可使有效系数对 γ 产生非解析依赖：耗散海森堡链（dissipative Heisenberg chain）的有效自旋扩散系数与偶极守恒分形子（dipole-conserving fracton）的有效亚扩散系数均以 γ^{1/2} 趋近其无噪值；数值结果与解析预测一致。

Conclusion: 朴素 ZNE 隐含假设有效系数在 γ→0 时对 γ 解析，该假设在有噪声破坏守恒律的情形下不成立；实际外推需考虑 γ^{1/2} 型非解析修正，以实现对零噪声输运系数的可靠估计。

Abstract: NISQ devices routinely estimate properties of the noiseless system from measurements at varying noise strengths $γ$. These zero-noise extrapolation (ZNE) schemes fail to estimate transport coefficients of conserved quantities when the noise violates the associated conservation laws. We show how to extrapolate measurements in noisy, one-dimensional, non-integrable spin chains to obtain the spin transport coefficients governing linear response in the zero-noise limit. As the wavevector of the Fourier mode of a conserved quantity goes to zero, its decay rate vanishes. The coefficient of the leading momentum dependence defines a transport coefficient in the noiseless system, and an analogous effective coefficient in the noisy one. Naive ZNE implicitly assumes that this effective coefficient is analytic in $γ$ as $γ\to 0$. Our main result is that universal non-linear corrections to hydrodynamics can produce non-analytic dependence on $γ$. In particular, we argue that the effective spin diffusion coefficient of a dissipative Heisenberg chain and the effective subdiffusion coefficient of a dipole-conserving fracton approach their noiseless value as $γ^{1/2}$. We benchmark these analytical predictions numerically.

</details>


### [170] [Rotated-Frame Determinant Discovery for Ground- and Excited-State Quantum Selected Configuration Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05941)
*Yukihiro Okuno*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 rotate–sample–unrotate（RSU）行列式发现方法，通过互补轨道框架采样，缓解有限采样下高概率行列式被重复观测的问题，并在 N₂ 分子上以更少采样次数达到化学精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QSCI 需要从有限次测量采样中构建变分行列式空间，但高概率行列式被反复观测，导致对精确 selected-CI 描述重要的构型难以被高效发现。

Method: 在同一量子态下于互补轨道框架中采样，再将旋转框架中观测到的行列式映射回正则基（canonical basis）生成有限候选集，并在正交化 selected-CI 空间中对哈密顿量对角化，而无需枚举完整的行列式空间。轨道框架由初始 selected-CI 波函数及其外部残差构造；激发态则结合生成式量子本征求解器（GQE）制备目标采样态，候选电路通过经典 selected-CI 对角化进行评估。

Result: 在 N₂ 上，拉伸构型下残差自然轨道（residual-natural-orbital）RSU 以 5,000 次采样达到化学精度（chemical accuracy），而传统 QSCI 需要 20,000 次采样；该改进在使用近似 GQE 制备态以及针对目标激发态时依然成立。

Conclusion: 结果表明 RSU 可改善 QSCI 在基态与激发态下的有限采样行列式发现效率。

Abstract: Quantum Selected Configuration Interaction (QSCI) constructs a variational determinant space from measurements of a prepared electronic state, but finite-shot sampling can repeatedly observe high-probability determinants, limiting efficient discovery of configurations important for an accurate selected-CI description. We introduce rotate--sample--unrotate (RSU) determinant discovery, in which the same state is sampled in complementary orbital frames and the observed rotated determinants generate a finite set of candidates in the canonical basis. The Hamiltonian is then diagonalized in an orthonormal selected-CI space without enumerating the full determinant space. State-targeted orbital frames are constructed from an initial selected-CI wavefunction and its external residual. For excited states, we combine RSU with the generative quantum eigensolver (GQE) to prepare target-specific sampling states. Candidate GQE circuits are evaluated by classical selected-CI diagonalization in finite sampled spaces, so the quantum procedure requires only state preparation and determinant sampling. For N$_2$, exact-state controls isolate the determinant-discovery effect: at the stretched geometry, residual-natural-orbital RSU reaches chemical accuracy with 5,000 shots, compared with 20,000 shots for conventional QSCI. The improvement persists with approximate GQE-prepared states and is also observed for target excited states. These results demonstrate improved finite-shot determinant discovery in QSCI for both ground and excited states.

</details>


### [171] [Spatial Cost of Recovering a Fusion Qubit in a Critical Spin Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06215)
*Yi Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在临界伊辛链（critical Ising chain）中，利用四个 Kramers–Wannier 缺陷的固定扇区基态二重态（ground doublet）实现融合量子比特（fusion qubit）的无差错恢复（vanishing-error recovery），其主导阶所需的最小物理自旋支撑至少为较短的三缺陷跨度；精确区间解码器（exact interval decoder）可达此界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究融合量子比特无差错恢复所需的最小物理资源下界，明确在任意预选自旋集合与无限制联合解码（joint decoding）条件下该下界成立的条件。

Method: 采用解析下界论证：在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）先于固定正比值下的大间距极限（large spacings）时，对任意预选自旋集合与无限制联合解码证明支撑下界；并构造精确区间解码器以验证可达性；辅以互补区域两逻辑轴的集体记录（collective records）分析成功近最小支撑的密度分布。

Result: 无差错恢复至少需要较短三缺陷跨度的物理自旋支撑（主导阶）；该界在所述极限次序下对任意预选自旋集合与无限制联合解码均成立；精确区间解码器达到该界；互补区域的集体记录迫使成功的近最小支撑在密度上集中于主导阶最短区间。

Conclusion: 三缺陷跨度主导阶界为融合量子比特恢复设定紧致的最小支撑阈值，且近最小支撑的实现被限制在最短区间内，揭示了资源下界与解码结构的对应关系。

Abstract: Vanishing-error recovery of a fusion qubit from a fixed-sector ground doublet of four Kramers--Wannier defects in a critical Ising chain requires at least the shorter three-defect span of physical spins to leading order. Across arbitrary preselected spin sets and unrestricted joint decoding, this bound holds when the thermodynamic limit precedes large spacings at fixed positive ratios. An exact interval decoder attains it. Collective records of both logical axes in the complement force successful near-minimal supports to concentrate in density on a leading-order shortest interval.

</details>


### [172] [Helium States in a CAS(2,2) Subspace Inspired by Quantum Photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06448)
*Forouzan Mirmasoudi,Badie Ghavami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在四模式福克态（Fock-state）光子学平台上实现变分量子收缩（variational quantum deflation, VQD）算法，用于求解中性氦原子在 CAS(2,2) 子空间内的基态与激发态能量，并以精确对角化结果作为基准进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在连续变量（continuous-variable）光子量子计算架构上求解原子电子结构问题，特别是高效获得基态之外的多个激发态能量，需要引入能够在受限活性空间内保持正交性的变分方法。

Method: 将 CAS(2,2) 电子希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）编码为玻色型福克空间，采用由单模相位平移（phase shift）、分束器（beam splitter）与交叉克尔相互作用（cross-Kerr interaction）构成的连续变量量子线路，以 |1100⟩ 福克态初始化，并结合 VQD 方法逐级优化基态至第三激发态。

Result: 以 CASCI 哈密顿量的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）为基准进行对比；在理想无噪声模拟下，由于总光子数及 α、β 两个自旋扇区的光子数守恒，目标 CAS 子空间内基态与激发态的存活概率（survival probability）为 1，且正交性条件满足、误差可忽略。

Conclusion: 所提方案表明，连续变量量子线路结合 VQD 算法能够在 CAS(2,2) 电子希尔伯特空间内确定基态与激发态能量，并在满足正交性约束的前提下保持极低误差，为该架构用于量子化学激发态求解提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: We present a four-mode Fock-state photonics implementation of the variational quantum deflation (VQD) algorithm to calculate the ground-state and excited-state energies for the neutral helium atom in a CAS(2,2) Subspace. Its CAS(2,2) electronic Hilbert space is encoded into a bosonic Fock-space representation and subsequently optimized using a continuous-variable quantum circuit. The circuit architecture under study is constructed of single-mode phase shifts, beam splitters, and cross-Kerr interactions. The variational circuit is initialized in the Fock state $\ket{1100}$ and its performance is benchmarked against exact diagonalization of the active-space configuration-interaction (CASCI) Hamiltonian. We also extend the method to optimize from the first to three excited states of the helium atom with the aid of the variational quantum deflation $(VQD)$ method in a CAS(2,2). We then investigate the performance of this circuit architecture as a basis for implementing the VQD method. In the ideal noiseless simulation setting, we see that the survival probability of the ground and the excited states within the target $CAS$ subspace is unity, because the total photon number and the photon number in the two spin sectors $α$ and $β$ are preserved. We demonstrate that the proposed approach enables the determination of the ground and excited-state energies within the CAS(2,2) electronic Hilbert space using a continuous-variable quantum circuit and the variational quantum deflation ($VQD$) algorithm, while satisfying the orthogonality conditions with negligible errors.

</details>

### [173] [Measurements of isolated photon pair production in association with jets in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 8 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06142)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于ATLAS 2012年20.3 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据（√s=8 TeV），测量了双孤立光子（diphoton）在伴随不同强子喷注（jet）数目条件下的一体化与微分产生截面（production cross section），并与Sherpa、GoSam、BlackHat等理论预言比较。NLO预言整体合理，但随喷注多重数增加出现显著偏差；Sherpa 2.2.14的NLO匹配预言描述最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双光子产生及其与喷注的关联是检验量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）高阶修正与微扰计算的重要过程，需要高精度实验数据来区分不同理论模型并约束多喷注末态的计算精度。

Method: 利用ATLAS实验在√s=8 TeV下20.3 fb⁻¹积分亮度（integrated luminosity）的质子-质子对撞数据，选取|η^γ|<1.37或1.56<|η^γ|<2.37、E_T^γ>22 GeV的光子，喷注要求为|η^j|<2.4时p_T^j>25 GeV、2.4<|η^j|<4.4时p_T^j>50 GeV；按喷注数目分类，测量包含性与排他性喷注数目的保真截面（fiducial cross section），以及基于16个双光子、喷注、双喷注和光子-喷注观测量（observable）的微分截面。

Result: 数据与领头阶（leading-order）Sherpa 1.4.1、固定阶次领头阶（next-to-leading-order, NLO）GoSam与BlackHat计算及NLO匹配的Sherpa 2.2.14预言进行了比较；NLO预言整体对数据给出合理描述，但在喷注多重数（jet multiplicity）增加时，尤其角变量（angular variable）上出现显著偏差，归因于计算中缺少高阶项。

Conclusion: Sherpa 2.2.14的NLO匹配预言在所有喷注类别与观测量上对数据给出最佳的整体描述，表明NLO匹配方法在多喷注双光子产生过程的描述上优于纯领头阶与固定阶计算。

Abstract: Measurements of integrated and differential production cross section of two isolated photons in events with and without associated hadronic jets based on the 2012 dataset of 20.3 fb$^{-1}$ integrated luminosity of proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=8$ TeV collected by the ATLAS experiment are presented. The measurements consider photons with pseudorapidities of $|η^γ|<1.37$ or $1.56<|η^γ|<2.37$ and transverse energy of $E_{T}^γ>22$ GeV. Jets are required to have $p_{T}^{j}>25$ GeV for $|η^{j}|<2.4$ and $p_{T}^{j}>50$ GeV for $2.4<|η^{j}|<4.4$. Events are classified into categories based on the number of jets and integrated fiducial cross sections of diphoton production in association with inclusive and exclusive number of jets are presented. Fiducial cross sections, differential in sixteen diphoton, jet, dijet and photon-jet observables, are presented and compared with leading-order Sherpa 1.4.1 predictions in all jet categories, fixed-order next-to-leading-order GoSam and BlackHat calculations in selected categories, and NLO-matched predictions from Sherpa 2.2.14 in all categories. Overall, the NLO predictions provide a reasonable description of the data, with significant discrepancies observed for increasing jet multiplicity, particularly for angular variables, attributable to the absence of higher-order terms in the calculations. The Sherpa 2.2.14 NLO-matched predictions provide the best overall description of the data across all jet categories and observables.

</details>


### [174] [Operational Challenges in Efficient and Sustainable Operation of the Event Processing Nodes GPU Farm at the ALICE Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06586)
*Federico Ronchetti,Giada Erba*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ALICE 的事件处理节点（Event Processing Nodes, EPN）集群是 CERN 规模最大的 GPU 计算设施，含 350 个 GPU 节点、2800 块 GPU，总算力约 48 PFLOP/s，已在 LHC Run 3 中实现 50 kHz 重离子碰撞的实时处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足 LHC Run 3 重离子碰撞高触发率下的实时处理需求，并兼顾可持续性、硬件寿命延长与运维开销降低。

Method: 以 350 个基于 GPU 的节点（共 2800 块 GPU）构建 EPN 集群，采用稳健的技术架构与明确的运维实践（operational practices）保障高可用性（high availability）。

Result: 集群提供约 48 PFLOP/s 总算力，成功实现 50 kHz 重离子碰撞的实时处理，并在整个取数（data taking）期间保持可靠、连续的运行。

Conclusion: EPN 集群验证了大规模 GPU 设施在实时重离子数据处理中的可行性，其可持续性设计与运维规范支撑了高可用与稳定运行。

Abstract: ALICE's Event Processing Nodes (EPN) farm is CERN's largest GPU computing facility by capacity, comprising 350 GPU-based nodes and 2800 GPUs delivering approximately a total of 48\,PFLOP/s. It has successfully enabled real-time processing of heavy-ion collisions at 50\,kHz during LHC Run~3. The facility was designed for sustainability, extended hardware lifetime, and reduced operational overhead. High availability is supported by a robust technical architecture and well-defined operational practices, ensuring reliable and continuous operation throughout data taking.

</details>

### [175] [Frequency-Frame Covariance for Reactive-Power Scalarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05913)
*Kai Sun*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文针对频率偏移参考系下无功功率读数的一致性问题，提出有限频谱、零均值周期波形的频率参考系协变（covariance）表述，导出读数算子须与波形表示一致变换的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量所得电压电流波形可能被表示在频率不同于物理基频的参考系中，若读数算子未作一致变换，由此计算的无功功率（reactive power）读数会发生改变，需要明确其成立条件。

Method: 对具有完整谐波信息的有限频谱（finite-spectrum）、零均值周期波形建立频率参考系协变表述，推导协变的充要条件；并将读数算子误差与波形投影所保留物理分量变化引起的误差加以分离。

Result: 协变要求频率相关的谐波权重须按物理谐波阶次（physical harmonic orders）而非相对参考系标号求值；导数/Iliovici读数可分解为相对导数贡献与显式参考贡献。三个解析算例分别说明无记忆电阻（memoryless resistor）的条件性载波恢复、RLC支路中相对零点的物理频率加权，以及投影后精确无功标量可与显著波形误差共存。

Conclusion: 研究范围限定为频率偏移坐标下确定性计算的一致性，而非仪器测量的不确定度（measurement uncertainty）；结论为频率参考系变换须与读数算子保持一致才能保证无功功率读数不变。

Abstract: Measured voltage and current waveforms may be represented in a reference frame whose frequency differs from the physical fundamental frequency. Reactive-power readouts calculated from such frequency-shifted coordinates may change if their readout operators are not transformed consistently. This paper presents a frequency-frame covariance formulation for finite-spectrum, zero-mean periodic waveforms with complete harmonic information over one physical period. A necessary and sufficient covariance condition is derived, requiring the readout operator to transform consistently with the waveform representation. Consequently, frequency-dependent harmonic weights must be evaluated at physical harmonic orders rather than relative frame labels. In particular, the derivative/Iliovici readout equals a relative-derivative contribution plus an explicit reference contribution. The formulation also separates readout-operator errors from errors caused by changes in the physical components retained by waveform projections. Three analytical cases illustrate conditional carrier recovery for a memoryless resistor, physical-frequency weighting at relative zero in an RLC branch, and the possibility that an exact reactive-power scalar can coexist with a substantial waveform error after projection. The scope is deterministic calculation consistency in frequency-shifted coordinates rather than instrument measurement uncertainty.

</details>

### [176] [A Construction Framework for Three-Dimensional Topological Magnetic Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06293)
*Maria Azhar,Sandra C. Shaju,Karin Everschor-Sitte*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文提出从简单母构型构建复杂三维磁结构（three-dimensional magnetic textures）的四条构造原则，并以斯格明子管（skyrmion tube）与半子管（meron tube）为例展示已知与新型拓扑织构的生成及稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维磁系统（three-dimensional magnetic systems）有望从简单磁构型（magnetic configurations）出发构建复杂拓扑织构，但缺乏系统化的构造思路及其对拓扑性质（topology）影响的统一认识。

Method: 提出四条构造原则：(i) 引入横向调制（transverse modulations）；(ii) 添加结构；(iii) 缠绕织构（entangling textures，如编织 braiding、扭曲 twisting）；(iv) 引入奇点（singularities）。以斯格明子管为显式母构型应用上述原则，并进一步应用于半子管作预测。

Result: 四条原则可重现若干已知三维磁织构；由半子管导出的新织构经微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）证实，在手性磁体（chiral magnets）中处于亚稳态（metastable）。

Conclusion: 研究建立了系统构建与发现复杂三维磁织构的一般性原则，为拓扑磁结构的理性设计提供框架。

Abstract: Three-dimensional magnetic systems offer diverse possibilities for constructing complex topological textures from simpler magnetic configurations. Here, we present different routes for constructing complex three-dimensional magnetic textures from simpler parent configurations and discuss their impact on the topology of the resulting structures. We identify four construction principles: (i) adding transverse modulations, (ii) adding structures, (iii) entangling textures (e.g. braiding, or twisting), and (iv) introducing singularities.
  Using the skyrmion tube as an explicit parent configuration, we show how these principles give rise to several known three-dimensional magnetic textures. We then apply the same principles to predict novel textures derived from a meron tube, which are confirmed by micromagnetic simulations to be metastable in chiral magnets. Our results establish general principles for the systematic construction and discovery of complex three-dimensional magnetic textures.

</details>


### [177] [Quantum Spin Wave Theory with Multi-Spin Interactions for Magnetic Materials Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06833)
*Andrey Rybakov,Marco Marino,Yaroslav M. Blanter,Eugenio Coronado,Jaime Ferrer*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该研究提出多项式局域自旋模型（polynomial localized spin model, PLSM），使线性自旋波理论（linear spin wave theory, LSWT）可直接应用于任意共线与非共线磁相的能量图景与自旋波谱分析，并发布开源实现 Magnopy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态磁性系统中，自旋哈密顿量（spin Hamiltonian）的参数化依赖从头计算（ab initio calculations）与实验拟合，通常经由多阶段计算完成；因此需要准确且灵活的方法来模拟其所描述的物理性质。

Method: 以自旋波理论（spin wave theory）为研究对象，将其应用于自旋算符含任意阶项的新哈密顿量表述 PLSM，从而对任意共线与非共线磁相实施 LSWT，并清晰分离模型的经典部分与量子部分。

Result: PLSM 使 LSWT 可直接用于任意真实磁性材料中任意共线与非共线相的能量图景与自旋波谱分析；同时给出开源实现 Magnopy，其可与现代参数化软件对接，支持任意共线与非共线体系，并兼顾脚本调用与独立使用。

Conclusion: 该工作为自旋哈密顿量的自旋波模拟提供了准确、灵活且开源的框架，扩展了 LSWT 在任意阶自旋算符模型与复杂磁相中的适用范围。

Abstract: In the field of magnetism within condensed matter physics, magnetic systems are microscopically described by a spin Hamiltonian. Its parameterization is accessible through both ab initio calculations and fits to experimental data. Frequently, this is achieved via multi-stage calculations that either relate the parameters to observable material properties or map a Kohn-Sham Hamiltonian onto an effective spin model. In this realm, accurate and flexible methods are essential for simulating the physical properties of the latter. The present study focuses on one such method: spin wave theory. The approach is applied to a new formulation of the spin Hamiltonian, named polynomial localized spin model (PLSM), which includes terms of arbitrary order in the spin operators. This enables the direct application of linear spin wave theory (LSWT) to the analysis of the energy landscape and spin-wave spectrum of arbitrary collinear and non-collinear phases in any realistic magnetic material. Moreover, the classical and quantum parts of the PLSM are cleanly separated. Finally, an open-source implementation of LSWT, named Magnopy, is presented. It interfaces with modern parameterization software, supports arbitrary collinear and non-collinear systems, and is designed for both scripting and standalone use.

</details>
